Let us prove that the second statement holds with high probability. we fix arbitrary
s\in\{\pm 1\}
and
i\in[n_{\mathrm{st}}]
. For each
r\in[m]
,
\mathbb{P}[r\in{\mathcal{M}}_{s}]=\mathbb{P}[r\in{\mathcal{A}}_{s}]=\mathbb{P}[r\in{\mathcal{B}}_{s}]=\mathbb{P}[r\in{\mathcal{X}}_{i}]=\frac{1}{2}
. By Höeffding’s inequality, we have

\mathbb{P}\left[\left|\left|{\mathcal{M}}_{s}\right|-\frac{m}{2}\right|\geq\sqrt{\frac{m}{2}\log\left(\frac{C_{\mathrm{st}}}{\delta}\right)}\right]\leq\frac{2\delta}{C_{\mathrm{st}}},

\mathbb{P}\left[\left|\left|{\mathcal{A}}_{s}\right|-\frac{m}{2}\right|\geq\sqrt{\frac{m}{2}\log\left(\frac{C_{\mathrm{st}}}{\delta}\right)}\right]\leq\frac{2\delta}{mC_{\mathrm{st}}},

\mathbb{P}\left[\left|\left|{\mathcal{B}}_{s}\right|-\frac{m}{2}\right|\geq\sqrt{\frac{m}{2}\log\left(\frac{C_{\mathrm{st}}}{\delta}\right)}\right]\leq\frac{2\delta}{C_{\mathrm{st}}},

and

\mathbb{P}\left[\left|\left|{\mathcal{X}}_{i}\right|-\frac{m}{2}\right|\geq\sqrt{\frac{m}{2}\log\left(\frac{C_{\mathrm{st}}n_{\mathrm{st}}}{\delta}\right)}\right]\leq\frac{2\delta}{C_{\mathrm{st}}n_{\mathrm{st}}}.

\blacksquare

For the third statement, we fix arbitrary
s,s^{\prime}\in\{\pm 1\}
and
r\in[m]
.
We have

\left\langle{\bm{w}}_{s,r}^{(0)},\frac{{\bm{\mu}}_{s^{\prime}}}{\lVert{\bm{\mu}}\rVert}\right\rangle,\left\langle{\bm{w}}_{s,r}^{(0)},\frac{{\bm{\nu}}_{s^{\prime}}}{\lVert{\bm{\nu}}\rVert}\right\rangle\overset{\emph{i.i.d.}}{\sim}{\mathcal{N}}(0,\sigma_{0}^{2}).

Hence, by Höeffding’s inequality, we have

\mathbb{P}\left[\left|\left\langle{\bm{w}}_{s,r}^{(0)},\frac{{\bm{\mu}}_{s^{\prime}}}{\lVert{\bm{\mu}}\rVert}\right\rangle\right|>\sigma_{0}\sqrt{2\log\left(\frac{C_{\mathrm{st}}m}{\delta}\right)}\right]\leq\frac{2\delta}{C_{\mathrm{st}}m}.

Similarly, we also have

\mathbb{P}\left[\left|\left\langle{\bm{w}}_{s,r}^{(0)},\frac{{\bm{\nu}}_{s^{\prime}}}{\lVert{\bm{\nu}}\rVert}\right\rangle\right|>\sigma_{0}\sqrt{2\log\left(\frac{C_{\mathrm{st}}m}{\delta}\right)}\right]\leq\frac{2\delta}{C_{\mathrm{st}}m}.

\blacksquare

Before moving on to the remaining part, note that for each
i\in[n_{\mathrm{st}}],s\in\{\pm 1\}
, and
r\in[m]
, we can write
\tilde{\bm{\xi}}_{i}
and
\Pi_{S}{\bm{w}}_{s,r}^{(0)}
as

\tilde{\bm{\xi}}_{i}=\sigma_{p}\sum_{h\in[d-4]}{\mathbf{z}}_{i,h}{\bm{b}}_{h},\quad\Pi_{S}{\bm{w}}_{s,r}^{(0)}=\sigma_{0}\sum_{h\in[d-4]}{\mathbf{z}}_{s,r,h}b_{h}

where
{\mathbf{z}}_{i,h},{\mathbf{z}}_{s,r,h}\overset{\emph{i.i.d.}}{\sim}{\mathcal{N}}(0,1)
. The sub-gaussian norm of standard normal distribution
{\mathcal{N}}(0,1)
is
\sqrt{\frac{8}{3}}
and then
\left({\mathbf{z}}_{i,h}\right)^{2}-1,\left({\mathbf{z}}_{s,r,h}\right)^{2}-1
’s are mean zero sub-exponential random variables with sub-exponential norm
\frac{8}{3}
(Lemma 2.7.6 in
Vershynin 2018
).
In addition,
{\mathbf{z}}_{s,r,h}{\mathbf{z}}_{i,h}
’s and
{\mathbf{z}}_{i,h}{\mathbf{z}}_{j,h}
’s with
i\neq j
are mean zero sub-exponential random variables with sub-exponential norm less than or equal to
\frac{8}{3}
(Lemma 2.7.7 in
Vershynin 2018
).

We use Bernstein’s inequality (Theorem 2.8.1 in
Vershynin 2018
), with
c
being the absolute constant stated therein. We then have the following for any
i\in[n_{\mathrm{st}}]
:

\displaystyle\quad\mathbb{P}\left[\left|\lVert\tilde{\bm{\xi}}_{i}\rVert^{2}-\sigma_{p}^{2}(d-4)\right|\geq C_{\mathrm{st}}\sigma_{p}^{2}d^{\frac{1}{2}}\sqrt{\log\left(\frac{C_{\mathrm{st}}n_{\mathrm{st}}}{\delta}\right)}\right]

\displaystyle=\mathbb{P}\left[\left|\sum_{h\in[d-4]}\left(\left({\mathbf{z}}_{i,h}\right)^{2}-1\right)\right|\geq C_{\mathrm{st}}d^{\frac{1}{2}}\sqrt{\log\left(\frac{C_{\mathrm{st}}n_{\mathrm{st}}}{\delta}\right)}\right]

\displaystyle\leq 2\exp\left(-\frac{9cC_{\mathrm{st}}^{2}d}{64(d-4)}\log\left(\frac{C_{\mathrm{st}}n_{\mathrm{st}}}{\delta}\right)\right)

\displaystyle\leq 2\exp\left(-\log\left(\frac{C_{\mathrm{st}}n_{\mathrm{st}}}{\delta}\right)\right)\leq\frac{2\delta}{C_{\mathrm{st}}n_{\mathrm{st}}}.

\blacksquare

For
i,j\in[n_{\mathrm{st}}]
with
i\neq j
, we have

\displaystyle\quad\mathbb{P}\left[\left|\langle\tilde{\bm{\xi}}_{i},\tilde{\bm{\xi}}_{j}\rangle\right|\geq C_{\mathrm{st}}\sigma_{p}^{2}d^{\frac{1}{2}}\sqrt{\log\left(\frac{C_{\mathrm{st}}n_{\mathrm{st}}^{2}}{\delta}\right)}\right]

\displaystyle=\mathbb{P}\left[\left|\sum_{h\in[d-4]}{\mathbf{z}}_{i,h}{\mathbf{z}}_{j,h}\right|\geq C_{\mathrm{st}}d^{\frac{1}{2}}\sqrt{\log\left(\frac{C_{\mathrm{st}}n_{\mathrm{st}}^{2}}{\delta}\right)}\right]

\displaystyle\leq 2\exp\left(-\frac{9cC_{\mathrm{st}}^{2}d}{64(d-4)}\log\left(\frac{C_{\mathrm{st}}n_{\mathrm{st}}^{2}}{\delta}\right)\right)

\displaystyle\leq\frac{2\delta}{C_{\mathrm{st}}n_{\mathrm{st}}^{2}}.

\blacksquare

For any
s\in\{\pm 1\},r\in[m]
and
i\in[n_{\mathrm{st}}]
, by applying Bernstein’s inequality, we have

\displaystyle\quad\mathbb{P}\left[\left|\left\langle{\bm{w}}_{s,r}^{(0)},\tilde{\bm{\xi}}_{i}\right\rangle\right|\geq C_{\mathrm{st}}\sigma_{0}\sigma_{p}d^{\frac{1}{2}}\sqrt{\log\left(\frac{C_{\mathrm{st}}mn_{\mathrm{st}}}{\delta}\right)}\right]

\displaystyle=\mathbb{P}\left[\left|\sum_{h\in[d-4]}{\mathbf{z}}_{i,h}{\mathbf{z}}_{s,r,h}\right|\geq C_{\mathrm{st}}d^{\frac{1}{2}}\sqrt{\log\left(\frac{C_{\mathrm{st}}mn_{\mathrm{st}}}{\delta}\right)}\right]

\displaystyle\leq 2\exp\left(-\frac{9cC_{\mathrm{st}}^{2}d}{64(d-4)}\log\left(\frac{C_{\mathrm{st}}mn_{\mathrm{st}}}{\delta}\right)\right)

\displaystyle\leq\frac{2\delta}{16mn_{\mathrm{st}}}.

\blacksquare

By applying Bernstein’s inequality, for any
s\in\{\pm 1\}
and
r\in[m]
, we have

\displaystyle\quad\mathbb{P}\left[\left\|\Pi_{S}{\bm{w}}_{s,r}^{(0)}\right\|^{2}\geq 2\sigma_{0}^{2}d\right]

\displaystyle\leq\mathbb{P}\left[\left|\left\|\Pi_{S}{\bm{w}}_{s,r}^{(0)}\right\|^{2}-\sigma_{0}^{2}(d-4)\right|\geq C_{\mathrm{st}}\sigma_{0}^{2}d^{\frac{1}{2}}\sqrt{\log\left(\frac{C_{\mathrm{st}}m}{\delta}\right)}\right]

\displaystyle=\mathbb{P}\left[\left|\sum_{h\in[d-4]}\left(\left({\mathbf{z}}_{s,r,h}\right)^{2}-1\right)\right|\geq C_{\mathrm{st}}d^{\frac{1}{2}}\sqrt{\log\left(\frac{C_{\mathrm{st}}m}{\delta}\right)}\right]

\displaystyle\leq 2\exp\left(-\frac{9cC_{\mathrm{st}}^{2}d}{64(d-4)}\log\left(\frac{C_{\mathrm{st}}m}{\delta}\right)\right)

\displaystyle\leq 2\exp\left(-\log\left(\frac{C_{\mathrm{st}}n_{\mathrm{st}}}{\delta}\right)\right)\leq\frac{2\delta}{C_{\mathrm{st}}m},

where the first inequality follows from
(C1)
.

\blacksquare

From union bound and a large choice of universal constant
C_{\mathrm{st}}>0
, we conclude that the event
E_{\mathrm{st}}
occurs with probability at least
1-\delta
.
∎

A.2.3
Properties Used Throughout the Proof

We introduce some notation and properties that are frequently used throughout the proof.

Let us define

\alpha_{\mathrm{st}}:=2C_{\mathrm{st}}\sigma_{0}\max\left\{\left\|{\bm{\mu}}\right\|,\left\|{\bm{\nu}}\right\|,\sigma_{p}d^{\frac{1}{2}}\right\}\sqrt{2\log\left(\frac{C_{\mathrm{st}}mn_{\mathrm{st}}}{\delta}\right)},

\beta_{\mathrm{st}}:=4C_{\mathrm{st}}n_{\mathrm{st}}\sqrt{\frac{1}{d}\log\left(\frac{C_{\mathrm{st}}n_{\mathrm{st}}}{\delta}\right)},

and

\kappa_{\mathrm{st}}:=8\log(12),\quad\lambda_{\mathrm{st}}:=\exp(2\kappa_{\mathrm{st}}).

Under Condition
3.1
and the event
E_{\mathrm{st}}
, the following hold:

•

\alpha_{\mathrm{st}}
and
\beta_{\mathrm{st}}
are small enough to satisfy

\alpha_{\mathrm{st}}\leq\min\left\{\frac{1}{100},\frac{p_{\mathrm{b}}n_{\mathrm{st}}\left\|{\bm{\nu}}\right\|^{2}}{\sigma_{p}^{2}d},\frac{\sigma_{p}^{2}d}{(2p_{\mathrm{e}}+p_{\mathrm{b}})n_{\mathrm{st}}\left\|{\bm{\mu}}\right\|^{2}}\right\},\quad\beta_{\mathrm{st}}\log T^{*}\leq\frac{1}{100}.

(6)

•

For any
s,s^{\prime}\in\{\pm 1\},r\in[m],
and
i\in[n_{\mathrm{st}}]
,

\left|\left\langle{\bm{w}}_{s,r}^{(0)},{\bm{\mu}}_{s^{\prime}}\right\rangle\right|,\left|\left\langle{\bm{w}}_{s,r}^{(0)},{\bm{\nu}}_{s^{\prime}}\right\rangle\right|,\left|\left\langle{\bm{w}}_{s,r}^{(0)},\tilde{\bm{\xi}}_{i}\right\rangle\right|\leq\alpha_{\mathrm{st}}.

(7)

•

From
(C3)
, for any
i,j\in[n_{\mathrm{st}}]
with
i\neq j
, we have

\frac{\sigma_{p}^{2}d}{2}\leq\|\tilde{\bm{\xi}}_{i}\|^{2}\leq\frac{3\sigma_{p}^{2}d}{2},\frac{\left|\langle\tilde{\bm{\xi}}_{i},\tilde{\bm{\xi}}_{j}\rangle\right|}{\lVert\tilde{\bm{\xi}}_{i}\rVert^{2}}\leq\frac{\beta_{\mathrm{st}}}{n_{\mathrm{st}}},\,\left|1-\frac{\lVert\tilde{\bm{\xi}}_{j}\rVert^{2}}{\lVert\tilde{\bm{\xi}}_{i}\rVert^{2}}\right|\leq\frac{\beta_{\mathrm{st}}}{n_{\mathrm{st}}},\,\left|\|\tilde{\bm{\xi}}_{i}\|^{2}-\sigma_{p}^{2}(d-4)\right|\leq\frac{\beta_{\mathrm{st}}\sigma_{p}^{2}d}{n_{\mathrm{st}}}.

(8)

•

For any
s\in\{\pm 1\},r\in[m],
and
i\in[n_{\mathrm{st}}]
, we have

\left|\frac{\left|{\mathcal{M}}_{s}\right|}{m}-\frac{1}{2}\right|,\left|\frac{\left|{\mathcal{A}}_{s}\right|}{m}-\frac{1}{2}\right|,\left|\frac{\left|{\mathcal{B}}_{s}\right|}{m}-\frac{1}{2}\right|,\left|\frac{\left|{\mathcal{X}}_{i}\right|}{m}-\frac{1}{2}\right|\leq\frac{1}{10}.

(9)

•

The learning rate
\eta
is small enough to satisfy

\eta\leq\min\left\{\frac{\beta_{\mathrm{st}}mn_{\mathrm{st}}}{2\sigma_{p}^{2}d},\frac{\beta_{\mathrm{st}}m}{2\lambda_{\mathrm{st}}\left\|{\bm{\mu}}\right\|^{2}},\frac{\beta_{\mathrm{st}}m}{2\lambda_{\mathrm{st}}\left\|{\bm{\nu}}\right\|^{2}}\right\}.

(10)

A.2.4
Technical Lemma

We also introduce a technical lemma that enables a tight characterization of the learning dynamics.

Lemma A.5

(Lemma D.1 in
Meng et al. 2024
)
.

Suppose that a sequence
a_{t},t\geq 0
follows the iterative formula

a_{t+1}=a_{t}+\frac{c}{1+be^{a_{t}}},

for some
c\in[0,1]
and
b\geq 0
. Then it holds that

x_{t}\leq a_{t}\leq\frac{c}{1+be^{a_{0}}}+x_{t}

for all
t\geq 0
. Here,
x_{t}
is the unique solution of

x_{t}+be^{x_{t}}=ct+a_{0}+be^{a_{0}}.

Appendix B
Proof of Theorem
3.3

For the proof, we first introduce properties preserved during training (Appendix
B.1
), then prove the convergence of the training loss (Appendix
B.2
), and finally establish a bound on the test error (Appendix
B.3
).

B.1
Preserved Properties during Training

In this subsection, we present several properties that remain preserved throughout training.

Lemma B.1
.

Under Condition
3.2
and the event
E_{\mathrm{wk}}
, we have the following for any iteration
t\in[0,T^{*}]
:

(W1)

0\leq\rho_{i}^{(t)}\leq 4\log T^{*}
for any
i\in[n_{\mathrm{wk}}]
.

(W2)

\frac{n_{\mathrm{wk}}(2p_{\mathrm{e}}+p_{\mathrm{b}})}{12}\mathrm{SNR}_{\bm{\mu}}^{2}\cdot\rho_{i}^{(t)}\leq M_{s}^{(t)}\leq 3n_{\mathrm{wk}}(2p_{\mathrm{e}}+p_{\mathrm{b}})\mathrm{SNR}_{\bm{\mu}}^{2}\cdot\rho_{i}^{(t)}
for any
i\in[n_{\mathrm{wk}}],s\in\{\pm 1\}
.

(W3)

\left|\rho_{i}^{(t)}-\rho_{j}^{(t)}\right|\leq\frac{\kappa_{\mathrm{wk}}}{4}
for any
i,j\in[n_{\mathrm{wk}}]
.

(W4)

\left|y_{i}f_{\mathrm{wk}}\left({\bm{w}}^{(t)},{\bm{X}}_{i}\right)-y_{j}f_{\mathrm{wk}}\left({\bm{w}}^{(t)},{\bm{X}}_{j}\right)\right|\leq\frac{\kappa_{\mathrm{wk}}}{2}
for any
i,j\in[n_{\mathrm{wk}}]
.

(W5)

1-\kappa_{\mathrm{wk}}\leq\frac{g_{j}^{(t)}}{g_{i}^{(t)}}\leq 1+\kappa_{\mathrm{wk}}
for any
i,j\in[n_{\mathrm{wk}}]
.

(W6)

\left|N_{s}^{(t)}\right|\leq(2p_{\mathrm{h}}+p_{\mathrm{b}})n_{\mathrm{wk}}\mathrm{SNR}_{\bm{\nu}}^{2}\cdot\rho_{i}^{(t)}
for any
s\in\{\pm 1\},i\in[n]
.

Proof of Lemma
B.1
.

It is trivial for the case
t=0
. Assume the conclusions hold at iteration
t=\tau
and we will prove for the case
t=\tau+1
.
Note that
(W2)
and
(W6)
at iteration
t=\tau
, along with
(C5)
and
(C6)
imply that

\left|N_{s}^{(\tau)}\right|\leq(2p_{\mathrm{h}}+p_{\mathrm{b}})n_{\mathrm{wk}}\mathrm{SNR}_{\bm{\nu}}^{2}\cdot\rho_{i}^{(\tau)}\leq\frac{1}{24}n_{\mathrm{wk}}(2p_{\mathrm{e}}+p_{\mathrm{b}})\mathrm{SNR}_{\bm{\mu}}^{2}\cdot\rho_{i}^{(\tau)}\leq\frac{1}{2}M_{s^{\prime}}^{(\tau)},

(11)

for any
s,s^{\prime}\in\{\pm 1\}
and
i\in[n]
.

(W1)
: We fix an arbitrary
i\in[n_{\mathrm{wk}}]
and we want to show
\rho_{i}^{(\tau+1)}\leq 4\log T^{*}
.
If
\rho_{i}^{(\tau)}\leq 2\log T^{*}
, then we have

\rho_{i}^{(\tau+1)}=\rho_{i}^{(\tau)}+\frac{\eta}{n_{\mathrm{wk}}}g_{i}^{(\tau)}\lVert{\bm{\xi}}_{i}\rVert^{2}\leq 2\log T^{*}+\frac{\eta}{n_{\mathrm{wk}}}\cdot\frac{3\sigma_{p}^{2}d}{2}\leq 4\log T^{*},

where the first inequality follows from
g_{i}^{(\tau)}\leq 1
and (
4
), and the last inequality follows from
(C4)
.

Otherwise, there exists
\hat{t}<\tau
such that
\rho_{i}^{(\hat{t})}\leq 2\log T^{*}<\rho_{i}^{(\hat{t}+1)}
since
\rho_{i}^{(t)}
is increasing in iteration
t
.

From (
4
) and
(C4)
, we have

\displaystyle\rho_{i}^{(\tau+1)}

\displaystyle=\rho_{i}^{(\hat{t})}+\left(\rho_{i}^{(\hat{t}+1)}-\rho_{i}^{(\hat{t})}\right)+\sum_{t=\hat{t}+1}^{\tau}\left(\rho_{i}^{(t+1)}-\rho_{i}^{(t)}\right)

\displaystyle=\rho_{i}^{(\hat{t})}+\frac{\eta}{n_{\mathrm{wk}}}g_{i}^{(\hat{t})}\left\|{\bm{\xi}}_{i}\right\|^{2}+\frac{\eta}{n_{\mathrm{wk}}}\sum_{t=\hat{t}+1}^{\tau}g_{i}^{(t)}\left\|{\bm{\xi}}_{i}\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle\leq 2\log T^{*}+\frac{\eta}{n_{\mathrm{wk}}}\cdot\frac{3}{2}\sigma_{p}^{2}d+\frac{\eta}{n_{\mathrm{wk}}}\cdot\frac{3}{2}\sigma_{p}^{2}d\sum_{t=\hat{t}+1}^{\tau}g_{i}^{(t)}

\displaystyle\leq 3\log T^{*}+\frac{3\eta\sigma_{p}^{2}d}{2n_{\mathrm{wk}}}\sum_{t=\hat{t}+1}^{\tau}\exp{\left(-y_{i}f_{\mathrm{wk}}\left({\bm{w}}^{(t)},{\bm{X}}_{i}\right)\right)}.

For any iteration
t\in\left[\hat{t}+1,\tau\right]
, we have

\displaystyle y_{i}f_{\mathrm{wk}}\left({\bm{w}}^{(t)},{\bm{X}}_{i}\right)

\displaystyle=\left\langle{\bm{w}}^{(t)},y_{i}{\bm{v}}_{i}^{(1)}\right\rangle+\left\langle{\bm{w}}^{(t)},y_{i}{\bm{v}}_{i}^{(2)}\right\rangle+\left\langle{\bm{w}}^{(t)},y_{i}{\bm{\xi}}_{i}\right\rangle

\displaystyle\geq-2\max\left\{\left|N_{1}^{(t)}\right|,\left|N_{-1}^{(t)}\right|\right\}+\rho_{i}^{(t)}+\sum_{j\in[n_{\mathrm{wk}}]\backslash\{i\}}y_{i}y_{j}\rho_{j}^{(t)}\frac{\left\langle{\bm{\xi}}_{i},{\bm{\xi}}_{j}\right\rangle}{\left\lVert{\bm{\xi}}_{j}\right\rVert^{2}}

\displaystyle\geq-2\max\left\{\left|N_{1}^{(t)}\right|,\left|N_{-1}^{(t)}\right|\right\}+\rho_{i}^{(t)}-\sum_{j\in[n_{\mathrm{wk}}]\backslash\{i\}}\rho_{j}^{(t)}\frac{\left|\left\langle{\bm{\xi}}_{i},{\bm{\xi}}_{j}\right\rangle\right|}{\left\lVert{\bm{\xi}}_{j}\right\rVert^{2}}

\displaystyle\geq-4n_{\mathrm{wk}}(2p_{\mathrm{h}}+p_{\mathrm{b}})\mathrm{SNR}_{\bm{\nu}}^{2}\cdot\rho_{i}^{(t)}+\rho_{i}^{(t)}-\sum_{j\in[n_{\mathrm{wk}}]\backslash\{i\}}\rho_{j}^{(t)}\frac{\left|\left\langle{\bm{\xi}}_{i},{\bm{\xi}}_{j}\right\rangle\right|}{\left\lVert{\bm{\xi}}_{j}\right\rVert^{2}}

\displaystyle\geq-4n_{\mathrm{wk}}(2p_{\mathrm{h}}+p_{\mathrm{b}})\mathrm{SNR}_{\bm{\nu}}^{2}\cdot 4\log T^{*}+2\log T^{*}-4\log T^{*}\cdot\beta_{\mathrm{wk}}

\displaystyle=\left(1-8n_{\mathrm{wk}}(2p_{\mathrm{h}}+p_{\mathrm{b}})\mathrm{SNR}_{\bm{\nu}}^{2}-2\beta_{\mathrm{wk}}\right)\cdot 2\log T^{*}

\displaystyle\geq\log T^{*},

where the first inequality follows from the fact that
M_{1}^{(t)},M_{-1}^{(t)}\geq 0
, the third from applying
(W2)
at iteration
t
, the fourth from
(W1)
at iteration
t
and (
4
), and the last from (
3
) and Condition
3.2
.

Now, we have our conclusion

\displaystyle\rho_{i}^{(\tau+1)}

\displaystyle\leq 3\log T^{*}+\frac{3\eta\sigma_{p}^{2}d}{2n_{\mathrm{wk}}}\sum_{t=\hat{t}+1}^{\tau}\exp{\left(-y_{i}f_{\mathrm{wk}}\left({\bm{w}}^{(t)},{\bm{X}}_{i}\right)\right)}

\displaystyle\leq 3\log T^{*}+\frac{3\eta\sigma_{p}^{2}d}{2n_{\mathrm{wk}}}\sum_{t=\hat{t}+1}^{\tau}\exp{\left(-\log T^{*}\right)}

\displaystyle\leq 3\log T^{*}+\frac{3\eta\sigma_{p}^{2}d}{2n_{\mathrm{wk}}}T^{*}\exp{\left(-\log T^{*}\right)}

\displaystyle\leq 4\log T^{*},

where we applied
(C4)
for the last inequality.

(W2)
: We fix arbitrary
s\in\{\pm 1\}
and
i\in[n_{\mathrm{wk}}]
. We have

\displaystyle M_{s}^{(\tau+1)}-M_{s}^{(\tau)}

\displaystyle=\frac{\eta}{n_{\mathrm{wk}}}\left(\sum_{j\in{\mathcal{S}}_{{\bm{\mu}}_{s}}^{(1)}}g_{j}^{(\tau)}+\sum_{j\in{\mathcal{S}}_{{\bm{\mu}}_{s}}^{(2)}}g_{j}^{(\tau)}\right)\cdot\lVert{\bm{\mu}}\rVert^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{\eta}{n_{\mathrm{wk}}}\cdot 2\cdot\left(\frac{p_{\mathrm{e}}}{2}+\frac{p_{\mathrm{b}}}{4}+\gamma_{\mathrm{wk}}\right)n_{\mathrm{wk}}\cdot\left(g_{i}^{(\tau)}\left(1+\kappa_{\mathrm{wk}}\right)\right)\cdot\lVert{\bm{\mu}}\rVert^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{\eta}{n_{\mathrm{wk}}}\cdot 2\cdot\frac{3}{2}\left(\frac{p_{\mathrm{e}}}{2}+\frac{p_{\mathrm{b}}}{4}\right)n_{\mathrm{wk}}\cdot 2g_{i}^{(\tau)}\cdot\lVert{\bm{\mu}}\rVert^{2}

\displaystyle=\frac{3}{2}\eta\left(2p_{\mathrm{e}}+p_{\mathrm{b}}\right)g_{i}^{(\tau)}\lVert{\bm{\mu}}\rVert^{2},

where the first inequality follows from
(W3)
at iteration
\tau
and (
5
), the second follows from (
3
).

From (
4
), we have

\rho_{i}^{(\tau+1)}-\rho_{i}^{(\tau)}=\frac{\eta}{n_{\mathrm{wk}}}g_{i}^{(\tau)}\lVert{\bm{\xi}}_{i}\rVert^{2}\geq\frac{\eta\sigma_{p}^{2}d}{2n_{\mathrm{wk}}}g_{i}^{(\tau)},

and thus,

M_{s}^{(\tau+1)}-M_{s}^{(\tau)}\leq 3n_{\mathrm{wk}}\left(2p_{\mathrm{e}}+p_{\mathrm{b}}\right)\mathrm{SNR}_{\bm{\mu}}^{2}\left(\rho_{i}^{(\tau+1)}-\rho_{i}^{(\tau)}\right).

Combining with
(W2)
at iteration
\tau
, we have

\displaystyle M_{s}^{(\tau+1)}

\displaystyle=M_{s}^{(\tau)}+\left(M_{s}^{(\tau+1)}-M_{s}^{(\tau)}\right)

\displaystyle\leq 3n_{\mathrm{wk}}(2p_{\mathrm{e}}+p_{\mathrm{b}})\mathrm{SNR}_{\bm{\mu}}^{2}\cdot\rho_{i}^{(\tau)}+3n_{\mathrm{wk}}\left(2p_{\mathrm{e}}+p_{\mathrm{b}}\right)\mathrm{SNR}_{\bm{\mu}}^{2}\left(\rho_{i}^{(\tau+1)}-\rho_{i}^{(\tau)}\right)

\displaystyle=3n_{\mathrm{wk}}(2p_{\mathrm{e}}+p_{\mathrm{b}})\mathrm{SNR}_{\bm{\mu}}^{2}\cdot\rho_{i}^{(\tau+1)}.

Similarly, we have

\displaystyle M_{s}^{(\tau+1)}-M_{s}^{(\tau)}

\displaystyle=\frac{\eta}{n_{\mathrm{wk}}}\left(\sum_{j\in{\mathcal{S}}_{{\bm{\mu}}_{s}}^{(1)}}g_{j}^{(\tau)}+\sum_{j\in{\mathcal{S}}_{{\bm{\mu}}_{s}}^{(2)}}g_{j}^{(\tau)}\right)\lVert{\bm{\mu}}\rVert^{2}

\displaystyle\geq\frac{\eta}{n_{\mathrm{wk}}}\cdot 2\cdot\left(\frac{p_{\mathrm{e}}}{2}+\frac{p_{\mathrm{b}}}{4}-\gamma_{\mathrm{wk}}\right)n_{\mathrm{wk}}\cdot\left(g_{i}^{(\tau)}\left(1-\kappa_{\mathrm{wk}}\right)\right)\lVert{\bm{\mu}}\rVert^{2}

\displaystyle\geq\frac{\eta}{n_{\mathrm{wk}}}\cdot 2\cdot\frac{1}{2}\left(\frac{p_{\mathrm{e}}}{2}+\frac{p_{\mathrm{b}}}{4}\right)n_{\mathrm{wk}}\cdot\frac{1}{2}g_{i}^{(\tau)}\cdot\lVert{\bm{\mu}}\rVert^{2}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{8}\eta\left(2p_{\mathrm{e}}+p_{\mathrm{b}}\right)g_{i}^{(\tau)}\cdot\lVert{\bm{\mu}}\rVert^{2},

where the first inequality follows from
(W5)
at iteration
\tau
and (
5
), and the second follows from (
3
).
From (
4
), we have

\rho_{i}^{(\tau+1)}-\rho_{i}^{(\tau)}=\frac{\eta}{n_{\mathrm{wk}}}g_{i}^{(\tau)}\lVert{\bm{\xi}}_{i}\rVert^{2}\leq\frac{3\eta\sigma_{p}^{2}d}{2n_{\mathrm{wk}}}g_{i}^{(\tau)},

and thus, we have

M_{s}^{(\tau+1)}-M_{s}^{(\tau)}\geq\frac{1}{12}n_{\mathrm{wk}}\left(2p_{\mathrm{e}}+p_{\mathrm{b}}\right)\mathrm{SNR}_{\bm{\mu}}^{2}\left(\rho_{i}^{(\tau+1)}-\rho_{i}^{(\tau)}\right).

Combining with
(W2)
at iteration
\tau
, we have

\displaystyle M_{s}^{(\tau+1)}

\displaystyle=M_{s}^{(\tau)}+\left(M_{s}^{(\tau+1)}-M_{s}^{(\tau)}\right)

\displaystyle\geq\frac{1}{12}n_{\mathrm{wk}}(2p_{\mathrm{e}}+p_{\mathrm{b}})\mathrm{SNR}_{\bm{\mu}}^{2}\cdot\rho_{i}^{(\tau)}+\frac{1}{12}n_{\mathrm{wk}}\left(2p_{\mathrm{e}}+p_{\mathrm{b}}\right)\mathrm{SNR}_{\bm{\mu}}^{2}\left(\rho_{i}^{(\tau+1)}-\rho_{i}^{(\tau)}\right)

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{12}n_{\mathrm{wk}}(2p_{\mathrm{e}}+p_{\mathrm{b}})\mathrm{SNR}_{\bm{\mu}}^{2}\cdot\rho_{i}^{(\tau+1)}.

(W3)
: We fix arbitrary
i,j\in[n_{\mathrm{wk}}]
with
i\neq j
. Without loss of generality, we assume that
\rho_{i}^{(\tau)}\geq\rho_{j}^{(\tau)}
.
From (
4
) and
(C4)
, we have

\rho_{i}^{(\tau+1)}-\rho_{j}^{(\tau+1)}=\rho_{i}^{(\tau)}-\rho_{j}^{(\tau)}+\frac{\eta}{n_{\mathrm{wk}}}\left(g_{i}^{(\tau)}\left\lVert{\bm{\xi}}_{i}\right\rVert^{2}-g_{j}^{(\tau)}\left\lVert{\bm{\xi}}_{j}\right\rVert^{2}\right)\geq-\frac{\eta}{n_{\mathrm{wk}}}\cdot\frac{3\sigma_{p}^{2}d}{2}\geq-\frac{\kappa_{\mathrm{wk}}}{4}.

Thus, we want to show that
\rho_{i}^{(\tau+1)}-\rho_{j}^{(\tau+1)}\leq\frac{\kappa_{\mathrm{wk}}}{4}
.

If
\rho_{i}^{(\tau)}-\rho_{j}^{(\tau)}<\frac{\kappa_{\mathrm{wk}}}{8}
, from triangular inequality, (
4
), and
(C4)
, we have

\rho_{i}^{(\tau+1)}-\rho_{j}^{(\tau+1)}=\rho_{i}^{(\tau)}-\rho_{j}^{(\tau)}+\frac{\eta}{n_{\mathrm{wk}}}\left(g_{i}^{(\tau)}\left\lVert{\bm{\xi}}_{i}\right\rVert^{2}-g_{j}^{(\tau)}\left\lVert{\bm{\xi}}_{j}\right\rVert^{2}\right)\leq\frac{\kappa_{\mathrm{wk}}}{8}+\frac{\eta}{n_{\mathrm{wk}}}\cdot\frac{3\sigma_{p}^{2}d}{2}\leq\frac{\kappa_{\mathrm{wk}}}{4}.

Otherwise, we have

\displaystyle\quad y_{i}f_{\mathrm{wk}}\left({\bm{w}}^{(\tau)},{\bm{X}}_{i}\right)-y_{j}f_{\mathrm{wk}}\left({\bm{w}}^{(\tau)},{\bm{X}}_{j}\right)

\displaystyle=\left\langle{\bm{w}}^{(\tau)},y_{i}\left({\bm{v}}_{i}^{(1)}+{\bm{v}}_{i}^{(2)}+{\bm{\xi}}_{i}\right)\right\rangle-\left\langle{\bm{w}}^{(\tau)},y_{j}\left({\bm{v}}_{j}^{(1)}+{\bm{v}}_{j}^{(2)}+{\bm{\xi}}_{j}\right)\right\rangle

\displaystyle\geq\left(\rho_{i}^{(\tau)}-\rho_{j}^{(\tau)}\right)-3M_{y_{j}}^{(\tau)}+\sum_{i^{\prime}\in[n_{\mathrm{wk}}]\backslash\{i\}}y_{i}y_{i^{\prime}}\rho_{i^{\prime}}^{(\tau)}\frac{\left|\left\langle{\bm{\xi}}_{i},{\bm{\xi}}_{i^{\prime}}\right\rangle\right|}{\left\lVert{\bm{\xi}}_{i^{\prime}}\right\rVert^{2}}-\sum_{j^{\prime}\in[n_{\mathrm{wk}}]\backslash\{j\}}y_{j}y_{j^{\prime}}\rho_{j^{\prime}}^{(\tau)}\frac{\left|\left\langle{\bm{\xi}}_{j},{\bm{\xi}}_{j^{\prime}}\right\rangle\right|}{\left\lVert{\bm{\xi}}_{j^{\prime}}\right\rVert^{2}}

\displaystyle\geq\left(\rho_{i}^{(\tau)}-\rho_{j}^{(\tau)}\right)-3M_{y_{j}}^{(\tau)}-\sum_{i^{\prime}\in[n_{\mathrm{wk}}]\backslash\{i\}}\rho_{i^{\prime}}^{(\tau)}\frac{\left|\left\langle{\bm{\xi}}_{i},{\bm{\xi}}_{i^{\prime}}\right\rangle\right|}{\left\lVert{\bm{\xi}}_{i^{\prime}}\right\rVert^{2}}-\sum_{j^{\prime}\in[n_{\mathrm{wk}}]\backslash\{j\}}\rho_{j^{\prime}}^{(\tau)}\frac{\left|\left\langle{\bm{\xi}}_{j},{\bm{\xi}}_{j^{\prime}}\right\rangle\right|}{\left\lVert{\bm{\xi}}_{j^{\prime}}\right\rVert^{2}}

\displaystyle\geq\frac{\kappa_{\mathrm{wk}}}{8}-3\cdot 3n_{\mathrm{wk}}(2p_{\mathrm{e}}+p_{\mathrm{b}})\mathrm{SNR}_{\bm{\mu}}^{2}\cdot 4\log T^{*}-2\cdot 4\log T^{*}\cdot\beta_{\mathrm{wk}}

\displaystyle\geq\frac{\kappa_{\mathrm{wk}}}{16}>0,

where the first inequality follows from (
11
), and the fourth inequality follows from (
3
) and Condition
3.2
.
Then, we have

\displaystyle\frac{g_{i}^{(\tau)}\left\lVert{\bm{\xi}}_{i}\right\rVert^{2}}{g_{j}^{(\tau)}\left\lVert{\bm{\xi}}_{j}\right\rVert^{2}}

\displaystyle=\frac{1+\exp{\left(y_{j}f_{\mathrm{wk}}\left({\bm{w}}^{(\tau)},{\bm{X}}_{j}\right)\right)}}{1+\exp{\left(y_{i}f_{\mathrm{wk}}\left({\bm{w}}^{(\tau)},{\bm{X}}_{i}\right)\right)}}\cdot\frac{\left\lVert{\bm{\xi}}_{i}\right\rVert^{2}}{\left\lVert{\bm{\xi}}_{j}\right\rVert^{2}}

\displaystyle\leq\exp{\left[y_{j}f_{\mathrm{wk}}\left({\bm{w}}^{(\tau)},{\bm{X}}_{j}\right)-y_{i}f_{\mathrm{wk}}\left({\bm{w}}^{(\tau)},{\bm{X}}_{i}\right)\right]}\cdot\left(1+\frac{\beta_{\mathrm{wk}}}{n_{\mathrm{wk}}}\right)

\displaystyle\leq\exp{\left[-\frac{\kappa_{\mathrm{wk}}}{16}+\frac{\beta_{\mathrm{wk}}}{n_{\mathrm{wk}}}\right]}

\displaystyle\leq 1.

Therefore, we have

\rho_{i}^{(\tau+1)}-\rho_{j}^{(\tau+1)}=\rho_{i}^{(\tau)}-\rho_{j}^{(\tau)}+\frac{\eta}{n_{\mathrm{wk}}}\left(g_{i}^{(\tau)}\left\lVert{\bm{\xi}}_{i}\right\rVert^{2}-g_{j}^{(\tau)}\left\lVert{\bm{\xi}}_{j}\right\rVert^{2}\right)\leq\rho_{i}^{(\tau)}-\rho_{j}^{(\tau)}\leq\frac{\kappa_{\mathrm{wk}}}{4}.

(W4)
:
For any
i,j\in[n_{\mathrm{wk}}]
, we have

\displaystyle\quad y_{i}f_{\mathrm{wk}}\left({\bm{w}}^{(\tau+1)},{\bm{X}}_{i}\right)-y_{j}f_{\mathrm{wk}}\left({\bm{w}}^{(\tau+1)},{\bm{X}}_{j}\right)

\displaystyle=\left\langle{\bm{w}}^{(\tau+1)},y_{i}\left({\bm{v}}_{i}^{(1)}+{\bm{v}}_{i}^{(2)}+{\bm{\xi}}_{i}\right)\right\rangle-\left\langle{\bm{w}}^{(\tau+1)},y_{j}\left({\bm{v}}_{j}^{(1)}+{\bm{v}}_{j}^{(2)}+{\bm{\xi}}_{j}\right)\right\rangle

\displaystyle\leq\left(\rho_{i}^{(\tau+1)}-\rho_{j}^{(\tau+1)}\right)+3M_{y_{j}}^{(\tau+1)}

\displaystyle\qquad+\sum_{i^{\prime}\in[n_{\mathrm{wk}}]\backslash\{i\}}y_{i}y_{i^{\prime}}\rho_{i^{\prime}}^{(\tau+1)}\frac{\left\langle{\bm{\xi}}_{i},{\bm{\xi}}_{i^{\prime}}\right\rangle}{\left\lVert{\bm{\xi}}_{i^{\prime}}\right\rVert^{2}}-\sum_{j^{\prime}\in[n_{\mathrm{wk}}]\backslash\{j\}}y_{j}y_{j^{\prime}}\rho_{j^{\prime}}^{(\tau+1)}\frac{\left\langle{\bm{\xi}}_{j},{\bm{\xi}}_{j^{\prime}}\right\rangle}{\left\lVert{\bm{\xi}}_{j^{\prime}}\right\rVert^{2}}

\displaystyle\leq\left(\rho_{i}^{(\tau+1)}-\rho_{j}^{(\tau+1)}\right)+3M_{y_{j}}^{(\tau+1)}+\sum_{i^{\prime}\in[n_{\mathrm{wk}}]\backslash\{i\}}\rho_{i^{\prime}}^{(\tau+1)}\frac{\left|\left\langle{\bm{\xi}}_{i},{\bm{\xi}}_{i^{\prime}}\right\rangle\right|}{\left\lVert{\bm{\xi}}_{i^{\prime}}\right\rVert^{2}}+\sum_{j^{\prime}\in[n_{\mathrm{wk}}]\backslash\{j\}}\rho_{j^{\prime}}^{(\tau+1)}\frac{\left|\left\langle{\bm{\xi}}_{j},{\bm{\xi}}_{j^{\prime}}\right\rangle\right|}{\left\lVert{\bm{\xi}}_{j^{\prime}}\right\rVert^{2}}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{\kappa_{\mathrm{wk}}}{8}+3\cdot 3n_{\mathrm{wk}}(2p_{\mathrm{e}}+p_{\mathrm{b}})\mathrm{SNR}_{\bm{\mu}}^{2}\cdot 4\log T^{*}+2\cdot 4\log T^{*}\cdot\beta_{\mathrm{wk}}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{\kappa_{\mathrm{wk}}}{2},

where the first inequality follows from (
11
), the third inequality follows from
(W1)
and
(W2)
at iteration
\tau+1
, which we have shown earlier, and the last inequality is due to (
3
) and Condition
3.2
.

(W5)
: Let us fix arbitrary
i,j\in[n_{\mathrm{wk}}]
and assume
y_{i}f_{\mathrm{wk}}\left({\bm{w}}^{(\tau+1)},{\bm{X}}_{i}\right)\geq y_{j}f_{\mathrm{wk}}\left({\bm{w}}^{(\tau+1)},{\bm{X}}_{j}\right)
, without loss of generality. Then, we have

\displaystyle 1\leq\frac{g_{j}^{(\tau+1)}}{g_{i}^{(\tau+1)}}

\displaystyle=\frac{1+\exp{\left(y_{i}f_{\mathrm{wk}}\left({\bm{w}}^{(\tau+1)},{\bm{X}}_{i}\right)\right)}}{1+\exp{\left(y_{j}f_{\mathrm{wk}}\left({\bm{w}}^{(\tau+1)},{\bm{X}}_{j}\right)\right)}}

\displaystyle\leq\exp{\left[y_{i}f_{\mathrm{wk}}\left({\bm{w}}^{(\tau+1)},{\bm{X}}_{i}\right)-y_{j}f_{\mathrm{wk}}\left({\bm{w}}^{(\tau+1)},{\bm{X}}_{j}\right)\right]}

\displaystyle\leq 1+2\left[y_{i}f_{\mathrm{wk}}\left({\bm{w}}^{(\tau+1)},{\bm{X}}_{i}\right)-y_{j}f_{\mathrm{wk}}\left({\bm{w}}^{(\tau+1)},{\bm{X}}_{j}\right)\right]

\displaystyle\leq 1+\kappa_{\mathrm{wk}},

where we use the inequality
e^{z}\leq 1+2z
for any
z\in(0,1)
, which is applicable due to
(W4)
at iteration
\tau+1
.
In addition, we have

\displaystyle 1\geq\frac{g_{i}^{(\tau+1)}}{g_{j}^{(\tau+1)}}

\displaystyle=\frac{1+\exp{\left(y_{j}f_{\mathrm{wk}}\left({\bm{w}}^{(\tau+1)},{\bm{X}}_{j}\right)\right)}}{1+\exp{\left(y_{i}f_{\mathrm{wk}}\left({\bm{w}}^{(\tau+1)},{\bm{X}}_{i}\right)\right)}}

\displaystyle\geq\exp{\left[y_{j}f_{\mathrm{wk}}\left({\bm{w}}^{(\tau+1)},{\bm{X}}_{j}\right)-y_{i}f_{\mathrm{wk}}\left({\bm{w}}^{(\tau+1)},{\bm{X}}_{i}\right)\right]}

\displaystyle\geq 1+\left[y_{j}f_{\mathrm{wk}}\left({\bm{w}}^{(\tau+1)},{\bm{X}}_{j}\right)-y_{i}f_{\mathrm{wk}}\left({\bm{w}}^{(\tau+1)},{\bm{X}}_{i}\right)\right]

\displaystyle\geq 1-\kappa_{\mathrm{wk}},

where we use the inequality
e^{z}\geq 1+z
for any
z\in\mathbb{R}
.

(W6)
: We fix arbitrary
s\in\{\pm 1\}
and
i\in[n_{\mathrm{wk}}]
. We have

\displaystyle\quad N_{s}^{(\tau+1)}-N_{s}^{(\tau)}

\displaystyle=\frac{\eta}{n_{\mathrm{wk}}}\left(\sum_{j\in{\mathcal{S}}_{{\bm{\nu}}_{s}}^{(1)}}g_{j}^{(\tau)}+\sum_{j\in{\mathcal{S}}_{{\bm{\nu}}_{s}}^{(2)}}g_{j}^{(\tau)}-\sum_{j\in{\mathcal{S}}_{-{\bm{\nu}}_{s}}^{(1)}}g_{j}^{(\tau)}-\sum_{j\in{\mathcal{S}}_{-{\bm{\nu}}_{s}}^{(2)}}g_{j}^{(\tau)}\right)\lVert{\bm{\nu}}\rVert^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{\eta}{n_{\mathrm{wk}}}\left[\left(\left|{\mathcal{S}}_{{\bm{\nu}}_{s}}^{(1)}\right|+\left|{\mathcal{S}}_{{\bm{\nu}}_{s}}^{(2)}\right|\right)\left(1+\kappa_{\mathrm{wk}}\right)-\left(\left|{\mathcal{S}}_{-{\bm{\nu}}_{s}}^{(1)}\right|+\left|{\mathcal{S}}_{-{\bm{\nu}}_{s}}^{(2)}\right|\right)\left(1-\kappa_{\mathrm{wk}}\right)\right]g_{i}^{(\tau)}\lVert{\bm{\nu}}\rVert^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\eta\left[2\left(\frac{p_{\mathrm{h}}}{4}+\frac{p_{\mathrm{b}}}{8}+\gamma_{\mathrm{wk}}\right)\left(1+\kappa_{\mathrm{wk}}\right)-2\left(\frac{p_{\mathrm{h}}}{4}+\frac{p_{\mathrm{b}}}{8}-\gamma_{\mathrm{wk}}\right)\left(1-\kappa_{\mathrm{wk}}\right)\right]g_{i}^{(\tau)}\lVert{\bm{\nu}}\rVert^{2}

\displaystyle=\eta g_{i}^{(\tau)}\left(\frac{2p_{\mathrm{h}}+p_{\mathrm{b}}}{2}\cdot\kappa_{\mathrm{wk}}+4\gamma_{\mathrm{wk}}\right)\lVert{\bm{\nu}}\rVert^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{\eta(2p_{\mathrm{h}}+p_{\mathrm{b}})g_{i}^{(\tau)}\left\|{\bm{\nu}}\right\|^{2}}{2}

\displaystyle=\frac{(2p_{\mathrm{h}}+p_{\mathrm{b}})n_{\mathrm{wk}}\lVert{\bm{\nu}}\rVert^{2}}{2\lVert{\bm{\xi}}_{i}\rVert^{2}}\left(\rho_{i}^{(\tau+1)}-\rho_{i}^{(\tau)}\right),

where the inequalities follow from
(W5)
at iteration
\tau
, (
3
), and (
4
) , respectively. Hence, we obtain

\displaystyle N_{s}^{(\tau+1)}

\displaystyle\leq N_{s}^{(\tau)}+\frac{(2p_{\mathrm{h}}+p_{\mathrm{b}})n_{\mathrm{wk}}\lVert{\bm{\nu}}\rVert^{2}}{2\lVert{\bm{\xi}}_{i}\rVert^{2}}\left(\rho_{i}^{(\tau+1)}-\rho_{i}^{(\tau)}\right)

\displaystyle\leq N_{s}^{(\tau)}+(2p_{\mathrm{h}}+p_{\mathrm{b}})n_{\mathrm{wk}}\mathrm{SNR}_{\bm{\nu}}^{2}\cdot\left(\rho_{i}^{(\tau+1)}-\rho_{i}^{(\tau)}\right)

\displaystyle\leq(2p_{\mathrm{h}}+p_{\mathrm{b}})n_{\mathrm{wk}}\mathrm{SNR}_{\bm{\nu}}^{2}\cdot\rho_{i}^{(\tau)}+(2p_{\mathrm{h}}+p_{\mathrm{b}})n_{\mathrm{wk}}\mathrm{SNR}_{\bm{\nu}}^{2}\cdot\left(\rho_{i}^{(\tau+1)}-\rho_{i}^{(\tau)}\right)

\displaystyle=(2p_{\mathrm{h}}+p_{\mathrm{b}})n_{\mathrm{wk}}\mathrm{SNR}_{\bm{\nu}}^{2}\cdot\rho_{i}^{(\tau+1)},

where the second and last inequalities follow from (
5
) and
(W6)
at iteration
\tau
, respectively.
Similarly, we have

\displaystyle\quad N_{s}^{(\tau+1)}-N_{s}^{(\tau)}

\displaystyle=\frac{\eta}{n_{\mathrm{wk}}}\left(\sum_{j\in{\mathcal{S}}_{{\bm{\nu}}_{s}}^{(1)}}g_{j}^{(\tau)}+\sum_{j\in{\mathcal{S}}_{{\bm{\nu}}_{s}}^{(2)}}g_{j}^{(\tau)}-\sum_{j\in{\mathcal{S}}_{-{\bm{\nu}}_{s}}^{(1)}}g_{j}^{(\tau)}-\sum_{j\in{\mathcal{S}}_{-{\bm{\nu}}_{s}}^{(2)}}g_{j}^{(\tau)}\right)\lVert{\bm{\nu}}\rVert^{2}

\displaystyle\geq\frac{\eta}{n_{\mathrm{wk}}}\left[\left(\left|{\mathcal{S}}_{{\bm{\nu}}_{s}}^{(1)}\right|+\left|{\mathcal{S}}_{{\bm{\nu}}_{s}}^{(2)}\right|\right)\left(1-\kappa_{\mathrm{wk}}\right)-\left(\left|{\mathcal{S}}_{-{\bm{\nu}}_{s}}^{(1)}\right|+\left|{\mathcal{S}}_{-{\bm{\nu}}_{s}}^{(2)}\right|\right)\left(1+\kappa_{\mathrm{wk}}\right)\right]g_{i}^{(\tau)}\lVert{\bm{\nu}}\rVert^{2}

\displaystyle\geq\eta\left[2\left(\frac{p_{\mathrm{h}}}{4}+\frac{p_{\mathrm{b}}}{8}+\gamma_{\mathrm{wk}}\right)\left(1-\kappa_{\mathrm{wk}}\right)-2\left(\frac{p_{\mathrm{h}}}{4}+\frac{p_{\mathrm{b}}}{8}-\gamma_{\mathrm{wk}}\right)\left(1+\kappa_{\mathrm{wk}}\right)\right]g_{i}^{(\tau)}\lVert{\bm{\nu}}\rVert^{2}

\displaystyle=-\eta g_{i}^{(\tau)}\left(\frac{2p_{\mathrm{h}}+p_{\mathrm{b}}}{2}\cdot\kappa_{\mathrm{wk}}+4\gamma_{\mathrm{wk}}\right)\lVert{\bm{\nu}}\rVert^{2}

\displaystyle\geq-\frac{\eta(2p_{\mathrm{h}}+p_{\mathrm{b}})g_{i}^{(\tau)}\lVert{\bm{\nu}}\rVert^{2}}{2}

\displaystyle=-\frac{(2p_{\mathrm{h}}+p_{\mathrm{b}})n_{\mathrm{wk}}\lVert{\bm{\nu}}\rVert^{2}}{2\lVert{\bm{\xi}}_{i}\rVert^{2}}\left(\rho_{i}^{(\tau+1)}-\rho_{i}^{(\tau)}\right),

where the inequalities follow from
(W5)
at iteration
\tau
, (
5
), and (
3
), respectively. Hence, we obtain

\displaystyle N_{s}^{(\tau+1)}

\displaystyle\geq N_{s}^{(\tau)}-\frac{(2p_{\mathrm{h}}+p_{\mathrm{b}})n_{\mathrm{wk}}\lVert{\bm{\nu}}\rVert^{2}}{2\lVert{\bm{\xi}}_{i}\rVert^{2}}\left(\rho_{i}^{(\tau+1)}-\rho_{i}^{(\tau)}\right)

\displaystyle\geq N_{s}^{(\tau)}-(2p_{\mathrm{h}}+p_{\mathrm{b}})n_{\mathrm{wk}}\mathrm{SNR}_{\bm{\nu}}^{2}\cdot\left(\rho_{i}^{(\tau+1)}-\rho_{i}^{(\tau)}\right)

\displaystyle\geq-(2p_{\mathrm{h}}+p_{\mathrm{b}})n_{\mathrm{wk}}\mathrm{SNR}_{\bm{\nu}}^{2}\cdot\rho_{i}^{(\tau)}-(2p_{\mathrm{h}}+p_{\mathrm{b}})n_{\mathrm{wk}}\mathrm{SNR}_{\bm{\nu}}^{2}\cdot\left(\rho_{i}^{(\tau+1)}-\rho_{i}^{(\tau)}\right)

\displaystyle=-(2p_{\mathrm{h}}+p_{\mathrm{b}})n_{\mathrm{wk}}\mathrm{SNR}_{\bm{\nu}}^{2}\cdot\rho_{i}^{(\tau+1)},

where the second and last inequalities follow from (
4
) and
(W6)
at iteration
\tau
, respectively.

Therefore, the conclusions hold at any iteration
t\in[0,T^{*}]
.
∎

B.2
Convergence of Training Loss

In this subsection, we prove that the training loss converges below
\varepsilon
within
\widetilde{\mathcal{O}}\left(\eta^{-1}\varepsilon^{-1}n_{\mathrm{wk}}d^{-1}\sigma_{p}^{-2}\right)
. All the arguments in this subsection are under Condition
3.2
and the event
E_{\mathrm{wk}}
.

Let us define

\hat{\bm{w}}:=2\log(4/\varepsilon)\sum_{i\in[n_{\mathrm{wk}}]}y_{i}{\bm{\xi}}_{i}\left\|{\bm{\xi}}_{i}\right\|^{-2},

which plays a crucial role in proving convergence.

Lemma B.2
.

Under Condition
3.2
and the event
E_{\mathrm{wk}}
, we have the following:

•

\left\|\hat{\bm{w}}\right\|\leq 3\log(4/\varepsilon)n_{\mathrm{wk}}^{\frac{1}{2}}d^{-\frac{1}{2}}\sigma_{p}^{-1}
.

•

y_{i}\left\langle\nabla_{\bm{w}}f_{\mathrm{wk}}\left({\bm{w}}^{(t)},{\bm{X}}_{i}\right),\hat{\bm{w}}\right\rangle\geq\log(4/\varepsilon)
for any
t\in[T,T^{*}]
.

•

\left\|\nabla_{\bm{w}}L_{\mathrm{wk}}\left({\bm{w}}^{(t)}\right)\right\|^{2}\leq 2\sigma_{p}^{2}d\cdot L_{\mathrm{wk}}\left({\bm{w}}^{(t)}\right)
for any
t\in[0,T^{*}]
.

Proof of Lemma
B.2
.

The first statement follows from

\displaystyle\left\|\hat{\bm{w}}\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle=\left(2\log(4/\varepsilon)\right)^{2}\left(\sum_{i\in[n_{\mathrm{wk}}]}y_{i}{\bm{\xi}}_{i}\left\|{\bm{\xi}}_{i}\right\|^{-2}\right)^{2}

\displaystyle=4\log^{2}(4/\varepsilon)\left(\sum_{i\in[n_{\mathrm{wk}}]}\left\|{\bm{\xi}}_{i}\right\|^{-2}+\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}i,j\in[n_{\mathrm{wk}}]\\
i\neq j\end{subarray}}y_{i}y_{j}\frac{\left\langle{\bm{\xi}}_{i},{\bm{\xi}}_{j}\right\rangle}{\left\|{\bm{\xi}}_{i}\right\|^{2}\left\|{\bm{\xi}}_{j}\right\|^{2}}\right)

\displaystyle\leq 4\log^{2}(4/\varepsilon)\left(\sum_{i\in[n_{\mathrm{wk}}]}\left\|{\bm{\xi}}_{i}\right\|^{-2}+\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}i,j\in[n_{\mathrm{wk}}]\\
i\neq j\end{subarray}}\frac{\left|\left\langle{\bm{\xi}}_{i},{\bm{\xi}}_{j}\right\rangle\right|}{\left\|{\bm{\xi}}_{i}\right\|^{2}\left\|{\bm{\xi}}_{j}\right\|^{2}}\right)

\displaystyle\leq 4\log^{2}(4/\varepsilon)\left(n_{\mathrm{wk}}\cdot\frac{2}{\sigma_{p}^{2}d}+n_{\mathrm{wk}}^{2}\cdot\frac{\beta_{\mathrm{wk}}}{n_{\mathrm{wk}}}\cdot\frac{2}{\sigma_{p}^{2}d}\right)

\displaystyle=4\log^{2}(4/\varepsilon)\frac{2n_{\mathrm{wk}}(1+\beta_{\mathrm{wk}})}{\sigma_{p}^{2}d}

\displaystyle\leq 9\log^{2}(4/\varepsilon)\frac{n_{\mathrm{wk}}}{\sigma_{p}^{2}d},

where the second inequality follows from (
4
) and the last inequality follows from (
3
).

Next, let us prove the second statement. For any
t\in[0,T^{*}]
, we have

\displaystyle\quad y_{i}\left\langle\nabla_{\bm{w}}f_{\mathrm{wk}}\left({\bm{w}}^{(t)},{\bm{X}}_{i}\right),\hat{\bm{w}}\right\rangle

\displaystyle=y_{i}\left\langle{\bm{v}}_{i}^{(1)}+{\bm{v}}_{i}^{(2)}+{\bm{\xi}}_{i},2\log(4/\varepsilon)\sum_{j\in[n_{\mathrm{wk}}]}y_{j}{\bm{\xi}}_{j}\left\|{\bm{\xi}}_{j}\right\|^{-2}\right\rangle

\displaystyle=2\log(4/\varepsilon)\sum_{j\in[n_{\mathrm{wk}}]}y_{i}y_{j}\frac{\left\langle{\bm{\xi}}_{i},{\bm{\xi}}_{j}\right\rangle}{\left\|{\bm{\xi}}_{j}\right\|^{2}}

\displaystyle\geq 2\log(4/\varepsilon)-\sum_{j\in[n_{\mathrm{wk}}]\backslash\{i\}}2\log(4/\varepsilon)\frac{\left|\left\langle{\bm{\xi}}_{i},{\bm{\xi}}_{j}\right\rangle\right|}{\left\|{\bm{\xi}}_{j}\right\|^{2}}

\displaystyle\geq 2(1-\beta_{\mathrm{wk}})\log(4/\varepsilon)

\displaystyle\geq\log(4/\varepsilon)

where the second inequality follows from (
4
) and the last inequality follows from (
3
).

Let us prove the last statement. For any
t\in[0,T^{*}]
, we have

\displaystyle\left\|\nabla_{\bm{w}}L_{\mathrm{wk}}\left({\bm{w}}^{(t)}\right)\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle=\left\|\frac{1}{n_{\mathrm{wk}}}\sum_{i\in[n_{\mathrm{wk}}]}g_{i}^{(t)}y_{i}\left({\bm{v}}_{i}^{(1)}+{\bm{v}}_{i}^{(2)}+{\bm{\xi}}_{i}\right)\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\left[\frac{1}{n_{\mathrm{wk}}}\sum_{i\in[n_{\mathrm{wk}}]}g_{i}^{(t)}\left\|{\bm{v}}_{i}^{(1)}+{\bm{v}}_{i}^{(2)}+{\bm{\xi}}_{i}\right\|\right]^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\left[\frac{1}{n_{\mathrm{wk}}}\sum_{i\in[n_{\mathrm{wk}}]}g_{i}^{(t)}\right]^{2}2\sigma_{p}^{2}d

\displaystyle\leq 2\sigma_{p}^{2}d\cdot\left[\frac{1}{n_{\mathrm{wk}}}\sum_{i\in[n_{\mathrm{wk}}]}g_{i}^{(t)}\right]

\displaystyle\leq 2\sigma_{p}^{2}d\cdot\left[\frac{1}{n_{\mathrm{wk}}}\sum_{i\in[n_{\mathrm{wk}}]}\ell\left(y_{i}f_{\mathrm{wk}}\left({\bm{w}},{\bm{X}}_{i}\right)\right)\right]

\displaystyle=2\sigma_{p}^{2}d\cdot L_{\mathrm{wk}}\left({\bm{w}}^{(t)}\right),

where the first inequality follows from the triangle inequality, the second follows from (
4
) and the bound
\left\|{\bm{\mu}}\right\|^{2},\left\|{\bm{\nu}}\right\|^{2}\leq\frac{\sigma_{p}^{2}d}{4}
implied by Condition
3.2
, the third follows from
\frac{1}{n_{\mathrm{wk}}}\sum_{i\in[n_{\mathrm{wk}}]}g_{i}^{(t)}\leq 1
, and the last follows from
-\ell^{\prime}(z)\leq\ell(z)
for all
z\in\mathbb{R}
.
∎

Lemma B.3
.

Under Condition
3.1
and the event
E_{\mathrm{wk}}
, for any iteration
T\in[0,T^{*}]
, we have

\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=0}^{T}L_{\mathrm{wk}}\left({\bm{w}}^{(t)}\right)\leq\frac{\left\|\hat{\bm{w}}\right\|^{2}}{\eta T}+\frac{\varepsilon}{2}.

Proof of Lemma
B.3
.

For any
t\in[0,T^{*}]
, we have

\displaystyle\quad\left\|{\bm{w}}^{(t)}-\hat{\bm{w}}\right\|^{2}-\left\|{\bm{w}}^{(t+1)}-\hat{\bm{w}}\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle=\left\|{\bm{w}}^{(t)}-\hat{\bm{w}}\right\|^{2}-\left\|{\bm{w}}^{(t)}-\hat{\bm{w}}-\eta\nabla L_{\mathrm{wk}}\left({\bm{w}}^{(t)}\right)\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle=2\eta\left\langle\nabla L_{\mathrm{wk}}\left({\bm{w}}^{(t)}\right),{\bm{w}}^{(t)}-\hat{\bm{w}}\right\rangle-\eta^{2}\left\|\nabla L_{\mathrm{wk}}\left({\bm{w}}^{(t)}\right)\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle=\frac{2\eta}{n_{\mathrm{wk}}}\sum_{i\in[n_{\mathrm{wk}}]}g_{i}^{(t)}\left(\left\langle y_{i}\nabla f_{\mathrm{wk}}\left({\bm{w}}^{(t)},{\bm{X}}_{i}\right),\hat{\bm{w}}\right\rangle-y_{i}f_{\mathrm{wk}}\left({\bm{w}}^{(t)},{\bm{X}}_{i}\right)\right)-\eta^{2}\left\|\nabla L_{\mathrm{wk}}\left({\bm{w}}^{(t)}\right)\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle\geq\frac{2\eta}{n_{\mathrm{wk}}}\sum_{i\in[n_{\mathrm{wk}}]}g_{i}^{(t)}\left(\log(4/\varepsilon)-y_{i}f_{\mathrm{wk}}\left({\bm{w}}^{(t)},{\bm{X}}_{i}\right)\right)-\eta^{2}\left\|\nabla L_{\mathrm{wk}}\left({\bm{w}}^{(t)}\right)\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle\geq\frac{2\eta}{n_{\mathrm{wk}}}\sum_{i\in[n_{\mathrm{wk}}]}\left[\ell\bigg(y_{i}\ f\left({\bm{w}}^{(t)},{\bm{X}}_{i}\right)\bigg)-\frac{\varepsilon}{4}\right]-\eta^{2}\left\|\nabla L_{\mathrm{wk}}\left({\bm{w}}^{(t)}\right)\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle\geq\eta L_{\mathrm{wk}}\left({\bm{w}}^{(t)}\right)-\frac{\eta\varepsilon}{2},

where the first inequality follows from Lemma
B.2
, the second follows from the convexity of
\ell
and the bound
\ell(\log(4/\varepsilon))\geq\varepsilon/4
, and the last follows from Lemma
B.2
and
(C4)
.

By applying a telescoping sum and using the fact that
{\bm{w}}^{(0)}=0
, we obtain the desired conclusion.
∎

Using lemmas above, we can prove that the training loss converges to below
\varepsilon
.
By applying Lemma
B.3
with iteration
\tilde{T}=\lceil 18\eta^{-1}\varepsilon^{-1}\log(4/\varepsilon)n_{\mathrm{wk}}d^{-1}\sigma_{p}^{-2}\rceil=\widetilde{\mathcal{O}}(\eta^{-1}\varepsilon^{-1}n_{\mathrm{wk}}d^{-1}\sigma_{p}^{-2})
and using Lemma
B.2
, we obtain

\frac{1}{\tilde{T}}\sum_{t=0}^{\tilde{T}}L_{\mathrm{wk}}\left({\bm{w}}^{(t)}\right)\leq\frac{\left\|\hat{\bm{w}}\right\|^{2}}{\eta\tilde{T}}+\frac{\varepsilon}{2}\leq\frac{9\log^{2}(4/\varepsilon)n_{\mathrm{wk}}d^{-1}\sigma_{p}^{-2}}{\eta\tilde{T}}+\frac{\varepsilon}{2}\leq\varepsilon.

Therefore, there exists
T_{\mathrm{wk}}\in[0,\tilde{T}]
such that
L_{\mathrm{wk}}({\bm{w}}^{(T_{\mathrm{wk}})})\leq\varepsilon
. In addition, for any
{\bm{w}}_{1},{\bm{w}}_{2}\in\mathbb{R}^{d}
, we have

\displaystyle\quad\left\|\nabla_{\bm{w}}L_{\mathrm{wk}}({\bm{w}}_{1})-\nabla_{\bm{w}}L_{\mathrm{wk}}({\bm{w}}_{2})\right\|

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{n_{\mathrm{wk}}}\left\|\sum_{i\in[n_{\mathrm{wk}}]}\left[y_{i}(\ell^{\prime}\big(y_{i}f_{\mathrm{wk}}({\bm{w}}_{1},{\bm{X}}_{i})\big)-\ell^{\prime}\big(y_{i}f_{\mathrm{wk}}({\bm{w}}_{2},{\bm{X}}_{i})\big)\left({\bm{v}}_{i}^{(1)}+{\bm{v}}_{i}^{(2)}+{\bm{\xi}}_{i}\right)\right]\right\|

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{n_{\mathrm{wk}}}\sum_{i\in[n_{\mathrm{wk}}]}\left[\left|\ell^{\prime}\big(y_{i}f_{\mathrm{wk}}({\bm{w}}_{1},{\bm{X}}_{i})\big)-\ell^{\prime}\big(y_{i}f_{\mathrm{wk}}({\bm{w}}_{2},{\bm{X}}_{i})\big)\right|\cdot\left\|{\bm{v}}_{i}^{(1)}+{\bm{v}}_{i}^{(2)}+{\bm{\xi}}_{i}\right\|\right]

\displaystyle\leq\frac{\sqrt{2}\sigma_{p}d^{\frac{1}{2}}}{2n_{\mathrm{wk}}}\sum_{i\in[n_{\mathrm{wk}}]}\left|\big(f_{\mathrm{wk}}({\bm{w}}_{1},{\bm{X}}_{i})-f_{\mathrm{wk}}({\bm{w}}_{2},{\bm{X}}_{i})\big)\right|

\displaystyle\leq\frac{\sqrt{2}\sigma_{p}d^{\frac{1}{2}}}{2n_{\mathrm{wk}}}\sum_{i\in[n_{\mathrm{wk}}]}\left\|{\bm{v}}_{i}^{(1)}+{\bm{v}}_{i}^{(2)}+{\bm{\xi}}_{i}\right\|\cdot\lVert{\bm{w}}_{1}-{\bm{w}}_{2}\rVert

\displaystyle\leq\sigma_{p}^{2}d\lVert{\bm{w}}_{1}-{\bm{w}}_{2}\rVert,

where the first and third inequalities follow from the Cauchy-Schwarz inequality, the second and last inequalities follow from (
4
) and the bound
\left\|{\bm{\mu}}\right\|^{2},\left\|{\bm{\nu}}\right\|^{2}\leq\frac{\sigma_{p}^{2}d}{4}
implied by Condition
3.2
, and for the second inequality, we also use the fact that
0\leq\ell^{\prime}\leq\frac{1}{4}
.

Since
L_{\mathrm{wk}}({\bm{w}})
is
\sigma_{p}^{2}d
-smooth and the learning rate satisfies (
10
), we can apply the descent lemma (Lemma 3.4 in
Bubeck 2015
). This proves the first part of our conclusion.

\square

B.3
Test Error

In this subsection, we prove the second part of our conclusion. All the arguments in this subsection are under Condition
3.2
and the event
E_{\mathrm{wk}}
.

Define
{\bm{v}}^{(1)}
,
{\bm{v}}^{(2)}
, and
{\bm{\xi}}
as the signal vectors and the noise vector in the test data
({\bm{X}},y)
, respectively.

For any iteration
t\in[T_{\mathrm{wk}},T^{*}]
and for the case given
({\bm{X}},y)\in{\mathcal{S}}_{\mathrm{e}}\cup{\mathcal{S}}_{\mathrm{b}}
, we can express the test accuracy as

\displaystyle\quad\mathbb{P}\left[yf_{\mathrm{wk}}\left({\bm{w}}^{(t)},{\bm{X}}\right)<0\,\middle|\,({\bm{X}},y)\in{\mathcal{S}}_{\mathrm{e}}\cup{\mathcal{S}}_{\mathrm{b}}\right]

\displaystyle=\mathbb{P}\left[\left\langle y{\bm{w}}^{(t)},{\bm{\xi}}\right\rangle<-\left\langle y{\bm{w}}^{(t)},{\bm{v}}^{(1)}\right\rangle-\left\langle y{\bm{w}}^{(t)},{\bm{v}}^{(2)}\right\rangle\,\middle|\,({\bm{X}},y)\in{\mathcal{S}}_{\mathrm{e}}\cup{\mathcal{S}}_{\mathrm{b}}\right]

\displaystyle\leq\mathbb{P}\left[\left\langle y{\bm{w}}^{(t)},{\bm{\xi}}\right\rangle<-\frac{M_{y}^{(t)}}{2}\right]

\displaystyle=\mathbb{P}\left[{\mathbf{z}}<-\frac{M_{y}^{(t)}}{2}\right],

where
{\mathbf{z}}\sim{\mathcal{N}}\left(0,\sigma_{p}^{2}\left\|\Pi_{S}{\bm{w}}^{(t)}\right\|^{2}\right)
, and the inequality follows from (
11
). By Höeffding’s inequality, we have

\mathbb{P}\left[yf_{\mathrm{wk}}\left({\bm{w}}^{(t)},{\bm{X}}\right)<0\,\middle|\,({\bm{X}},y)\in{\mathcal{S}}_{\mathrm{e}}\cup{\mathcal{S}}_{\mathrm{b}}\right]\leq\exp\left(-\frac{\left(M_{y}^{(t)}\right)^{2}}{8\sigma_{p}^{2}\left\|\Pi_{S}{\bm{w}}^{(t)}\right\|^{2}}\right).

Let us characterize
\left\|\Pi_{S}{\bm{w}}^{(t)}\right\|^{2}
. We have

\displaystyle\left\|\Pi_{S}{\bm{w}}^{(t)}\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle=\left\|\sum_{i\in[n_{\mathrm{wk}}]}y_{i}\rho_{i}^{(t)}{\bm{\xi}}_{i}\left\|{\bm{\xi}}_{i}\right\|^{-2}\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{i\in[n_{\mathrm{wk}}]}\left(\rho_{i}^{(t)}\right)^{2}\left\|{\bm{\xi}}_{i}\right\|^{-2}+\sum_{i\in[n_{\mathrm{wk}}]}\sum_{j\in[n_{\mathrm{wk}}]\setminus\{i\}}\rho_{i}^{(t)}\rho_{j}^{(t)}\frac{\left|\left\langle{\bm{\xi}}_{i},{\bm{\xi}}_{j}\right\rangle\right|}{\left\|{\bm{\xi}}_{i}\right\|^{2}\left\|{\bm{\xi}}_{j}\right\|^{2}}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{2}{\sigma_{p}^{2}d}\sum_{i\in[n_{\mathrm{wk}}]}\left(\rho_{i}^{(t)}\right)^{2}+\sum_{i\in[n_{\mathrm{wk}}]}\sum_{j\in[n_{\mathrm{wk}}]}\frac{\left(\rho_{i}^{(t)}\right)^{2}+\left(\rho_{j}^{(t)}\right)^{2}}{2}\cdot\frac{\beta_{\mathrm{wk}}}{n_{\mathrm{wk}}}\cdot\frac{2}{\sigma_{p}^{2}d}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{4}{\sigma_{p}^{2}d}\sum_{i\in[n_{\mathrm{wk}}]}\left(\rho_{i}^{(t)}\right)^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{4}{\sigma_{p}^{2}d}n_{\mathrm{wk}}\left(\frac{12}{n_{\mathrm{wk}}(2p_{\mathrm{e}}+p_{\mathrm{b}})\cdot\mathrm{SNR}_{\bm{\mu}}^{2}}\right)^{2}\left(M_{y}^{(t)}\right)^{2}

\displaystyle=\frac{576\sigma_{p}^{2}d}{n_{\mathrm{wk}}(2p_{\mathrm{e}}+p_{\mathrm{b}})^{2}\left\|{\bm{\mu}}\right\|^{4}}\left(M_{y}^{(t)}\right)^{2},

where the first and second inequality follows from AM-GM inequality and (
4
), the third inequality follows from (
3
), and the last inequality follows from
(W2)
. Hence, we have

\mathbb{P}\left[yf_{\mathrm{wk}}\left({\bm{w}}^{(t)},{\bm{X}}\right)<0\,\middle|\,({\bm{X}},y)\in{\mathcal{S}}_{\mathrm{e}}\cup{\mathcal{S}}_{\mathrm{b}}\right]\leq\exp\left(-\frac{n_{\mathrm{wk}}(2p_{\mathrm{e}}+p_{\mathrm{b}})^{2}\left\|{\bm{\mu}}\right\|^{4}}{4608\sigma_{p}^{4}d}\right).

Appendix C
Proof of Theorem
3.4

It suffices to prove the following restatement of Theorem
3.4
.

Theorem C.1

(Weak-to-Strong Training, Data-Scarce Regime)
.

Let
{\bm{W}}^{(t)}
be the iterates of weak-to-strong training,
with the weak model
f_{\mathrm{wk}}({\bm{w}}^{*},\cdot)
satisfying the conclusion of Theorem
3.3
.
For any
\varepsilon>0
and
\delta\in(0,1)
satisfying Condition
3.2
,
with probability at least
1-\delta
, there exists
T_{\mathrm{w2s}}=\mathcal{O}(\eta^{-1}\varepsilon^{-1}mn_{\mathrm{st}}d^{-1}\sigma_{p}^{-2})
such that for any
t\in[T_{\mathrm{w2s}},T^{*}]
the following statements hold:

1.

The training loss converges below
\varepsilon
:
L_{\mathrm{st}}\left({\bm{W}}^{(t)}\right)<\varepsilon
.

2.

Let
({\bm{X}},y)\sim{\mathcal{D}}
be an unseen test example, independent of the training set
\{(\tilde{\bm{X}}_{i},\hat{y}_{i})\}_{i=1}^{n_{\mathrm{st}}}
.

•

(Benign Overfitting) When
n_{\mathrm{st}}p_{\mathrm{b}}^{2}\left\|{\bm{\nu}}\right\|^{4}/(\sigma_{p}^{4}d)\geq C_{2}
, we have

\mathbb{P}\left[yf_{\mathrm{st}}\left({\bm{W}}^{(t)},{\bm{X}}\right)<0\,\middle|\,({\bm{X}},y)\in{\mathcal{S}}_{\mathrm{e}}\cup{\mathcal{S}}_{\mathrm{b}}\right]\leq\exp\left(-\frac{n_{\mathrm{st}}(2p_{\mathrm{e}}+p_{\mathrm{b}})^{2}\lVert{\bm{\mu}}\rVert^{4}}{C_{3}\sigma_{p}^{4}d}\right),

and

\mathbb{P}\left[yf_{\mathrm{st}}\left({\bm{W}}^{(t)},{\bm{X}}\right)<0\,\middle|\,({\bm{X}},y)\in{\mathcal{S}}_{\mathrm{h}}\right]\leq\exp\left(-\frac{n_{\mathrm{st}}p_{\mathrm{b}}^{2}\lVert{\bm{\nu}}\rVert^{4}}{C_{3}\sigma_{p}^{4}d}\right).

•

(Harmful Overfitting) When
n_{\mathrm{st}}p_{\mathrm{b}}^{2}\left\|{\bm{\nu}}\right\|^{4}/(\sigma_{p}^{4}d)\leq C_{4}
,

\mathbb{P}\left[yf_{\mathrm{st}}\left({\bm{W}}^{(t)},{\bm{X}}\right)<0\right]\geq 0.12p_{\mathrm{h}}.

Here,
C_{2},C_{3},C_{4}>0
are constants.

For the proof, we first introduce properties preserved during training (Appendix
C.1
), then prove the convergence of the training loss (Appendix
C.2
), and finally establish a bound on the test error (Appendix
C.3
).

C.1
Preserved Properties during Training

In this subsection, we present several properties that remain preserved throughout training.

Lemma C.2
.

Suppose for some iteration
t\in[0,T^{*}]
, it satisfies
\left|\underline{M}_{s,r}^{(t)}\right|,\left|\underline{N}_{s,r}^{(t)}\right|\leq\alpha_{\mathrm{st}}+\beta_{\mathrm{st}}
,
0\leq\overline{\rho}_{r,i}^{(t)}\leq 4\log T^{*}
, and
-\alpha_{\mathrm{st}}-5\beta_{\mathrm{st}}\log T^{*}\leq\underline{\rho}_{r,i}^{(t)}\leq 0
for any
s\in\{\pm 1\},r\in[m]
, and
i\in[n_{\mathrm{st}}]
. Then, for any
i\in[n_{\mathrm{st}}]
it holds that

F_{-\hat{y}_{i}}\left({\bm{W}}_{-\hat{y}_{i}}^{(t)},\tilde{\bm{X}}_{i}\right)\leq\frac{\kappa_{\mathrm{st}}}{16},\quad\left|\sigma\left(\left\langle{\bm{w}}_{\hat{y}_{i},r}^{(t)},\tilde{\bm{\xi}}_{i}\right\rangle\right)-\overline{\rho}_{r,i}^{(t)}\right|\leq\frac{\kappa_{\mathrm{st}}}{16}.

Proof of Lemma
C.2
.

For any
i\in[n_{\mathrm{st}}]
, we have

\displaystyle\quad F_{-\hat{y}_{i}}\left({\bm{W}}_{-\hat{y}_{i}}^{(t)},\tilde{\bm{X}}_{i}\right)

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{m}\sum_{r\in[m]}\left[\sigma\left(\left\langle{\bm{w}}_{-\hat{y}_{i},r}^{(t)},\tilde{\bm{v}}_{i}^{(1)}\right\rangle\right)+\sigma\left(\left\langle{\bm{w}}_{-\hat{y}_{i},r}^{(t)},\tilde{\bm{v}}_{i}^{(2)}\right\rangle\right)+\sigma\left(\left\langle{\bm{w}}_{-\hat{y}_{i},r}^{(t)},\tilde{\bm{\xi}}_{i}\right\rangle\right)\right]

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{m}\sum_{r\in[m]}\left[\left|\left\langle{\bm{w}}_{-\hat{y}_{i},r}^{(t)},\tilde{\bm{v}}_{i}^{(1)}\right\rangle\right|+\left|\left\langle{\bm{w}}_{-\hat{y}_{i},r}^{(t)},\tilde{\bm{v}}_{i}^{(2)}\right\rangle\right|+\left|\left\langle{\bm{w}}_{-\hat{y}_{i},r}^{(t)},\tilde{\bm{\xi}}_{i}\right\rangle\right|\right]

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{m}\sum_{r\in[m]}\left[\left|\left\langle{\bm{w}}_{-\hat{y}_{i},r}^{(0)},\tilde{\bm{v}}_{i}^{(1)}\right\rangle\right|+\left|\left\langle{\bm{w}}_{-\hat{y}_{i},r}^{(0)},\tilde{\bm{v}}_{i}^{(2)}\right\rangle\right|+2\cdot(\alpha_{\mathrm{st}}+\beta_{\mathrm{st}})+\left|\left\langle{\bm{w}}_{-\hat{y}_{i},r}^{(t)},\tilde{\bm{\xi}}_{i}\right\rangle\right|\right]

\displaystyle\leq(4\alpha_{\mathrm{st}}+2\beta_{\mathrm{st}})+\frac{1}{m}\sum_{r\in[m]}\left|\left\langle{\bm{w}}_{-\hat{y}_{i},r}^{(t)},\tilde{\bm{\xi}}_{i}\right\rangle\right|,

where the last two inequalities follow from the given bounds on
\left|\underline{M}_{s,r}^{(t)}\right|,\left|\underline{N}_{s,r}^{(t)}\right|
and (
7
). In addition, for any
r\in[m]
, we have

\displaystyle\left\langle{\bm{w}}_{-\hat{y}_{i},r}^{(t)},\tilde{\bm{\xi}}_{i}\right\rangle

\displaystyle=\left\langle{\bm{w}}_{-\hat{y}_{i},r}^{(0)},\tilde{\bm{\xi}}_{i}\right\rangle+\underline{\rho}_{r,i}^{(t)}+\sum_{j\in[n_{\mathrm{st}}]\setminus\{i\}}\rho_{-\hat{y}_{i},r,j}^{(t)}\frac{\langle\tilde{\bm{\xi}}_{i},\tilde{\bm{\xi}}_{j}\rangle}{\lVert\tilde{\bm{\xi}}_{j}\rVert^{2}}

\displaystyle\geq\left\langle{\bm{w}}_{-\hat{y}_{i},r}^{(0)},\tilde{\bm{\xi}}_{i}\right\rangle+\underline{\rho}_{r,i}^{(t)}-\sum_{j\in[n_{\mathrm{st}}]\setminus\{i\}}\left|\rho_{-\hat{y}_{i},r,j}^{(t)}\right|\frac{|\langle\tilde{\bm{\xi}}_{i},\tilde{\bm{\xi}}_{j}\rangle|}{\lVert\tilde{\bm{\xi}}_{j}\rVert^{2}}

\displaystyle\geq-2\alpha_{\mathrm{st}}-9\beta_{\mathrm{st}}\log T^{*},

where the last inequality follows from the given bound on
\overline{\rho}_{r,i}^{(t)},\underline{\rho}_{r,i}^{(t)}
, (
7
), and (
8
). Similarly, for any
r\in[m]
, we have

\displaystyle\left\langle{\bm{w}}_{-\hat{y}_{i},r}^{(t)},\tilde{\bm{\xi}}_{i}\right\rangle

\displaystyle=\left\langle{\bm{w}}_{-\hat{y}_{i},r}^{(0)},\tilde{\bm{\xi}}_{i}\right\rangle+\underline{\rho}_{r,i}^{(t)}+\sum_{j\in[n_{\mathrm{st}}]\setminus\{i\}}\rho_{-\hat{y}_{i},r,j}^{(t)}\frac{\langle\tilde{\bm{\xi}}_{i},\tilde{\bm{\xi}}_{j}\rangle}{\lVert\tilde{\bm{\xi}}_{j}\rVert^{2}}

\displaystyle\leq\left\langle{\bm{w}}_{-\hat{y}_{i},r}^{(0)},\tilde{\bm{\xi}}_{i}\right\rangle+\underline{\rho}_{r,i}^{(t)}+\sum_{j\in[n_{\mathrm{st}}]\setminus\{i\}}\left|\rho_{-\hat{y}_{i},r,j}^{(t)}\right|\frac{|\langle\tilde{\bm{\xi}}_{i},\tilde{\bm{\xi}}_{j}\rangle|}{\lVert\tilde{\bm{\xi}}_{j}\rVert^{2}}

\displaystyle\leq\alpha_{\mathrm{st}}+4\beta_{\mathrm{st}}\log T^{*},

where the last inequality follows from the given bound on
\overline{\rho}_{r,i}^{(t)},\underline{\rho}_{r,i}^{(t)}
, (
7
), and (
8
). Hence, we have

F_{-\hat{y}_{i}}\left({\bm{W}}_{-\hat{y}_{i}}^{(t)},\tilde{\bm{X}}_{i}\right)\leq 6\alpha_{\mathrm{st}}+2\beta_{\mathrm{st}}+9\beta_{\mathrm{st}}\log T^{*}\leq\frac{\kappa_{\mathrm{st}}}{16},

where the last inequality follows from (
6
).

Next, we prove the second part. For any
i\in[n_{\mathrm{st}}]
and
r\in[m]
, we have

\displaystyle\left|\sigma\left(\left\langle{\bm{w}}_{\hat{y}_{i},r}^{(t)},\tilde{\bm{\xi}}_{i}\right\rangle\right)-\overline{\rho}_{r,i}^{(t)}\right|

\displaystyle=\left|\sigma\left(\left\langle{\bm{w}}_{\hat{y}_{i},r}^{(t)},\tilde{\bm{\xi}}_{i}\right\rangle\right)-\sigma\left(\overline{\rho}_{r,i}^{(t)}\right)\right|

\displaystyle\leq\left|\left\langle{\bm{w}}_{\hat{y}_{i},r}^{(t)},\tilde{\bm{\xi}}_{i}\right\rangle-\overline{\rho}_{r,i}^{(t)}\right|

\displaystyle\leq\left\langle{\bm{w}}_{\hat{y}_{i},r}^{(0)},\tilde{\bm{\xi}}_{i}\right\rangle+\sum_{j\in[n_{\mathrm{st}}]\setminus\{i\}}\left|\rho_{\hat{y}_{i},r,j}^{(t)}\right|\frac{|\langle\tilde{\bm{\xi}}_{i},\tilde{\bm{\xi}}_{j}\rangle|}{\lVert\tilde{\bm{\xi}}_{i}\rVert^{2}}

\displaystyle\leq\alpha_{\mathrm{st}}+4\beta_{\mathrm{st}}\log T^{*}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{\kappa_{\mathrm{st}}}{16},

where the third inequality follows from the given bound on
\overline{\rho}_{r,i}^{(t)},\underline{\rho}_{r,i}^{(t)}
, (
7
), and (
8
).
∎

Lemma C.3
.

Under Condition
3.2
and the event
E_{\mathrm{st}}
, we have the following for any iteration
t\in[0,T^{*}]
:

(S1)

-\alpha_{\mathrm{st}}-5\beta_{\mathrm{st}}\log T^{*}\leq\underline{\rho}_{r,i}^{(t)}\leq 0
and
0\leq\overline{\rho}_{r,i}^{(t)}\leq 4\log T^{*}
for any
i\in[n_{\mathrm{st}}]
and
r\in[m]
.

(S2)

If
t\geq 1
, then for any
s\in\{\pm 1\}
, we have
\overline{M}_{s,r}^{(t)}\geq\overline{M}_{s,r}^{(t-1)}
for all
r\in[m]
,
\overline{N}_{s,r}^{(t)}\geq\overline{N}_{s,r}^{(t-1)}
for all
r\in{\mathcal{A}}_{s}
, and
\overline{N}_{s,r}^{(t)}\leq\overline{N}_{s,r}^{(t-1)}
for all
r\in{\mathcal{B}}_{s}
. In addition,
\left|\underline{M}_{s,r}^{(t)}\right|,\left|\underline{N}_{s,r}^{(t)}\right|\leq\alpha_{\mathrm{st}}+\beta_{\mathrm{st}}
for all
r\in[m]
.

(S3)

For any
s\in\{\pm 1\}
and
i\in[n_{\mathrm{st}}]
, we have

\displaystyle\frac{n_{\bm{\mu}}\mathrm{SNR}_{\bm{\mu}}^{2}}{12\lambda_{\mathrm{st}}}\cdot\sum_{r\in[m]}\overline{\rho}_{r,i}^{(t)}\leq

\displaystyle\sum_{r\in[m]}\overline{M}_{s,r}^{(t)}\leq 6\lambda_{\mathrm{st}}n_{\bm{\mu}}\mathrm{SNR}_{\bm{\mu}}^{2}\cdot\sum_{r\in[m]}\overline{\rho}_{r,i}^{(t)}

\displaystyle\frac{n_{\bm{\nu}}\mathrm{SNR}_{\bm{\nu}}^{2}}{12\lambda_{\mathrm{st}}}\cdot\sum_{r\in[m]}\overline{\rho}_{r,i}^{(t)}\leq

\displaystyle\sum_{r\in{\mathcal{A}}_{s}}\overline{N}_{s,r}^{(t)}\leq 6\lambda_{\mathrm{st}}n_{\bm{\nu}}\mathrm{SNR}_{\bm{\nu}}^{2}\cdot\sum_{r\in[m]}\overline{\rho}_{r,i}^{(t)}

\displaystyle\frac{n_{\bm{\nu}}\mathrm{SNR}_{\bm{\nu}}^{2}}{12\lambda_{\mathrm{st}}}\cdot\sum_{r\in[m]}\overline{\rho}_{r,i}^{(t)}\leq

\displaystyle-\sum_{r\in{\mathcal{B}}_{s}}\overline{N}_{s,r}^{(t)}\leq 6\lambda_{\mathrm{st}}n_{\bm{\nu}}\mathrm{SNR}_{\bm{\nu}}^{2}\cdot\sum_{r\in[m]}\overline{\rho}_{r,i}^{(t)}.

(S4)

\left|\hat{y}_{i}f_{\mathrm{st}}\left({\bm{W}}^{(t)},\tilde{\bm{X}}_{i}\right)-\frac{1}{m}\sum_{r\in[m]}\overline{\rho}_{r,i}^{(t)}\right|\leq\frac{\kappa_{\mathrm{st}}}{4}
for any
i\in[n_{\mathrm{st}}]
.

(S5)

\frac{1}{m}\left|\sum_{r\in[m]}\overline{\rho}_{r,i}^{(t)}-\sum_{r\in[m]}\overline{\rho}_{r,j}^{(t)}\right|\leq\kappa_{\mathrm{st}}
for any
i,j\in[n_{\mathrm{st}}]
.

(S6)

\frac{\tilde{g}_{j}^{(t)}}{\tilde{g}_{i}^{(t)}}\leq\lambda_{\mathrm{st}}
for any
i,j\in[n_{\mathrm{st}}]
.

(S7)

For any
i\in[n_{\mathrm{st}}]
and
r\in[m]
,
\left\langle{\bm{w}}_{\hat{y}_{i},r}^{(t)},\tilde{\bm{\xi}}_{i}\right\rangle>0
if
\left\langle{\bm{w}}_{\hat{y}_{i},r}^{(0)},\tilde{\bm{\xi}}_{i}\right\rangle>0
. Furthermore, for any
i\in[n_{\mathrm{st}}]
and
r\in{\mathcal{X}}_{i}
,
\overline{\rho}_{r,i}^{(t)}=\max_{r^{\prime}\in[m]}\overline{\rho}_{r^{\prime},i}^{(t)}
.

(S8)

Let
x_{t}
be the unique solution of

x_{t}+\exp(x_{t}+\kappa_{\mathrm{st}}/16)=\frac{\eta\sigma_{p}^{2}d}{8mn_{\mathrm{st}}}t+\exp(\kappa_{\mathrm{st}}/4).

It holds that for any
i\in[n_{\mathrm{st}}]
,

x_{t}\leq\frac{1}{m}\sum_{r\in[m]}\overline{\rho}_{r,i}^{(t)}.

Proof of Lemma
C.3
.

It is trivial for the case
t=0
. Assume the conclusions hold at iteration
t\leq\tau
and we will prove for the case
t=\tau+1
.

(S1)
: We fix arbitrary
i\in[n_{\mathrm{st}}]
and
r\in[m]
.

Let us prove the first statement. If
\underline{\rho}_{r,i}^{(\tau)}\geq-\alpha_{\mathrm{st}}-4\beta_{\mathrm{st}}\log T^{*}
, then we have

\underline{\rho}_{r,i}^{(\tau+1)}=\underline{\rho}_{r,i}^{(\tau)}-\frac{\eta}{mn_{\mathrm{st}}}\tilde{g}_{i}^{(\tau)}\lVert\tilde{\bm{\xi}}_{i}\rVert^{2}\geq-\alpha_{\mathrm{st}}-4\beta_{\mathrm{st}}\log T^{*}-\frac{3\eta\sigma_{p}^{2}d}{2mn_{\mathrm{st}}}\geq-\alpha_{\mathrm{st}}-5\beta_{\mathrm{st}}\log T^{*},

where the first inequality follows from (
8
) and the second inequality follows from (
10
). Otherwise, we have

\displaystyle\left\langle{\bm{w}}_{-\hat{y}_{i},r}^{(\tau)},\tilde{\bm{\xi}}_{i}\right\rangle

\displaystyle=\left\langle{\bm{w}}_{-\hat{y}_{i},r}^{(0)},\tilde{\bm{\xi}}_{i}\right\rangle+\underline{\rho}_{r,i}^{(\tau)}+\sum_{j\in[n_{\mathrm{st}}]\setminus\{i\}}\rho_{-\hat{y}_{i},r,j}^{(\tau)}\frac{\langle\tilde{\bm{\xi}}_{i},\tilde{\bm{\xi}}_{j}\rangle}{\lVert\tilde{\bm{\xi}}_{j}\rVert^{2}}

\displaystyle\leq\alpha_{\mathrm{st}}+(-\alpha_{\mathrm{st}}-4\beta_{\mathrm{st}}\log T^{*})+\sum_{j\in[n_{\mathrm{st}}]\setminus\{i\}}\left|\rho_{-\hat{y}_{i},r,j}^{(\tau)}\right|\frac{\left|\langle\tilde{\bm{\xi}}_{i},\tilde{\bm{\xi}}_{j}\rangle\right|}{\lVert\tilde{\bm{\xi}}_{j}\rVert^{2}}

\displaystyle\leq-4\beta_{\mathrm{st}}\log T^{*}+n_{\mathrm{st}}\cdot 4\log T^{*}\cdot\frac{\beta_{\mathrm{st}}}{n_{\mathrm{st}}}

\displaystyle=0.

It implies
\underline{\rho}_{r,i}^{(\tau+1)}=\underline{\rho}_{r,i}^{(\tau)}\geq-\alpha_{\mathrm{st}}-5\beta_{\mathrm{st}}\log T^{*}
and we have desired conclusion.

Next, we prove the second statement.
If
\overline{\rho}_{r,i}^{(\tau)}<3\log T^{*}
, then we have

\overline{\rho}_{r,i}^{(\tau+1)}\leq\overline{\rho}_{r,i}^{(\tau)}+\frac{\eta}{mn_{\mathrm{st}}}\tilde{g}_{i}^{(\tau)}\lVert\tilde{\bm{\xi}}_{i}\rVert^{2}\leq 3\log T^{*}+\frac{3\eta\sigma_{p}^{2}d}{2mn_{\mathrm{st}}}\leq 4\log T^{*},

where the second inequality follows from (
8
) and the third inequality follows from (
10
). Otherwise, there exists
\hat{t}<\tau
such that
\overline{\rho}_{r,i}^{(\hat{t})}\leq 3\log T^{*}<\overline{\rho}_{r,i}^{(\hat{t}+1)}
. Then, we have

\displaystyle\overline{\rho}^{(\tau+1)}_{r,i}

\displaystyle=\overline{\rho}^{(\hat{t})}_{r,i}+\left(\overline{\rho}^{(\hat{t}+1)}_{r,i}-\overline{\rho}^{(\hat{t})}_{r,i}\right)+\sum_{t=\hat{t}+1}^{\tau}\left(\overline{\rho}^{(t+1)}_{r,i}-\overline{\rho}^{(t)}_{r,i}\right)

\displaystyle\leq 3\log T^{*}+\frac{\eta}{mn_{\mathrm{st}}}\tilde{g}_{i}^{(\hat{t})}\|\tilde{\bm{\xi}}_{i}\|^{2}+\frac{\eta\|\tilde{\bm{\xi}}_{i}\|^{2}}{mn_{\mathrm{st}}}\sum_{t=\hat{t}+1}^{\tau}\tilde{g}_{i}^{(t)}

\displaystyle\leq 3\log T^{*}+\frac{\log T^{*}}{2}+\frac{3\eta\sigma_{p}^{2}d}{2mn_{\mathrm{st}}}\sum_{t=\hat{t}+1}^{\tau}\frac{1}{1+\exp\left(F_{\hat{y}_{i}}\left({{\bm{W}}_{\hat{y}_{i}}^{(t)},\tilde{\bm{X}}_{i}}\right)-F_{-\hat{y}_{i}}\left({\bm{W}}_{-\hat{y}_{i}}^{(t)},\tilde{\bm{X}}_{i}\right)\right)}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{7}{2}\log T^{*}+\frac{3\eta\sigma_{p}^{2}d}{2mn_{\mathrm{st}}}\sum_{t=\hat{t}+1}^{\tau}\exp\left(-F_{\hat{y}_{i}}\left({\bm{W}}_{\hat{y}_{i}}^{(t)},\tilde{\bm{X}}_{i}\right)+F_{-\hat{y}_{i}}\left({\bm{W}}_{-\hat{y}_{i}}^{(t)},\tilde{\bm{X}}_{i}\right)\right)

\displaystyle\leq\frac{7}{2}\log T^{*}+\frac{3\eta\sigma_{p}^{2}d}{2mn_{\mathrm{st}}}\sum_{t=\hat{t}+1}^{\tau}\exp\left(-F_{\hat{y}_{i}}\left({\bm{W}}^{(t)}_{\hat{y}_{i}},\tilde{\bm{X}}_{i}\right)+\frac{\kappa_{\mathrm{st}}}{16}\right),

where the second inequality follows from (
10
) and (
8
) and the last inequality follows from Lemma
C.2
.
For any
t=\hat{t}+1,\cdots,\tau
and
r^{\prime}\in{\mathcal{X}}_{i}
, by applying
(S7)
with iteration
t
, we have

\displaystyle\left\langle{\bm{w}}_{\hat{y}_{i},r^{\prime}}^{(t)},\tilde{\bm{\xi}}_{i}\right\rangle

\displaystyle=\left\langle{\bm{w}}_{\hat{y}_{i},r^{\prime}}^{(0)},\tilde{\bm{\xi}}_{i}\right\rangle+\overline{\rho}_{r^{\prime},i}^{(t)}+\sum_{j\in[n_{\mathrm{st}}]\setminus\{i\}}\rho_{\hat{y}_{i},r,j}^{(t)}\cdot\frac{\langle\tilde{\bm{\xi}}_{i},\tilde{\bm{\xi}}_{j}\rangle}{\lVert\tilde{\bm{\xi}}_{j}\rVert^{2}}

\displaystyle\geq\overline{\rho}_{r,i}^{(t)}-\alpha_{\mathrm{st}}-4\beta_{\mathrm{st}}\log T^{*}

\displaystyle\geq 3\log T^{*}-\alpha_{\mathrm{st}}-4\beta_{\mathrm{st}}\log T^{*}.

Therefore, we have

\displaystyle\sum_{t=\hat{t}+1}^{\tau}\exp\left(-F_{\hat{y}_{i}}\left({\bm{W}}^{(t)}_{\hat{y}_{i}},\tilde{\bm{X}}_{i}\right)\right)

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{t=\hat{t}+1}^{\tau}\exp\left(-\frac{1}{m}\sum_{r^{\prime}\in{\mathcal{X}}_{i}}\left\langle{\bm{w}}_{\hat{y}_{i},r^{\prime}}^{(t)},\tilde{\bm{\xi}}_{i}\right\rangle\right)

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{t=\hat{t}+1}^{\tau}\exp\left(-\frac{(3\log T^{*}-\alpha_{\mathrm{st}}-4\beta_{\mathrm{st}}\log T^{*})\left|{\mathcal{X}}_{i}\right|}{m}\right)

\displaystyle\leq T^{*}\exp\left(-\frac{(3\log T^{*}-\alpha_{\mathrm{st}}-4\beta_{\mathrm{st}}\log T^{*})\left|{\mathcal{X}}_{i}\right|}{m}\right)

\displaystyle\leq T^{*}\exp(-\log T^{*})=1,

where the last inequality follows from (
6
) and (
9
). Finally, we conclude

\overline{\rho}^{(\tau+1)}_{r,i}\leq\frac{7}{2}\log T^{*}+\frac{3\eta\sigma_{p}^{2}d}{2mn_{\mathrm{st}}}\exp(\kappa_{\mathrm{st}}/16)\leq 4\log T^{*},

where the last inequality follows from (
10
).

(S2)
: We fix an arbitrary
s\in\{\pm 1\}
and
i\in[n_{\mathrm{st}}]
.

For any
r\in[m]
, we have

\displaystyle\quad\frac{mn_{\mathrm{st}}}{\eta\lVert{\bm{\mu}}\rVert^{2}}\left(\overline{M}_{s,r}^{(\tau+1)}-\overline{M}_{s,r}^{(\tau)}\right)

\displaystyle=\sum_{l\in[2]}\left(\sum_{j\in{\mathcal{C}}_{{\bm{\mu}}_{s}}^{(l)}}\tilde{g}_{j}^{(\tau)}-\sum_{j\in{\mathcal{F}}_{{\bm{\mu}}_{s}}^{(l)}}\tilde{g}_{j}^{(\tau)}\right)\cdot\mathbbm{1}\left[\left\langle{\bm{w}}_{s,r}^{(\tau)},{\bm{\mu}}_{s}\right\rangle>0\right]

\displaystyle\geq\sum_{l\in[2]}\left(\left|{\mathcal{C}}_{{\bm{\mu}}_{s}}^{(l)}\right|/\lambda_{\mathrm{st}}-\left|{\mathcal{F}}_{{\bm{\mu}}_{s}}^{(l)}\right|\lambda_{\mathrm{st}}\right)\tilde{g}_{i}^{(\tau)}\cdot\mathbbm{1}\left[\left\langle{\bm{w}}_{s,r}^{(\tau)},{\bm{\mu}}_{s}\right\rangle>0\right]

\displaystyle\geq 2\Big(\left(1-C_{\mathrm{st}}^{-1}\right)n_{\bm{\mu}}/\lambda_{\mathrm{st}}-C_{\mathrm{st}}^{-1}n_{\bm{\mu}}\lambda_{\mathrm{st}}\Big)\tilde{g}_{i}^{(\tau)}\cdot\mathbbm{1}\left[\left\langle{\bm{w}}_{s,r}^{(\tau)},{\bm{\mu}}_{s}\right\rangle>0\right]

\displaystyle\geq\frac{n_{\bm{\mu}}\tilde{g}_{i}^{(\tau)}}{\lambda_{\mathrm{st}}}\cdot\mathbbm{1}\left[\left\langle{\bm{w}}_{s,r}^{(\tau)},{\bm{\mu}}_{s}\right\rangle>0\right]

(12)

\displaystyle\geq 0,

where the first inequality follows from
(S6)
with iteration
\tau
and the third inequality follows from large choice of
C_{\mathrm{st}}
.

For any
r\in{\mathcal{A}}_{s}
, from
(S2)
at iteration
0,\dots,\tau
, we have
\left\langle{\bm{w}}_{s,r}^{(\tau)},{\bm{\nu}}_{s}\right\rangle>0
. Hence, we have

\displaystyle\frac{mn_{\mathrm{st}}}{\eta\lVert{\bm{\nu}}\rVert^{2}}\left(\overline{N}_{s,r}^{(\tau+1)}-\overline{N}_{s,r}^{(\tau)}\right)

\displaystyle=\sum_{l\in[2]}\left(\sum_{j\in{\mathcal{C}}_{{\bm{\nu}}_{s}}^{(l)}}\tilde{g}_{j}^{(\tau)}-\sum_{j\in{\mathcal{F}}_{{\bm{\nu}}_{s}}^{(l)}}\tilde{g}_{j}^{(\tau)}\right)

\displaystyle\geq\sum_{l\in[2]}\left(\left|{\mathcal{C}}_{{\bm{\nu}}_{s}}^{(l)}\right|/\lambda_{\mathrm{st}}-\left|{\mathcal{F}}_{{\bm{\nu}}_{s}}^{(l)}\right|\lambda_{\mathrm{st}}\right)\tilde{g}_{i}^{(\tau)}

\displaystyle\geq 2\Big(\left(1-C_{\mathrm{st}}^{-1}\right)n_{\bm{\nu}}/\lambda_{\mathrm{st}}-C_{\mathrm{st}}^{-1}n_{\bm{\nu}}\lambda_{\mathrm{st}}\Big)\tilde{g}_{i}^{(\tau)}

\displaystyle\geq\frac{n_{\bm{\nu}}\tilde{g}_{i}^{(\tau)}}{\lambda_{\mathrm{st}}}

(13)

\displaystyle\geq 0,

where the first inequality follows from
(S6)
with iteration
\tau
and the third inequality follows from the large choice of
C_{\mathrm{st}}
.

Similarly, for any
r\in{\mathcal{B}}_{s}
, from
(S2)
with iteration
0,\dots,\tau
, we have
\left\langle{\bm{w}}_{s,r}^{(\tau)},{\bm{\nu}}_{s}\right\rangle<0
. Hence, we have

\displaystyle\frac{mn_{\mathrm{st}}}{\eta\lVert{\bm{\nu}}\rVert^{2}}\left(\overline{N}_{s,r}^{(\tau)}-\overline{N}_{s,r}^{(\tau+1)}\right)

\displaystyle=\sum_{l\in[2]}\left(\sum_{j\in{\mathcal{C}}_{-{\bm{\nu}}_{s}}^{(l)}}\tilde{g}_{j}^{(\tau)}-\sum_{j\in{\mathcal{F}}_{-{\bm{\nu}}_{s}}^{(l)}}\tilde{g}_{j}^{(\tau)}\right)

\displaystyle\geq\sum_{l\in[2]}\left(\left|{\mathcal{C}}_{-{\bm{\nu}}_{s}}^{(l)}\right|/\lambda_{\mathrm{st}}-\left|{\mathcal{F}}_{-{\bm{\nu}}_{s}}^{(l)}\right|\lambda_{\mathrm{st}}\right)\tilde{g}_{i}^{(\tau)}

\displaystyle\geq 2\Big(\left(1-C_{\mathrm{st}}^{-1}\right)n_{{\bm{\nu}}_{s}}/\lambda_{\mathrm{st}}-C_{\mathrm{st}}^{-1}n_{{\bm{\nu}}_{s}}\lambda_{\mathrm{st}}\Big)\tilde{g}_{i}^{(\tau)}

\displaystyle\geq\frac{n_{{\bm{\nu}}_{s}}\tilde{g}_{i}^{(\tau)}}{\lambda_{\mathrm{st}}}

\displaystyle\geq 0,

where the first inequality follows from
(S6)
with iteration
\tau
and the third inequality follows from large choice of
C_{\mathrm{st}}>0
.

Let us prove the last part.
For any
r\in[m]
, if
\underline{M}_{s,r}^{(\tau)}\leq-\alpha_{\mathrm{st}}
, then we have
\left\langle{\bm{w}}_{s,r}^{(\tau)},{\bm{\mu}}_{-s}\right\rangle<0
.
Hence,
\left|\underline{M}_{s,r}^{(\tau+1)}\right|=\left|\underline{M}_{s,r}^{(\tau)}\right|\leq\alpha_{\mathrm{st}}+\beta_{\mathrm{st}}
by Lemma
A.3
. Otherwise,
\underline{M}_{s,r}^{(\tau)}>-\alpha_{\mathrm{st}}
implies

\displaystyle\quad\frac{mn_{\mathrm{st}}}{\eta\lVert{\bm{\mu}}\rVert^{2}}\left(\underline{M}_{s,r}^{(\tau+1)}-\underline{M}_{s,r}^{(\tau)}\right)

\displaystyle=-\sum_{l\in[2]}\left(\sum_{j\in{\mathcal{C}}_{{\bm{\mu}}_{-s}}^{(l)}}\tilde{g}_{j}^{(\tau)}-\sum_{j\in{\mathcal{F}}_{{\bm{\mu}}_{-s}}^{(l)}}\tilde{g}_{j}^{(\tau)}\right)\cdot\mathbbm{1}\left[\left\langle{\bm{w}}_{s,r}^{(\tau)},{\bm{\mu}}_{-s}\right\rangle>0\right]

\displaystyle\leq-\sum_{l\in[2]}\left(\left|{\mathcal{C}}_{{\bm{\mu}}_{-s}}^{(l)}\right|/\lambda_{\mathrm{st}}-\left|{\mathcal{F}}_{{\bm{\mu}}_{-s}}^{(l)}\right|\lambda_{\mathrm{st}}\right)\tilde{g}_{i}^{(\tau)}\cdot\mathbbm{1}\left[\left\langle{\bm{w}}_{s,r}^{(\tau)},{\bm{\mu}}_{-s}\right\rangle>0\right]

\displaystyle\leq-2\Big(\left(1-C_{\mathrm{st}}^{-1}\right)\cdot n_{\bm{\mu}}/\lambda_{\mathrm{st}}-C_{\mathrm{st}}^{-1}n_{\bm{\mu}}\lambda_{\mathrm{st}}\Big)\tilde{g}_{i}^{(\tau)}\mathbbm{1}\left[\left\langle{\bm{w}}_{s,r}^{(\tau)},{\bm{\mu}}_{-s}\right\rangle>0\right]

\displaystyle\leq 0,

where the first inequality follows from
(S6)
with iteration
\tau
and the last inequality follows from the large choice of
C_{\mathrm{st}}
.
Thus,
\underline{M}_{s,r}^{(\tau+1)}\leq\underline{M}_{s,r}^{(\tau)}\leq\alpha_{\mathrm{st}}+\beta_{\mathrm{st}}
. In addition,

\displaystyle\quad\frac{mn_{\mathrm{st}}}{\eta\lVert{\bm{\mu}}\rVert^{2}}\left(\underline{M}_{s,r}^{(\tau+1)}-\underline{M}_{s,r}^{(\tau)}\right)

\displaystyle=-\sum_{l\in[2]}\left(\sum_{j\in{\mathcal{C}}_{{\bm{\mu}}_{-s}}^{(l)}}\tilde{g}_{j}^{(\tau)}-\sum_{j\in{\mathcal{F}}_{{\bm{\mu}}_{-s}}^{(l)}}\tilde{g}_{j}^{(\tau)}\right)\cdot\mathbbm{1}\left[\left\langle{\bm{w}}_{s,r}^{(\tau)},{\bm{\mu}}_{-s}\right\rangle>0\right]

\displaystyle\geq-\sum_{l\in[2]}\left(\left|{\mathcal{C}}_{{\bm{\mu}}_{-s}}^{(l)}\right|\lambda_{\mathrm{st}}-\left|{\mathcal{F}}_{{\bm{\mu}}_{-s}}^{(l)}\right|/\lambda_{\mathrm{st}})\right)\tilde{g}_{i}^{(\tau)}\cdot\mathbbm{1}\left[\left\langle{\bm{w}}_{s,r}^{(\tau)},{\bm{\mu}}_{-s}\right\rangle>0\right]

\displaystyle\geq-2\Big(\left(1-C_{\mathrm{st}}^{-1}\right)n_{{\bm{\mu}}}\lambda_{\mathrm{st}}-C_{\mathrm{st}}^{-1}n_{\bm{\mu}}/\lambda_{\mathrm{st}}\Big)\tilde{g}_{i}^{(\tau)}\cdot\mathbbm{1}\left[\left\langle{\bm{w}}_{s,r}^{(\tau)},{\bm{\mu}}_{-s}\right\rangle>0\right]

\displaystyle\geq-2\lambda_{\mathrm{st}}n_{\bm{\mu}}

\displaystyle\geq-2\lambda_{\mathrm{st}}n_{\mathrm{st}},

where the first inequality follows from
(S6)
with iteration
\tau
. Therefore, we have

\underline{M}_{s,r}^{(\tau+1)}\geq\underline{M}_{s,r}^{(\tau)}-\frac{2\lambda_{\mathrm{st}}\eta\lVert{\bm{\mu}}\rVert^{2}}{m}\geq-\alpha_{\mathrm{st}}-\frac{2\lambda_{\mathrm{st}}\eta\lVert{\bm{\mu}}\rVert^{2}}{m}\geq-\alpha_{\mathrm{st}}-\beta_{\mathrm{st}},

where the last inequality follows from (
10
).

From Lemma
A.3
, for any
r\in[m]
,

\left|\underline{N}_{s,r}^{(\tau+1)}-\underline{N}_{s,r}^{(\tau)}\right|\leq\frac{2\eta\left\|{\bm{\nu}}\right\|^{2}}{m}\leq\alpha_{\mathrm{st}}.

Therefore, it suffices to show that
\underline{N}_{s,r}^{(\tau+1)}\leq\underline{N}_{s,r}^{(\tau)}
when
\underline{N}_{s,r}^{(\tau)}>\alpha_{\mathrm{st}}
and
\underline{N}_{s,r}^{(\tau+1)}\geq\underline{N}_{s,r}^{(\tau)}
when
\underline{N}_{s,r}^{(\tau)}<-\alpha_{\mathrm{st}}
.
If
\underline{N}_{s,r}^{(\tau)}>\alpha_{\mathrm{st}}
, then we have

\left\langle{\bm{w}}_{s,r}^{(\tau)},{\bm{\nu}}_{-s}\right\rangle=\left\langle{\bm{w}}_{s,r}^{(0)},{\bm{\nu}}_{-s}\right\rangle+\underline{N}_{s,r}^{(\tau)}>0.

Hence, we have

\displaystyle\quad\frac{mn_{\mathrm{st}}}{\eta\lVert{\bm{\nu}}\rVert^{2}}\left(\underline{N}_{s,r}^{(\tau+1)}-\underline{N}_{s,r}^{(\tau)}\right)

\displaystyle=-\sum_{l\in[2]}\left(\sum_{j\in{\mathcal{C}}_{{\bm{\nu}}_{-s}}^{(l)}}\tilde{g}_{j}^{(\tau)}-\sum_{j\in{\mathcal{F}}_{{\bm{\nu}}_{-s}}^{(l)}}\tilde{g}_{j}^{(\tau)}\right)

\displaystyle\leq-\sum_{l\in[2]}\left(\left|{\mathcal{C}}_{{\bm{\nu}}_{-s}}^{(l)}\right|/\lambda_{\mathrm{st}}-\left|{\mathcal{F}}_{{\bm{\nu}}_{-s}}^{(l)}\right|\lambda_{\mathrm{st}}\right)\tilde{g}_{i}^{(\tau)}

\displaystyle\leq-2\Big(\left(1-C_{\mathrm{st}}^{-1}\right)n_{{\bm{\nu}}}/\lambda_{\mathrm{st}}-C_{\mathrm{st}}^{-1}\cdot n_{{\bm{\nu}}}\lambda_{\mathrm{st}}\Big)\tilde{g}_{i}^{(\tau)}

\displaystyle\leq 0,

where the first inequality follows from
(S6)
with iteration
\tau
and the last inequality follows from the large choice of
C_{\mathrm{st}}
.
Using the similar argument, we can also show that
\underline{N}_{s,r}^{(\tau+1)}\geq\underline{N}_{s,r}^{(\tau)}
when
\underline{N}_{s,r}^{(\tau)}<-\alpha_{\mathrm{st}}
and we have desired conclusion.

(S3)
: We fix arbitrary
s\in\{\pm 1\}
and
i\in[n_{\mathrm{st}}]
.

From (
C.1
) and
(S2)
at iteration
0,\dots,\tau
, we have

\displaystyle\sum_{r\in[m]}\overline{M}_{s,r}^{(\tau+1)}-\sum_{r\in[m]}\overline{M}_{s,r}^{(\tau)}

\displaystyle\geq\frac{\eta\left\|{\bm{\mu}}\right\|^{2}}{mn_{\mathrm{st}}}\cdot\frac{n_{\bm{\mu}}\tilde{g}_{i}^{(\tau)}}{\lambda_{\mathrm{st}}}\cdot\left|{\mathcal{M}}_{s}\right|

\displaystyle\geq\frac{n_{\bm{\mu}}\mathrm{SNR}_{\bm{\mu}}^{2}}{12\lambda_{\mathrm{st}}n_{\mathrm{st}}}\eta\tilde{g}_{i}^{(\tau)}\lVert\tilde{\bm{\xi}}_{i}\rVert^{2}

\displaystyle\geq\frac{n_{\bm{\mu}}\mathrm{SNR}_{\bm{\mu}}^{2}}{12\lambda_{\mathrm{st}}}\left(\sum_{r\in[m]}\overline{\rho}_{r,i}^{(\tau+1)}-\sum_{r\in[m]}\overline{\rho}_{r,i}^{(\tau)}\right),

where the second inequality follows from (
8
) and (
9
). Combining with
(S3)
at iteration
\tau
, we have

\frac{n_{{\bm{\mu}}}\mathrm{SNR}_{\bm{\mu}}^{2}}{12\lambda_{\mathrm{st}}}\cdot\sum_{r\in[m]}\overline{\rho}_{r,i}^{(\tau+1)}\leq\sum_{r\in[m]}\overline{M}_{s,r}^{(\tau+1)}.

For any
r\in[m]
, we have

\displaystyle\quad\frac{mn_{\mathrm{st}}}{\eta\lVert{\bm{\mu}}\rVert^{2}}\left(\overline{M}_{s,r}^{(\tau+1)}-\overline{M}_{s,r}^{(\tau)}\right)

\displaystyle=\sum_{l\in[2]}\left(\sum_{j\in{\mathcal{C}}_{{\bm{\mu}}_{s}}^{(l)}}\tilde{g}_{j}^{(\tau)}-\sum_{j\in{\mathcal{F}}_{{\bm{\mu}}_{s}}^{(l)}}\tilde{g}_{j}^{(\tau)}\right)\cdot\mathbbm{1}\left[\left\langle{\bm{w}}_{s,r}^{(\tau)},{\bm{\mu}}_{s}\right\rangle>0\right]

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{l\in[2]}\left(\left|{\mathcal{C}}_{{\bm{\mu}}_{s}}^{(l)}\right|\lambda_{\mathrm{st}}-\left|{\mathcal{F}}_{{\bm{\mu}}_{s}}^{(l)}\right|/\lambda_{\mathrm{st}}\right)\tilde{g}_{i}^{(\tau)}\cdot\mathbbm{1}\left[\left\langle{\bm{w}}_{s,r}^{(\tau)},{\bm{\mu}}_{s}\right\rangle>0\right]

\displaystyle\leq\ \lambda_{\mathrm{st}}\sum_{l\in[2]}\left|{\mathcal{C}}_{{\bm{\mu}}_{s}}^{(l)}\right|\tilde{g}_{i}^{(\tau)}\cdot\mathbbm{1}\left[\left\langle{\bm{w}}_{s,r}^{(\tau)},{\bm{\mu}}_{s}\right\rangle>0\right]

\displaystyle\leq 2\lambda_{\mathrm{st}}\left(1+C_{\mathrm{st}}^{-1}\right)n_{\bm{\mu}}\tilde{g}_{i}^{(\tau)}\cdot\mathbbm{1}\left[\left\langle{\bm{w}}_{s,r}^{(\tau)},{\bm{\mu}}_{s}\right\rangle>0\right]

\displaystyle\leq 3\lambda_{\mathrm{st}}n_{\bm{\mu}}\tilde{g}_{i}^{(\tau)}\cdot\mathbbm{1}\left[\left\langle{\bm{w}}_{s,r}^{(\tau)},{\bm{\mu}}_{s}\right\rangle>0\right]

where the first inequality follows from
(S6)
with iteration
\tau
.
Hence, we have

\displaystyle\sum_{r\in[m]}\overline{M}_{s,r}^{(\tau+1)}-\sum_{r\in[m]}\overline{M}_{s,r}^{(\tau)}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{\lambda_{\mathrm{st}}\eta\left\|{\bm{\mu}}\right\|^{2}}{mn_{\mathrm{st}}}n_{\bm{\mu}}\tilde{g}_{i}^{(\tau)}\left|{\mathcal{M}}_{s}\right|

\displaystyle\leq\frac{6\lambda_{\mathrm{st}}\eta}{n_{\mathrm{st}}}n_{{\bm{\mu}}}\mathrm{SNR}_{\bm{\mu}}^{2}\tilde{g}_{i}^{(\tau)}\lVert\tilde{\bm{\xi}}_{i}\rVert^{2}

\displaystyle\leq 6\lambda_{\mathrm{st}}n_{\bm{\mu}}\mathrm{SNR}_{\bm{\mu}}^{2}\left(\sum_{r\in[m]}\overline{\rho}_{r,i}^{(\tau+1)}-\sum_{r\in[m]}\overline{\rho}_{r,i}^{(\tau)}\right),

where the second and third inequalities follow from (
8
) and (
9
), and
(S7)
with iteration
\tau
.
Combining with
(S3)
at iteration
\tau
, we have

\sum_{r\in[m]}\overline{M}_{s,r}^{(\tau+1)}\leq 6\lambda_{\mathrm{st}}n_{{\bm{\mu}}}\mathrm{SNR}_{\bm{\mu}}^{2}\cdot\sum_{r\in[m]}\overline{\rho}_{r,i}^{(\tau+1)}.

From (
C.1
) and
(S2)
at iteration
0,\dots,\tau
, we have

\displaystyle\sum_{r\in{\mathcal{A}}_{s}}\overline{N}_{s,r}^{(\tau+1)}-\sum_{r\in{\mathcal{A}}_{s}}\overline{N}_{s,r}^{(\tau)}

\displaystyle\geq\frac{\eta\left\|{\bm{\nu}}\right\|^{2}}{mn_{\mathrm{st}}}\cdot\frac{n_{\bm{\nu}}\tilde{g}_{i}^{(\tau)}}{2\lambda_{\mathrm{st}}}\cdot\left|{\mathcal{A}}_{s}\right|

\displaystyle\geq\frac{n_{\bm{\nu}}\mathrm{SNR}_{\bm{\nu}}^{2}}{12\lambda_{\mathrm{st}}n_{\mathrm{st}}}\eta\tilde{g}_{i}^{(\tau)}\lVert\tilde{\bm{\xi}}_{i}\rVert^{2}

\displaystyle\geq\frac{n_{\bm{\nu}}\mathrm{SNR}_{\bm{\nu}}^{2}}{12\lambda_{\mathrm{st}}}\left(\sum_{r\in[m]}\overline{\rho}_{r,i}^{(\tau+1)}-\sum_{r\in[m]}\overline{\rho}_{r,i}^{(\tau)}\right),

where the second inequality follows from (
8
) and (
9
). Combining with
(S3)
at iteration
\tau
, we have

\frac{n_{{\bm{\nu}}}\mathrm{SNR}_{\bm{\nu}}^{2}}{12\lambda_{\mathrm{st}}}\cdot\sum_{r\in[m]}\overline{\rho}_{r,i}^{(\tau+1)}\leq\sum_{r\in{\mathcal{A}}_{s}}\overline{N}_{s,r}^{(\tau+1)}.

For any
r\in{\mathcal{A}}_{s}
, we have

\displaystyle\frac{mn_{\mathrm{st}}}{\eta\lVert{\bm{\nu}}\rVert^{2}}\left(\overline{N}_{s,r}^{(\tau+1)}-\overline{N}_{s,r}^{(\tau)}\right)

\displaystyle=\sum_{l\in[2]}\left(\sum_{j\in{\mathcal{C}}_{{\bm{\nu}}_{s}}^{(l)}}\tilde{g}_{j}^{(\tau)}-\sum_{j\in{\mathcal{F}}_{{\bm{\nu}}_{s}}^{(l)}}\tilde{g}_{j}^{(\tau)}\right)

\displaystyle\leq\lambda_{\mathrm{st}}\sum_{l\in[2]}\left|{\mathcal{C}}_{{\bm{\nu}}_{s}}^{(l)}\right|\tilde{g}_{i}^{(\tau)}

\displaystyle\leq 2\lambda_{\mathrm{st}}\left(1+C_{\mathrm{st}}^{-1}\right)n_{\bm{\nu}}\tilde{g}_{i}^{(\tau)}

\displaystyle\leq 3\lambda_{\mathrm{st}}n_{\bm{\nu}}\tilde{g}_{i}^{(\tau)},

where the first inequality follows from
(S6)
and the third inequality follows from the large choice of
C_{\mathrm{st}}
. Hence, we have

\displaystyle\sum_{r\in{\mathcal{A}}_{s}}\overline{N}_{s,r}^{(\tau+1)}-\sum_{r\in{\mathcal{A}}_{s}}\overline{N}_{s,r}^{(\tau)}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{\eta\left\|{\bm{\nu}}\right\|^{2}}{mn_{\mathrm{st}}}\cdot 3\lambda_{\mathrm{st}}n_{\bm{\nu}}\tilde{g}_{i}^{(\tau)}\left|{\mathcal{A}}_{s}\right|

\displaystyle\leq\frac{6\lambda_{\mathrm{st}}n_{{\bm{\nu}}}\mathrm{SNR}_{\bm{\nu}}^{2}}{n_{\mathrm{st}}}\eta\tilde{g}_{i}^{(\tau)}\lVert\tilde{\bm{\xi}}_{i}\rVert^{2}

\displaystyle\leq 6\lambda_{\mathrm{st}}n_{\bm{\nu}}\mathrm{SNR}_{\bm{\nu}}^{2}\left(\sum_{r\in[m]}\overline{\rho}_{r,i}^{(\tau+1)}-\sum_{r\in[m]}\overline{\rho}_{r,i}^{(\tau)}\right),

where the second and third inequalities follow from (
8
) and (
9
).
Combining with
(S3)
at iteration
\tau
, we have

\sum_{r\in{\mathcal{A}}_{s}}\overline{N}_{s,r}^{(\tau+1)}\leq 6\lambda_{\mathrm{st}}n_{\bm{\nu}}\mathrm{SNR}_{\bm{\nu}}^{2}\cdot\sum_{r\in[m]}\overline{\rho}_{r,i}^{(\tau+1)}.

Using a similar argument, we can also show that

\frac{n_{\bm{\nu}}\mathrm{SNR}_{\bm{\nu}}^{2}}{12\lambda_{\mathrm{st}}}\cdot\sum_{r\in[m]}\overline{\rho}_{r,i}^{(\tau+1)}\leq-\sum_{r\in{\mathcal{B}}_{s}}\overline{N}_{s,r}^{(\tau+1)}\leq 6\lambda_{\mathrm{st}}n_{{\bm{\nu}}}\mathrm{SNR}_{\bm{\nu}}^{2}\cdot\sum_{r\in[m]}\overline{\rho}_{r,i}^{(\tau+1)}.

(S4)
: We fix arbitrary
i\in[n_{\mathrm{st}}]
. From
(S3)
at iteration
\tau+1
which we have already shown, we have

\displaystyle\frac{1}{m}\sum_{r\in{\mathcal{M}}_{s}}\overline{M}_{s,r}^{(\tau+1)}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{m}\sum_{r\in[m]}\overline{M}_{s,r}^{(\tau+1)}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{6\lambda_{\mathrm{st}}n_{\bm{\mu}}\mathrm{SNR}_{\bm{\mu}}^{2}}{m}\cdot\sum_{r\in[m]}\overline{\rho}_{r,i}^{(\tau+1)}

\displaystyle\leq 24\lambda_{\mathrm{st}}n_{\bm{\mu}}\mathrm{SNR}_{\bm{\mu}}^{2}\log T^{*}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{\kappa_{\mathrm{st}}}{64},

where the first equality follows from
(S2)
at iteration
0,\dots,\tau
, the second inequality follows from
(S1)
and the last inequality follows from Condition
3.2
.
Similarly, we have

\frac{1}{m}\sum_{r\in{\mathcal{A}}_{s}}\overline{N}_{s,r}^{(\tau+1)}\leq\frac{6\lambda_{\mathrm{st}}n_{\bm{\nu}}\mathrm{SNR}_{\bm{\nu}}^{2}}{m}\cdot\sum_{r\in[m]}\overline{\rho}_{r,i}^{(\tau+1)}\leq 24\lambda_{\mathrm{st}}n_{\bm{\nu}}\mathrm{SNR}_{\bm{\nu}}^{2}\log T^{*}\leq\frac{\kappa_{\mathrm{st}}}{64}

and

-\frac{1}{m}\sum_{r\in{\mathcal{B}}_{s}}\overline{N}_{s,r}^{(\tau+1)}\leq\frac{6\lambda_{\mathrm{st}}n_{\bm{\nu}}\mathrm{SNR}_{\bm{\nu}}^{2}}{m}\cdot\sum_{r\in[m]}\overline{\rho}_{r,i}^{(\tau+1)}\leq 24\lambda_{\mathrm{st}}n_{\bm{\nu}}\mathrm{SNR}_{\bm{\nu}}^{2}\log T^{*}\leq\frac{\kappa_{\mathrm{st}}}{64}.

Therefore, for any
s\in\{\pm 1\}
, due to (
6
) and the above three inequalities, we have

\frac{1}{m}\sum_{r\in[m]}\sigma\left(\left\langle{\bm{w}}_{s,r}^{(\tau+1)},{\bm{\mu}}_{s}\right\rangle\right),\frac{1}{m}\sum_{r\in[m]}\sigma\left(\left\langle{\bm{w}}_{s,r}^{(\tau+1)},{\bm{\nu}}_{s}\right\rangle\right),\frac{1}{m}\sum_{r\in[m]}\sigma\left(\left\langle{\bm{w}}_{s,r}^{(\tau+1)},-{\bm{\nu}}_{s}\right\rangle\right)\leq\frac{\kappa_{\mathrm{st}}}{32}.

(14)

Together with applying Lemma
C.2
and , we have

\displaystyle\left|\hat{y}_{i}f_{\mathrm{st}}\left({\bm{W}}^{(\tau+1)},\tilde{\bm{X}}_{i}\right)-\frac{1}{m}\sum_{r\in[m]}\overline{\rho}_{r,i}^{(\tau+1)}\right|

\displaystyle=\left|F_{\hat{y}_{i}}\left({\bm{W}}_{\hat{y}_{i}}^{(\tau)},\tilde{\bm{X}}_{i}\right)-\frac{1}{m}\sum_{r\in[m]}\overline{\rho}_{r,i}^{(\tau)}\right|+F_{-\hat{y}_{i}}\left({\bm{W}}_{-\hat{y}_{i}}^{(\tau)},\tilde{\bm{X}}_{i}\right)

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{m}\sum_{r\in[m]}\left|\sigma\left(\left\langle{\bm{w}}_{\hat{y}_{i},r}^{(\tau+1)},\tilde{\bm{\xi}}_{i}\right\rangle-\overline{\rho}_{r,i}^{(\tau+1)}\right)\right|+\frac{1}{m}\sum_{l\in[2]}\sum_{r\in[m]}\sigma\left(\left\langle{\bm{w}}_{\hat{y}_{i},r},\tilde{\bm{v}}_{i}^{(l)}\right\rangle-\overline{\rho}_{r,i}^{(\tau+1)}\right)+\frac{\kappa_{\mathrm{st}}}{16}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{\kappa_{\mathrm{st}}}{4}.

(S5)
: We fix
i,j\in[n_{\mathrm{st}}]
and we assume
\frac{1}{m}\sum_{r\in[m]}\left[\overline{\rho}_{r,i}^{(\tau)}-\overline{\rho}_{r,j}^{(\tau)}\right]>0
, without loss of generality.

From the triangular inequality, (
8
), and (
10
), we have

\displaystyle\quad\left|\frac{1}{m}\sum_{r\in[m]}\left[\overline{\rho}_{r,i}^{(\tau+1)}-\overline{\rho}_{r,j}^{(\tau+1)}\right]-\frac{1}{m}\sum_{r\in[m]}\left[\overline{\rho}_{r,i}^{(\tau)}-\overline{\rho}_{r,j}^{(\tau)}\right]\right|
