\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{m}\sum_{r\in[m]}\left[\overline{\rho}_{r,i}^{(\tau+1)}-\overline{\rho}_{r,i}^{(\tau)}\right]+\frac{1}{m}\sum_{r\in[m]}\left[\overline{\rho}_{r,j}^{(\tau+1)}-\overline{\rho}_{r,j}^{(\tau)}\right]

\displaystyle\leq\frac{\eta}{mn_{\mathrm{st}}}\tilde{g}_{i}^{(\tau)}\lVert\tilde{\bm{\xi}}_{i}\rVert^{2}+\frac{\eta}{mn_{\mathrm{st}}}\tilde{g}_{j}^{(\tau)}\lVert\tilde{\bm{\xi}}_{j}\rVert^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{3\eta\sigma_{p}^{2}d}{mn_{\mathrm{st}}}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{\kappa_{\mathrm{st}}}{2}.

Hence, we have
\frac{1}{m}\sum_{r\in[m]}\left[\overline{\rho}_{r,i}^{(\tau+1)}-\overline{\rho}_{r,j}^{(\tau+1)}\right]>-\frac{\kappa_{\mathrm{st}}}{2}
.

Also, if
\frac{1}{m}\sum_{r\in[m]}\left[\overline{\rho}_{r,i}^{(\tau)}-\overline{\rho}_{r,j}^{(\tau)}\right]<\frac{\kappa_{\mathrm{st}}}{2}
, then we have

\frac{1}{m}\sum_{r\in[m]}\left[\overline{\rho}_{r,i}^{(\tau+1)}-\overline{\rho}_{r,j}^{(\tau+1)}\right]\leq\frac{1}{m}\sum_{r\in[m]}\left[\overline{\rho}_{r,i}^{(\tau)}-\overline{\rho}_{r,j}^{(\tau)}\right]+\frac{\kappa_{\mathrm{st}}}{2}\leq\kappa_{\mathrm{st}}.

Otherwise, we have
\frac{\kappa_{\mathrm{st}}}{2}\leq\frac{1}{m}\sum_{r\in[m]}\left[\overline{\rho}_{r,i}^{(\tau)}-\overline{\rho}_{r,j}^{(\tau)}\right]\leq\kappa_{\mathrm{st}}
.
Together with applying Lemma
C.2
and (
14
), we have

\displaystyle\quad\hat{y}_{i}f_{\mathrm{st}}\left({\bm{W}}^{(\tau)},\tilde{\bm{X}}_{i}\right)-\hat{y}_{j}f_{\mathrm{st}}\left({\bm{W}}^{(\tau)},\tilde{\bm{X}}_{j}\right)

\displaystyle=F_{\hat{y}_{i}}\left({\bm{W}}_{\hat{y}_{i}}^{(\tau)},\tilde{\bm{X}}_{i}\right)-F_{-\hat{y}_{i}}\left({\bm{W}}_{-\hat{y}_{i}}^{(\tau)},\tilde{\bm{X}}_{i}\right)-F_{\hat{y}_{j}}\left({\bm{W}}_{\hat{y}_{j}}^{(\tau)},\tilde{\bm{X}}_{j}\right)+F_{-\hat{y}_{j}}\left({\bm{W}}_{-\hat{y}_{j}}^{(\tau)},\tilde{\bm{X}}_{j}\right)

\displaystyle\geq F_{\hat{y}_{i}}\left({\bm{W}}_{\hat{y}_{i}}^{(\tau)},\tilde{\bm{X}}_{i}\right)-F_{\hat{y}_{j}}\left({\bm{W}}_{\hat{y}_{j}}^{(\tau)},\tilde{\bm{X}}_{j}\right)-\frac{\kappa_{\mathrm{st}}}{16}

\displaystyle\geq\frac{1}{m}\sum_{r\in[m]}\left[\sigma\left(\left\langle{\bm{w}}_{\hat{y}_{i},r}^{(\tau)},\tilde{\bm{\xi}}_{i}\right\rangle\right)-\sigma\left(\left\langle{\bm{w}}_{\hat{y}_{j},r}^{(\tau)},\tilde{\bm{\xi}}_{j}\right\rangle\right)\right]-\frac{1}{m}\sum_{l\in[2]}\sum_{r\in[m]}\sigma\left(\left\langle{\bm{w}}_{\hat{y}_{j},r}^{(\tau)},\tilde{\bm{v}}_{j}^{(l)}\right\rangle\right)-\frac{\kappa_{\mathrm{st}}}{16}

\displaystyle\geq\frac{1}{m}\sum_{r\in[m]}\left[\overline{\rho}_{r,i}^{(\tau)}-\overline{\rho}_{r,j}^{(\tau)}\right]-\frac{\kappa_{\mathrm{st}}}{4}

\displaystyle\geq\frac{\kappa_{\mathrm{st}}}{4}.

Therefore, we have

\displaystyle\frac{\tilde{g}_{i}^{(\tau)}}{\tilde{g}_{j}^{(\tau)}}

\displaystyle=\frac{1+\exp\left(\hat{y}_{j}f_{\mathrm{st}}\left({\bm{W}}^{(\tau)},\tilde{\bm{X}}_{j}\right)\right)}{1+\exp\left(\hat{y}_{i}f_{\mathrm{st}}\left({\bm{W}}^{(\tau)},\tilde{\bm{X}}_{i}\right)\right)}

\displaystyle=\frac{\exp\left(-\hat{y}_{j}f_{\mathrm{st}}\left({\bm{W}}^{(\tau)},\tilde{\bm{X}}_{j}\right)\right)+1}{\exp\left(-\hat{y}_{j}f_{\mathrm{st}}\left({\bm{W}}^{(\tau)},\tilde{\bm{X}}_{j}\right)\right)+\exp\left(\hat{y}_{i}f_{\mathrm{st}}\left({\bm{W}}^{(\tau)},\tilde{\bm{X}}_{i}\right)-\hat{y}_{j}f_{\mathrm{st}}\left({\bm{W}}^{(\tau)},\tilde{\bm{X}}_{j}\right)\right)}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{\exp\left(-\hat{y}_{j}f_{\mathrm{st}}\left({\bm{W}}^{(\tau)},\tilde{\bm{X}}_{j}\right)\right)+1}{\exp\left(-\hat{y}_{j}f_{\mathrm{st}}\left({\bm{W}}^{(\tau)},\tilde{\bm{X}}_{j}\right)\right)+\exp\left(\kappa_{\mathrm{st}}/4\right)}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{\exp(\kappa_{\mathrm{st}}/16)+1}{\exp(\kappa_{\mathrm{st}}/16)+\exp(\kappa_{\mathrm{st}}/4))}

\displaystyle\leq\exp\left(-\kappa_{\mathrm{st}}/8\right),

where the second inequality follows from

-\hat{y}_{j}f_{\mathrm{st}}\left({\bm{W}}^{(\tau)},\tilde{\bm{X}}_{j}\right)\leq F_{-\hat{y}_{j}}\left({\bm{W}}_{-\hat{y}_{j}}^{(\tau)},\tilde{\bm{X}}_{j}\right)\leq\frac{\kappa_{\mathrm{st}}}{16}

and the last inequality follows from applying
\frac{z(z^{3}+1)}{z+1}=z(z^{2}-z+1)\geq z^{2}
with
z=\exp(\kappa_{\mathrm{st}}/16)
.

Therefore, we have

\displaystyle\quad\sum_{r\in[m]}\left[\overline{\rho}_{r,i}^{(\tau+1)}-\overline{\rho}_{r,j}^{(\tau+1)}\right]-\sum_{r\in[m]}\left[\overline{\rho}_{r,i}^{(\tau)}-\overline{\rho}_{r,j}^{(\tau)}\right]

\displaystyle\leq\frac{\eta}{mn_{\mathrm{st}}}\left(\tilde{g}_{i}^{(\tau)}m\lVert\tilde{\bm{\xi}}_{i}\rVert^{2}-\tilde{g}_{j}^{(\tau)}\left|{\mathcal{X}}_{j}\right|\lVert\tilde{\bm{\xi}}_{j}\rVert^{2}\right)

\displaystyle=\frac{\eta}{mn_{\mathrm{st}}}\tilde{g}_{j}^{(\tau)}\left|{\mathcal{X}}_{j}\right|\lVert\tilde{\bm{\xi}}_{j}\rVert^{2}\left(\frac{\tilde{g}_{i}^{(\tau)}m\lVert\tilde{\bm{\xi}}_{i}\rVert^{2}}{\tilde{g}_{j}^{(\tau)}\left|{\mathcal{X}}_{j}\right|\lVert\tilde{\bm{\xi}}_{j}\rVert^{2}}-1\right)

\displaystyle\leq\frac{\eta}{mn_{\mathrm{st}}}\tilde{g}_{j}^{(\tau)}\left|{\mathcal{X}}_{j}\right|\lVert\tilde{\bm{\xi}}_{j}\rVert^{2}\left(\exp(-\kappa_{\mathrm{st}}/8)\cdot 4\cdot\left(1+\beta_{\mathrm{st}}/n\right)-1\right)

\displaystyle\leq\frac{\eta}{mn_{\mathrm{st}}}\tilde{g}_{j}^{(\tau)}\left|{\mathcal{X}}_{j}\right|\lVert\tilde{\bm{\xi}}_{j}\rVert^{2}\left(12\exp(-\kappa_{\mathrm{st}}/8)-1\right)

\displaystyle=0,

where the third inequality is due to (
6
) and
1+z\leq e^{z}
for any
z\in\mathbb{R}
. Hence, we have

\frac{1}{m}\sum_{r\in[m]}\left[\overline{\rho}_{r,i}^{(\tau+1)}-\overline{\rho}_{r,j}^{(\tau+1)}\right]\leq\frac{1}{m}\sum_{r\in[m]}\left[\overline{\rho}_{r,i}^{(\tau)}-\overline{\rho}_{r,j}^{(\tau)}\right]\leq\kappa_{\mathrm{st}}.

(S6)
: We fix arbitrary
i,j\in[n_{\mathrm{st}}]
and we assume
\hat{y}_{i}f_{\mathrm{st}}\left({\bm{W}}^{(\tau+1)},\tilde{\bm{X}}_{i}\right)\geq\hat{y}_{j}f_{\mathrm{st}}\left({\bm{W}}^{(\tau+1)},\tilde{\bm{X}}_{j}\right)
, without loss of generality. By combining
(S4)
and
(S5)
at iteration
\tau+1
which we already have shown, we have

\displaystyle\quad\hat{y}_{i}f_{\mathrm{st}}\left({\bm{W}}^{(\tau+1)},\tilde{\bm{X}}_{i}\right)-\hat{y}_{j}f_{\mathrm{st}}\left({\bm{W}}^{(\tau+1)},\tilde{\bm{X}}_{j}\right)

\displaystyle\leq\left|\frac{1}{m}\sum_{r\in[m]}\left[\overline{\rho}_{r,i}^{(\tau+1)}-\overline{\rho}_{r,j}^{(\tau+1)}\right]\right|

\displaystyle\quad+\left|\hat{y}_{i}f_{\mathrm{st}}\left({\bm{W}}^{(\tau+1)},\tilde{\bm{X}}_{i}\right)-\frac{1}{m}\sum_{r\in[m]}\overline{\rho}_{r,i}^{(\tau+1)}\right|+\left|\hat{y}_{j}f_{\mathrm{st}}\left({\bm{W}}^{(\tau+1)},\tilde{\bm{X}}_{j}\right)-\frac{1}{m}\sum_{r\in[m]}\overline{\rho}_{r,j}^{(\tau+1)}\right|

\displaystyle\leq 2\kappa_{\mathrm{st}}.

Then, we have

\displaystyle\frac{\tilde{g}_{j}^{(\tau+1)}}{\tilde{g}_{i}^{(\tau+1)}}

\displaystyle=\frac{1+\exp{\left(\hat{y}_{i}f_{\mathrm{st}}\left({\bm{W}}^{(\tau+1)},\tilde{\bm{X}}_{i}\right)\right)}}{1+\exp{\left(\hat{y}_{j}f_{\mathrm{st}}\left({\bm{W}}^{(\tau+1)},\tilde{\bm{X}}_{j}\right)\right)}}

\displaystyle\leq\exp{\left[\ \hat{y}_{i}f_{\mathrm{st}}\left({\bm{W}}^{(\tau+1)},\tilde{\bm{X}}_{i}\right)-\hat{y}_{j}f_{\mathrm{st}}\left({\bm{W}}^{(\tau+1)},\tilde{\bm{X}}_{j}\right)\right]}

\displaystyle\leq\exp(2\kappa_{\mathrm{st}})

\displaystyle=\lambda_{\mathrm{st}}.

(S7)
: We fix arbitrary
i\in[n_{\mathrm{st}}]
. From
(S7)
at iteration
\tau
, we have
\left\langle{\bm{w}}_{\hat{y}_{i},r}^{(\tau)},\tilde{\bm{\xi}}_{i}\right\rangle>0
for any
r\in{\mathcal{X}}_{i}
. Therefore, we have

\overline{\rho}_{r,i}^{(\tau+1)}=\overline{\rho}_{r,i}^{(\tau)}+\frac{\eta}{mn_{\mathrm{st}}}\tilde{g}_{i}^{(\tau)}\lVert\tilde{\bm{\xi}}_{i}\rVert^{2}

and

\displaystyle\quad\left\langle{\bm{w}}_{\hat{y}_{i},r}^{(\tau+1)},\tilde{\bm{\xi}}_{i}\right\rangle-\left\langle{\bm{w}}_{\hat{y}_{i},r}^{(\tau)},\tilde{\bm{\xi}}_{i}\right\rangle

\displaystyle=\left(\overline{\rho}_{r,i}^{(\tau+1)}-\overline{\rho}_{r,i}^{(\tau)}\right)+\sum_{j\in[n_{\mathrm{st}}]\setminus\{i\}}\left(\rho_{\hat{y}_{i},r,j}^{(\tau+1)}-\rho_{\hat{y}_{i},r,j}^{(\tau)}\right)\frac{\langle\tilde{\bm{\xi}}_{i},\tilde{\bm{\xi}}_{j}\rangle}{\lVert\tilde{\bm{\xi}}_{j}\rVert^{2}}

\displaystyle\geq\frac{\eta}{mn_{\mathrm{st}}}\tilde{g}_{i}^{(\tau)}\lVert\tilde{\bm{\xi}}_{i}\rVert^{2}-\frac{\eta}{mn_{\mathrm{st}}}\sum_{j\in[n_{\mathrm{st}}]\setminus\{i\}}\tilde{g}_{j}^{(\tau)}\left|\langle\tilde{\bm{\xi}}_{i},\tilde{\bm{\xi}}_{j}\rangle\right|

\displaystyle=\frac{\eta}{mn_{\mathrm{st}}}\tilde{g}_{i}^{(\tau)}\lVert\tilde{\bm{\xi}}_{i}\rVert^{2}\left(1-\sum_{j\in[n_{\mathrm{st}}]\setminus\{i\}}\frac{\tilde{g}_{j}^{(\tau)}}{\tilde{g}_{i}^{(\tau)}}\cdot\frac{\left|\langle\tilde{\bm{\xi}}_{i},\tilde{\bm{\xi}}_{j}\rangle\right|}{\lVert\tilde{\bm{\xi}}_{i}\rVert^{2}}\right)

\displaystyle\geq\frac{\eta}{mn_{\mathrm{st}}}\tilde{g}_{i}^{(\tau)}\lVert\tilde{\bm{\xi}}_{i}\rVert^{2}(1-\lambda_{\mathrm{st}}\beta_{\mathrm{st}})

\displaystyle\geq 0,

where we use
(S6)
at iteration
\tau
, (
8
) for the second inequality, and (
6
) for the last inequality. Hence, we have
\left\langle{\bm{w}}_{\hat{y}_{i},r}^{(\tau+1)},\tilde{\bm{\xi}}_{i}\right\rangle>0
. Now we prove the second part. For any
r\in{\mathcal{X}}_{i}
and
r^{\prime}\in[m]
, we have

\overline{\rho}_{r^{\prime},i}^{(\tau+1)}\leq\overline{\rho}_{r^{\prime},i}^{(\tau)}+\frac{\eta}{mn_{\mathrm{st}}}\tilde{g}_{i}^{(\tau)}\lVert\tilde{\bm{\xi}}_{i}\rVert^{2}\leq\overline{\rho}_{r,i}^{(\tau)}+\frac{\eta}{mn_{\mathrm{st}}}\tilde{g}_{i}^{(\tau)}\lVert\tilde{\bm{\xi}}_{i}\rVert^{2}=\overline{\rho}_{r,i}^{(\tau+1)},

where the second inequality is due to
(S7)
with iteration
\tau
.

(S8)
: From
(S7)
at iteration
\tau
, we have

\displaystyle\frac{1}{m}\sum_{r\in[m]}\overline{\rho}_{r,i}^{(\tau+1)}

\displaystyle\geq\frac{1}{m}\sum_{r\in[m]}\overline{\rho}_{r,i}^{(\tau)}+\frac{\eta}{mn_{\mathrm{st}}}\tilde{g}_{i}^{(\tau)}\cdot\frac{\left|{\mathcal{X}}_{i}\right|}{m}\cdot\lVert\tilde{\bm{\xi}}_{i}\rVert^{2}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{m}\sum_{r\in[m]}\overline{\rho}_{r,i}^{(\tau)}+\frac{\eta}{mn_{\mathrm{st}}}\cdot\frac{1}{1+\exp\left(\hat{y}_{i}f_{\mathrm{st}}\left({\bm{W}}^{(\tau)},\tilde{\bm{X}}_{i}\right)\right)}\cdot\frac{\left|{\mathcal{X}}_{i}\right|}{m}\cdot\lVert\tilde{\bm{\xi}}_{i}\rVert^{2}.

From
(S4)
at iteration
\tau
, (
8
), and (
9
), we have

\frac{1}{m}\sum_{r\in[m]}\overline{\rho}_{r,i}^{(\tau+1)}\geq\frac{1}{m}\sum_{r\in[m]}\overline{\rho}_{r,i}^{(\tau)}+\frac{\eta\sigma_{p}^{2}d}{8mn_{\mathrm{st}}}\cdot\frac{1}{1+\exp(\kappa_{\mathrm{st}}/4)\exp\left(\frac{1}{m}\sum_{r\in[m]}\overline{\rho}_{r,i}^{(\tau)}\right)}.

By applying Lemma
A.5
, the fact that
z+\frac{c}{1+be^{z}}
is an increasing function for any
c\in[0,1],b>0
, and the comparison theorem, we have our conclusion.
∎

C.2
Convergence of Training Loss

In this subsection, we prove that the training loss converges below
\varepsilon
within
\mathcal{O}(\eta^{-1}\varepsilon^{-1}n_{\mathrm{st}}md^{-1}\sigma_{p}^{-2})
. All the arguments in this subsection are under Condition
3.1
and the event
E_{\mathrm{st}}
.

For any
t\in[0,T^{*}]
, from the definition of
x_{t}
, we have

x_{t}\leq\log\left(\frac{\eta\sigma_{p}^{2}d}{8mn_{\mathrm{st}}\exp(\kappa_{\mathrm{st}}/4)}t+1\right).

Combining the inequality above with the definition of
x_{t}
, we have

\displaystyle\exp(x_{t})

\displaystyle\geq\frac{\eta\sigma_{p}^{2}d}{8mn_{\mathrm{st}}\exp(\kappa_{\mathrm{st}}/4)}t+1-\exp(-\kappa_{\mathrm{st}}/4)\log\left(\frac{\eta\sigma_{p}^{2}d}{8mn_{\mathrm{st}}\exp(\kappa_{\mathrm{st}}/4)}t+1\right)

\displaystyle\geq\frac{\eta\sigma_{p}^{2}d}{8mn_{\mathrm{st}}\exp(\kappa_{\mathrm{st}}/4)}t+1-\log\left(\frac{\eta\sigma_{p}^{2}d}{8mn_{\mathrm{st}}\exp(\kappa_{\mathrm{st}}/4)}t+1\right)

\displaystyle\geq\frac{\eta\sigma_{p}^{2}d}{16mn_{\mathrm{st}}\exp(\kappa_{\mathrm{st}}/4)}t+\frac{1}{2}

\displaystyle\geq\frac{\eta\sigma_{p}^{2}d}{16mn_{\mathrm{st}}\exp(\kappa_{\mathrm{st}}/4)}t,

(15)

where we use the inequality
\log z<\frac{z}{2}
for any
z>1
.

For any
t\in[0,T^{*}]
and
i\in[n_{\mathrm{st}}]
, by applying
(S4)
and
(S8)
, we have

\displaystyle\hat{y}_{i}f\left({\bm{W}}^{(t)},\tilde{\bm{X}}_{i}\right)

\displaystyle\geq-\frac{\kappa_{\mathrm{st}}}{4}+\frac{1}{m}\sum_{r\in[m]}\overline{\rho}_{r,i}^{(t)}

\displaystyle\geq-\frac{\kappa_{\mathrm{st}}}{4}+{x}_{t}

\displaystyle\geq-\frac{\kappa_{\mathrm{st}}}{4}+\log\left(\frac{\eta\sigma_{p}^{2}d}{16mn_{\mathrm{st}}\exp(\kappa_{\mathrm{st}}/4)}t\right)

\displaystyle=\log\left(\frac{\eta\sigma_{p}^{2}d}{16mn_{\mathrm{st}}\exp(\kappa_{\mathrm{st}}/2)}t\right)

\displaystyle\geq\log\left(\frac{\eta\sigma_{p}^{2}d}{16\lambda_{\mathrm{st}}mn_{\mathrm{st}}}t\right),

where the third inequality follows from (
C.2
) and the fourth inequality follows from (
6
). Therefore, we have

L_{\mathrm{st}}\left({\bm{W}}^{(t)}\right)\leq\log\left(1+\frac{{16\lambda_{\mathrm{st}}mn_{\mathrm{st}}}}{\eta\sigma_{p}^{2}d}\cdot t^{-1}\right)\leq\frac{{16\lambda_{\mathrm{st}}mn_{\mathrm{st}}}}{\eta\sigma_{p}^{2}d}\cdot t^{-1},

where the inequality follows from
\log(1+z)\leq z
for
z>0
.
If
t\geq 16\lambda_{\mathrm{st}}\eta^{-1}\varepsilon^{-1}mn_{\mathrm{st}}d^{-1}\sigma_{p}^{-2}
, then we have
L_{\mathrm{st}}\left({\bm{W}}^{(t)}\right)\leq\varepsilon
. Hence, by defining
T_{\mathrm{st}}:=\lceil 16\lambda_{\mathrm{st}}\eta^{-1}\varepsilon^{-1}mn_{\mathrm{st}}d^{-1}\sigma_{p}^{-2}\rceil
, we have the first conclusion.

C.3
Test Error

In this subsection, we prove the second part of our conclusion. All the arguments in this subsection are under Condition
3.1
and the event
E_{\mathrm{st}}
.

Define
{\bm{v}}^{(1)}
,
{\bm{v}}^{(2)}
, and
{\bm{\xi}}
as the signal vectors and the noise vector in the test data
({\bm{X}},y)
, respectively. We fix an arbitrary iteration
t\in[T_{\mathrm{st}},T^{*}]
. From the choice of iteration
t
and (
C.2
), for any
i\in[n_{\mathrm{st}}]
, we have

\log\left(\varepsilon^{-1}\right)\leq\log\left(\frac{\eta\sigma_{p}^{2}d}{16\lambda_{\mathrm{st}}mn_{\mathrm{st}}}t\right)\leq\log\left(\frac{\eta\sigma_{p}^{2}d}{16mn_{\mathrm{st}}\exp(\kappa_{\mathrm{st}}/2)}t\right)\leq x_{t}\leq\frac{1}{m}\sum_{r\in[m]}\overline{\rho}_{r,i}^{(t)}.

(16)

C.3.1
Test Error Upper Bound

We define a function
h:S\rightarrow\mathbb{R}
as
h({\bm{z}}):=\frac{1}{m}\sum_{r\in[m]}\sigma\left(\left\langle{\bm{w}}_{-y,r}^{(t)},{\bm{z}}\right\rangle\right)
for any
{\mathbf{z}}\in S
. It plays a crucial role when we prove the upper bounds on test error. We have

\mathbb{E}[h({\bm{\xi}})]=\frac{1}{m}\mathbb{E}_{{\mathbf{z}}_{1},\dots,{\mathbf{z}}_{m}}\left[\sum_{r\in[m]}\sigma({\mathbf{z}}_{r})\right]=\frac{1}{2m}\mathbb{E}_{{\mathbf{z}}_{1},\dots,{\mathbf{z}}_{m}}\left[\sum_{r\in[m]}\left|{\mathbf{z}}_{r}\right|\right]=\frac{\sigma_{p}}{\sqrt{2\pi}m}\sum_{r\in[m]}\left\|\Pi_{S}{\bm{w}}^{(t)}_{-y,r}\right\|,

where
{\mathbf{z}}_{r}\sim{\mathcal{N}}\left(0,\sigma_{p}^{2}\left\|\Pi_{S}{\bm{w}}^{(t)}_{-y,r}\right\|^{2}\right)
for each
r\in[m]
. Also, for any
{\bm{z}}_{1},{\bm{z}}_{2}\in S
, we have

\displaystyle\left|h({\bm{z}}_{1})-h({\bm{z}}_{2})\right|

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{m}\sum_{r\in[m]}\left|\sigma\left(\left\langle{\bm{w}}_{-y,r}^{(t)},{\bm{z}}_{1}\right\rangle\right)-\sigma\left(\left\langle{\bm{w}}_{-y,r}^{(t)},{\bm{z}}_{2}\right\rangle\right)\right|

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{m}\sum_{r\in[m]}\left|\left\langle{\bm{w}}_{-y,r}^{(t)},{\bm{z}}_{1}\right\rangle-\left\langle{\bm{w}}_{-y,r}^{(t)},{\bm{z}}_{2}\right\rangle\right|

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{m}\sum_{r\in[m]}\left|\left\langle\Pi_{S}{\bm{w}}_{-y,r}^{(t)},{\bm{z}}_{1}\right\rangle-\left\langle\Pi_{S}{\bm{w}}_{-y,r}^{(t)},{\bm{z}}_{2}\right\rangle\right|

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{m}\sum_{r\in[m]}\left\|\Pi_{S}{\bm{w}}_{-y,r}^{(t)}\right\|\left\|{\bm{z}}_{1}-{\bm{z}}_{2}\right\|.

Hence,
h
is
\frac{1}{m}\sum_{r\in[m]}\left\|\Pi_{S}{\bm{w}}_{-y,r}^{(t)}\right\|
-Lipschitz.

The following lemma characterizes
\sum_{r\in[m]}\left\|\Pi_{S}{\bm{w}}_{-y,r}^{(t)}\right\|
’s which is related to key properties of
h
.

Lemma C.4
.

For any
s\in\{\pm 1\}
, it holds that

\sum_{r\in[m]}\left\|\Pi_{S}{\bm{w}}_{s,r}^{(t)}\right\|\leq 20\sigma_{p}^{-1}d^{-\frac{1}{2}}\left(\sum_{i\in[n_{\mathrm{st}}]}\left(\sum_{r\in[m]}\overline{\rho}_{r,i}^{(t)}\right)^{2}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}.

Proof of Lemma
C.4
.

From triangular inequality and the event
E_{\mathrm{st}}
, for each
r\in[m]
, we have

\left\|\Pi_{S}{\bm{w}}_{s,r}^{(t)}\right\|\leq\left\|\Pi_{S}{\bm{w}}_{s,r}^{(0)}\right\|+\left\|\sum_{i\in[n_{\mathrm{st}}]}\rho_{s,r,i}^{(t)}\tilde{\bm{\xi}}_{i}\lVert\tilde{\bm{\xi}}_{i}\rVert^{-2}\right\|\leq\sqrt{2}\sigma_{0}d^{\frac{1}{2}}+\left\|\sum_{i\in[n_{\mathrm{st}}]}\rho_{s,r,i}^{(t)}\tilde{\bm{\xi}}_{i}\lVert\tilde{\bm{\xi}}_{i}\rVert^{-2}\right\|.

In addition, we have

\displaystyle\quad\left\|\sum_{i\in[n_{\mathrm{st}}]}\rho_{s,r,i}^{(t)},\tilde{\bm{\xi}}_{i}\lVert\tilde{\bm{\xi}}_{i}\rVert^{-2}\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle=\sum_{i\in[n_{\mathrm{st}}]}\left(\rho_{s,r,i}^{(t)}\right)^{2}\lVert\tilde{\bm{\xi}}_{i}\rVert^{-2}+\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}i,j\in[n_{\mathrm{st}}]\\
i\neq j\end{subarray}}\rho_{s,r,i}^{(t)}\rho_{s,r,j}^{(t)}\langle\tilde{\bm{\xi}}_{i},\tilde{\bm{\xi}}_{j}\rangle\lVert\tilde{\bm{\xi}}_{i}\rVert^{-2}\lVert\tilde{\bm{\xi}}_{j}\rVert^{-2}

\displaystyle\leq 2\sigma_{p}^{-2}d^{-1}\sum_{i\in[n_{\mathrm{st}}]}\left(\rho_{s,r,i}^{(t)}\right)^{2}+2\beta_{\mathrm{st}}n_{\mathrm{st}}^{-1}\sigma_{p}^{-2}d^{-1}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}i,j\in[n_{\mathrm{st}}]\\
i\neq j\end{subarray}}\left|\rho_{s,r,i}^{(t)}\right|\left|\rho_{s,r,j}^{(t)}\right|

\displaystyle\leq 2\sigma_{p}^{-2}d^{-1}\sum_{i\in[n_{\mathrm{st}}]}\left(\rho_{s,r,i}^{(t)}\right)^{2}+\beta_{\mathrm{st}}n_{\mathrm{st}}^{-1}\sigma_{p}^{-2}d^{-1}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}i,j\in[n_{\mathrm{st}}]\\
i\neq j\end{subarray}}\frac{\left(\rho_{s,r,i}^{(t)}\right)^{2}+\left(\rho_{s,r,j}^{(t)}\right)^{2}}{2},

\displaystyle\leq 4\sigma_{p}^{-2}d^{-1}\sum_{i\in[n_{\mathrm{st}}]}\left(\rho_{s,r,i}^{(t)}\right)^{2}

where the first inequality follows from (
8
) and the second inequality follows from AM-GM inequality, and the last inequality follows from (
6
).
From the Cauchy-Schwarz inequality, we have

\displaystyle\sum_{r\in[m]}\left\|\sum_{i\in[n_{\mathrm{st}}]}\rho_{s,r,i}^{(t)}\tilde{\bm{\xi}}_{i}\lVert\tilde{\bm{\xi}}_{i}\rVert^{-2}\right\|

\displaystyle\leq 2\sigma_{p}^{-1}d^{-\frac{1}{2}}\sum_{r\in[m]}\left(\sum_{i\in[n_{\mathrm{st}}]}\left(\rho_{s,r,i}^{(t)}\right)^{2}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}

\displaystyle\leq 2m^{\frac{1}{2}}\sigma_{p}^{-1}d^{-\frac{1}{2}}\left(\sum_{r\in[m]}\sum_{i\in[n_{\mathrm{st}}]}\left(\rho_{s,r,i}^{(t)}\right)^{2}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}.

In addition, from
(S1)
with iteration
t
, we have

\displaystyle\sum_{i\in[n_{\mathrm{st}}]}\sum_{r\in[m]}\left(\rho_{s,r,i}^{(t)}\right)^{2}

\displaystyle=\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}i\in[n_{\mathrm{st}}]\\
\hat{y}_{i}=s\end{subarray}}\sum_{r\in[m]}\left(\overline{\rho}_{r,i}^{(t)}\right)^{2}+\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}i\in[n_{\mathrm{st}}]\\
\hat{y}_{i}=-s\end{subarray}}\sum_{r\in[m]}\left(\underline{\rho}_{r,i}^{(t)}\right)^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}i\in[n_{\mathrm{st}}]\\
\hat{y}_{i}=s\end{subarray}}\sum_{r\in[m]}\left(\overline{\rho}_{r,i}^{(t)}\right)^{2}+(\alpha_{\mathrm{st}}+5\beta_{\mathrm{st}}\log T^{*})^{2}mn_{\mathrm{st}}.

For any
i\in[n_{\mathrm{st}}]
such that
\hat{y}_{i}=s
, we have

\sum_{r\in[m]}\left(\overline{\rho}_{r,i}^{(t)}\right)^{2}\leq m\left(\max_{r\in[m]}\overline{\rho}_{r,i}^{(t)}\right)^{2}\leq 16m^{-1}\left(\sum_{r\in[m]}\overline{\rho}_{r,i}^{(t)}\right)^{2},

where the last inequality follows from
(S7)
and (
9
). Therefore, we have

\displaystyle\sum_{i\in[n_{\mathrm{st}}]}\sum_{r\in[m]}\left(\rho_{s,r,i}^{(t)}\right)^{2}

\displaystyle\leq 16m^{-1}\sum_{i\in[n_{\mathrm{st}}]}\left(\sum_{r\in[m]}\overline{\rho}_{r,i}^{(t)}\right)^{2}+(\alpha_{\mathrm{st}}+5\beta_{\mathrm{st}}\log T^{*})^{2}mn_{\mathrm{st}}

\displaystyle\leq 25m^{-1}\sum_{i\in[n_{\mathrm{st}}]}\left(\sum_{r\in[m]}\overline{\rho}_{r,i}^{(t)}\right)^{2},

where the last inequality follows from (
16
) and (
6
).
We conclude

\displaystyle\quad\sum_{r\in[m]}\left\|\Pi_{S}{\bm{w}}_{s,r}^{(t)}\right\|

\displaystyle\leq\sqrt{2}m\sigma_{0}d^{\frac{1}{2}}+10\sigma_{p}^{-1}d^{-\frac{1}{2}}\left(\sum_{i\in[n_{\mathrm{st}}]}\left(\sum_{r\in[m]}\overline{\rho}_{r,i}^{(t)}\right)^{2}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}

\displaystyle\leq 20\sigma_{p}^{-1}d^{-\frac{1}{2}}\left(\sum_{i\in[n_{\mathrm{st}}]}\left(\sum_{r\in[m]}\overline{\rho}_{r,i}^{(t)}\right)^{2}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}},

where the second inequality follows from (
16
), (
6
), and
(C3)
.
∎

By Theorem 5.2.2 in
Vershynin 2018
, for any
z>0
, it holds that

\mathbb{P}[h({\bm{\xi}})-\mathbb{E}[h({\bm{\xi}})]\geq z]\leq\exp\left(-\frac{cz^{2}}{\sigma_{p}^{2}\left\|h\right\|_{\mathrm{Lip}}^{2}}\right)

where
c
is a universal constant and
\left\|\cdot\right\|_{\mathrm{Lip}}
denotes the best Lipschitz constant. Combining with Lemma
C.4
, we have

\mathbb{P}[h({\bm{\xi}})-\mathbb{E}[h({\bm{\xi}})]\geq z]\leq\exp\left(-\frac{cm^{2}d}{400\sum_{i\in[n_{\mathrm{st}}]}\left(\sum_{r\in[m]}\overline{\rho}_{r,i}^{(t)}\right)^{2}}z^{2}\right).

(17)

Now, we characterize the test error. First, we consider the case
({\bm{X}},y)\in{\mathcal{S}}_{\mathrm{e}}\cup{\mathcal{S}}_{\mathrm{b}}
. We have

\displaystyle\quad yf_{\mathrm{st}}\left({\bm{W}}^{(t)},{\bm{X}}\right)

\displaystyle=F_{y}\left({\bm{W}}_{y}^{(t)},{\bm{X}}\right)-F_{-y}\left({\bm{W}}_{-y}^{(t)},{\bm{X}}\right)

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{m}\sum_{l\in[2]}\sum_{r\in[m]}\sigma\left(\left\langle{\bm{w}}_{y,r}^{(t)},{\bm{v}}^{(l)}\right\rangle\right)+\frac{1}{m}\sum_{r\in[m]}\sigma\left(\left\langle{\bm{w}}_{y,r}^{(t)},{\bm{\xi}}\right\rangle\right)

\displaystyle\quad-\frac{1}{m}\sum_{l\in[2]}\sum_{r\in[m]}\sigma\left(\left\langle{\bm{w}}_{-y,r}^{(t)},{\bm{v}}^{(l)}\right\rangle\right)-\frac{1}{m}\sum_{r\in[m]}\sigma\left(\left\langle{\bm{w}}_{-y,r}^{(t)},{\bm{\xi}}\right\rangle\right)

\displaystyle\geq-\frac{1}{m}\sum_{r\in[m]}\sigma\left(\left\langle{\bm{w}}_{-y,r}^{(t)},{\bm{\xi}}\right\rangle\right)+\sum_{r\in[m]}\sigma\left(\left\langle{\bm{w}}_{y,r}^{(t)},{\bm{\mu}}_{y}\right\rangle\right)-\frac{1}{m}\sum_{l\in[2]}\sum_{r\in[m]}\sigma\left(\left\langle{\bm{w}}_{-y,r}^{(t)},{\bm{v}}^{(l)}\right\rangle\right)

\displaystyle\geq-\frac{1}{m}\sum_{r\in[m]}\sigma\left(\left\langle{\bm{w}}_{-y,r}^{(t)},{\bm{\xi}}\right\rangle\right)+\frac{1}{m}\sum_{r\in[m]}\overline{M}_{y,r}^{(t)}-2(2\alpha_{\mathrm{st}}+\beta_{\mathrm{st}})

\displaystyle=-h({\bm{\xi}})+\frac{1}{m}\sum_{r\in[m]}\overline{M}_{y,r}^{(t)}-2(2\alpha_{\mathrm{st}}+\beta_{\mathrm{st}}),

where the second inequality follows from (
7
) and
(S2)
. From
(S3)
,
(S8)
, and (
16
), we have

\displaystyle\frac{1}{m}\sum_{r\in[m]}\overline{M}_{y,r}^{(t)}

\displaystyle\geq\frac{1}{12\lambda_{\mathrm{st}}}n_{\bm{\mu}}\mathrm{SNR}_{\bm{\mu}}^{2}\cdot x_{t}

\displaystyle\geq\frac{1}{12\lambda_{\mathrm{st}}}n_{\bm{\mu}}\mathrm{SNR}_{\bm{\mu}}^{2}\log\left(\varepsilon^{-1}\right)

\displaystyle\geq 4(2\alpha_{\mathrm{st}}+\beta_{\mathrm{st}}),

where the last inequality follows from (
6
).
Therefore, we have

yf_{\mathrm{st}}\left({\bm{W}}^{(t)},{\bm{X}}\right)\geq-h({\bm{\xi}})+\frac{1}{2m}\sum_{r\in[m]}\overline{M}_{y,r}^{(t)}

and thus

\mathbb{P}\left[yf_{\mathrm{st}}\left({\bm{W}}^{(t)},{\bm{X}}\right)<0\,\middle|\,({\bm{X}},y)\in{\mathcal{S}}_{\mathrm{e}}\cup{\mathcal{S}}_{\mathrm{b}}\right]\leq\mathbb{P}\left[h({\bm{\xi}})>\frac{1}{2m}\sum_{r\in[m]}\overline{M}_{y,r}^{(t)}\right].

From Lemma
C.4
, we have

\displaystyle\quad\frac{1}{2m}\sum_{r\in[m]}\overline{M}_{y,r}^{(t)}-\mathbb{E}[h({\bm{\xi}})]

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{2m}\sum_{r\in[m]}\overline{M}_{y,r}^{(t)}-\frac{\sigma_{p}}{\sqrt{2\pi}m}\sum_{r\in[m]}\left\|\Pi_{S}{\bm{w}}_{-y,r}^{(t)}\right\|

\displaystyle\geq\frac{n_{\bm{\mu}}\mathrm{SNR}_{{\bm{\mu}}}^{2}}{24\lambda_{\mathrm{st}}mn_{\mathrm{st}}^{\frac{1}{2}}}\left(\sum_{i\in[n_{\mathrm{st}}]}\left(\sum_{r\in[m]}\overline{\rho}_{r,i}^{(t)}\right)^{2}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}-\frac{20}{\sqrt{2\pi}md^{\frac{1}{2}}}\left(\sum_{i\in[n_{\mathrm{st}}]}\left(\sum_{r\in[m]}\overline{\rho}_{r,i}^{(t)}\right)^{2}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}

\displaystyle\geq\frac{n_{\bm{\mu}}\mathrm{SNR}_{{\bm{\mu}}}^{2}}{48\lambda_{\mathrm{st}}mn_{\mathrm{st}}^{\frac{1}{2}}}\left(\sum_{i\in[n_{\mathrm{st}}]}\left(\sum_{r\in[m]}\overline{\rho}_{r,i}^{(t)}\right)^{2}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}

where the last inequality follows from the condition
n_{\mathrm{st}}p_{\mathrm{b}}^{2}\left\|{\bm{\nu}}\right\|^{4}\geq C_{2}\sigma_{p}^{4}d
and
(C5)
.

From (
17
), we have

\displaystyle\mathbb{P}\left[h({\bm{\xi}})>\frac{1}{2m}\sum_{r\in[m]}\overline{M}_{s,r}^{(t)}\right]

\displaystyle=\mathbb{P}\left[h({\bm{\xi}})-\mathbb{E}[h({\bm{\xi}})]>\frac{1}{2m}\sum_{r\in[m]}\overline{M}_{y,r}^{(t)}-\mathbb{E}[h({\bm{\xi}})]\right]

\displaystyle\leq\mathbb{P}\left[h({\bm{\xi}})-\mathbb{E}[h({\bm{\xi}})]>\frac{n_{\bm{\mu}}\mathrm{SNR}_{{\bm{\mu}}}^{2}}{48\lambda_{\mathrm{st}}mn_{\mathrm{st}}^{\frac{1}{2}}}\left(\sum_{i\in[n_{\mathrm{st}}]}\left(\sum_{r\in[m]}\overline{\rho}_{r,i}^{(t)}\right)^{2}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}\right]

\displaystyle\leq\exp\left(-\frac{cn_{\bm{\mu}}^{2}\left\|{\bm{\mu}}\right\|^{4}}{400\cdot 48^{2}\lambda_{\mathrm{st}}^{2}\cdot n_{\mathrm{st}}\sigma_{p}^{4}d}\right)

\displaystyle\leq\exp\left(-\frac{n_{\mathrm{st}}(2p_{\mathrm{e}}+p_{\mathrm{b}})^{2}\left\|{\bm{\mu}}\right\|^{4}}{C_{3}\sigma_{p}^{4}d}\right),

with some constant
C_{3}>0
.

Using a similar argument, we can prove the upper bound on test error for the case
({\bm{X}},y)\in{\mathcal{S}}_{\mathrm{h}}
. In this case, we have

\displaystyle\quad yf_{\mathrm{st}}\left({\bm{W}}^{(t)},{\bm{X}}\right)

\displaystyle=F_{y}\left({\bm{W}}_{y}^{(t)},{\bm{X}}\right)-F_{-y}\left({\bm{W}}_{-y}^{(t)},{\bm{X}}\right)

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{m}\sum_{l\in[2]}\sum_{r\in[m]}\sigma\left(\left\langle{\bm{w}}_{y,r}^{(t)},{\bm{v}}^{(l)}\right\rangle\right)+\frac{1}{m}\sum_{r\in[m]}\sigma\left(\left\langle{\bm{w}}_{y,r}^{(t)},{\bm{\xi}}\right\rangle\right)

\displaystyle\quad-\frac{1}{m}\sum_{l\in[2]}\sum_{r\in[m]}\sigma\left(\left\langle{\bm{w}}_{-y,r}^{(t)},{\bm{v}}^{(l)}\right\rangle\right)-\frac{1}{m}\sum_{r\in[m]}\sigma\left(\left\langle{\bm{w}}_{-y,r}^{(t)},{\bm{\xi}}\right\rangle\right)

\displaystyle\geq-\frac{1}{m}\sum_{r\in[m]}\sigma\left(\left\langle{\bm{w}}_{-y,r}^{(t)},{\bm{\xi}}\right\rangle\right)

\displaystyle\quad+\frac{1}{m}\sum_{l\in[2]}\sum_{r\in[m]}\sigma\left(\left\langle{\bm{w}}_{y,r}^{(t)},{\bm{v}}^{(l)}\right\rangle\right)-\frac{1}{m}\sum_{l\in[2]}\sum_{r\in[m]}\sigma\left(\left\langle{\bm{w}}_{-y,r}^{(t)},{\bm{v}}^{(l)}\right\rangle\right)

\displaystyle\geq-\frac{1}{m}\sum_{r\in[m]}\sigma\left(\left\langle{\bm{w}}_{-y,r}^{(t)},{\bm{\xi}}\right\rangle\right)+\frac{2}{m}\min\left\{\sum_{r\in{\mathcal{A}}_{y}}\overline{N}_{y,r}^{(t)},-\sum_{r\in{\mathcal{B}}_{y}}\overline{N}_{y,r}^{(t)}\right\}-2(2\alpha_{\mathrm{st}}+\beta_{\mathrm{st}})

\displaystyle=-h({\bm{\xi}})+\frac{2}{m}\min\left\{\sum_{r\in{\mathcal{A}}_{y}}\overline{N}_{y,r}^{(t)},-\sum_{r\in{\mathcal{B}}_{y}}\overline{N}_{y,r}^{(t)}\right\}-2(2\alpha_{\mathrm{st}}+\beta_{\mathrm{st}})

where the first inequality follows from (
7
) and
(S2)
. From
(S3)
,
(S8)
, and (
16
), we have

\displaystyle\frac{1}{m}\sum_{r\in{\mathcal{A}}_{y}}\overline{N}_{y,r}^{(t)},-\frac{1}{m}\sum_{r\in{\mathcal{B}}_{y}}\overline{N}_{y,r}^{(t)}

\displaystyle\geq\frac{1}{12\lambda_{\mathrm{st}}}n_{\bm{\nu}}\mathrm{SNR}_{\bm{\nu}}^{2}\cdot x_{t}

\displaystyle\geq\frac{1}{12\lambda_{\mathrm{st}}}n_{\bm{\nu}}\mathrm{SNR}_{\bm{\nu}}^{2}\cdot\log\left(\frac{\eta\sigma_{p}^{2}d}{16mn_{\mathrm{st}}\exp(\kappa_{\mathrm{st}}/4)}t\right)

\displaystyle\geq\frac{1}{12\lambda_{\mathrm{st}}}n_{\bm{\nu}}\mathrm{SNR}_{\bm{\nu}}^{2}\log\left(\varepsilon^{-1}\right)

\displaystyle\geq 4(2\alpha_{\mathrm{st}}+\beta_{\mathrm{st}}),

(18)

where the last inequality follows from (
6
).
Therefore, we have

yf_{\mathrm{st}}\left({\bm{W}}^{(t)},{\bm{X}}\right)\geq-h({\bm{\xi}})+\frac{1}{m}\min\left\{\sum_{r\in{\mathcal{A}}_{y}}\overline{N}_{y,r}^{(t)},-\sum_{r\in{\mathcal{B}}_{y}}\overline{N}_{y,r}^{(t)}\right\}

and thus

\mathbb{P}\left[yf_{\mathrm{st}}\left({\bm{W}}^{(t)},{\bm{X}}\right)<0\,\middle|\,({\bm{X}},y)\in{\mathcal{S}}_{\mathrm{h}}\right]\leq\mathbb{P}\left[h({\bm{\xi}})>\frac{1}{m}\min\left\{\sum_{r\in{\mathcal{A}}_{y}}\overline{N}_{y,r}^{(t)},-\sum_{r\in{\mathcal{B}}_{y}}\overline{N}_{y,r}^{(t)}\right\}\right].

From Lemma
C.4
and Condition
3.1
, we have

\displaystyle\quad\frac{1}{m}\min\left\{\sum_{r\in{\mathcal{A}}_{y}}\overline{N}_{y,r}^{(t)},-\sum_{r\in{\mathcal{B}}_{y}}\overline{N}_{y,r}^{(t)}\right\}-\mathbb{E}[h({\bm{\xi}})]

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{m}\min\left\{\sum_{r\in{\mathcal{A}}_{y}}\overline{N}_{y,r}^{(t)},-\sum_{r\in{\mathcal{B}}_{y}}\overline{N}_{y,r}^{(t)}\right\}-\frac{\sigma_{p}}{\sqrt{2\pi}m}\sum_{r\in[m]}\left\|\Pi_{S}{\bm{w}}_{-y,r}^{(t)}\right\|

\displaystyle\geq\frac{1}{12\lambda_{\mathrm{st}}mn_{\mathrm{st}}}n_{\bm{\nu}}\mathrm{SNR}_{{\bm{\nu}}}^{2}\cdot\sum_{i\in[n_{\mathrm{st}}]}\sum_{r\in[m]}\overline{\rho}_{r,i}^{(t)}-\frac{3}{\sqrt{2\pi}mn_{\mathrm{st}}^{\frac{1}{2}}d^{\frac{1}{2}}}\sum_{i\in[n_{\mathrm{st}}]}\sum_{r\in[m]}\overline{\rho}_{r,i}^{(t)}

\displaystyle\geq\frac{1}{24\lambda_{\mathrm{st}}mn_{\mathrm{st}}}n_{\bm{\nu}}\mathrm{SNR}_{{\bm{\nu}}}^{2}\sum_{i\in[n_{\mathrm{st}}]}\sum_{r\in[m]}\overline{\rho}_{r,i}^{(t)},

where the last inequality follows from the condition given in the statement.
From (
17
), we have

\displaystyle\quad\mathbb{P}\left[h({\bm{\xi}})>\frac{1}{m}\min\left\{\sum_{r\in{\mathcal{A}}_{y}}\overline{N}_{y,r}^{(t)},-\sum_{r\in{\mathcal{B}}_{y}}\overline{N}_{y,r}^{(t)}\right\}\right]

\displaystyle=\mathbb{P}\left[h({\bm{\xi}})-\mathbb{E}[h({\bm{\xi}})]>\frac{1}{m}\min\left\{\sum_{r\in{\mathcal{A}}_{y}}\overline{N}_{y,r}^{(t)},-\sum_{r\in{\mathcal{B}}_{y}}\overline{N}_{y,r}^{(t)}\right\}-\mathbb{E}[h({\bm{\xi}})]\right]

\displaystyle\leq\mathbb{P}\left[h({\bm{\xi}})-\mathbb{E}[h({\bm{\xi}})]>\frac{1}{24\lambda_{\mathrm{st}}mn_{\mathrm{st}}}n_{\bm{\nu}}\mathrm{SNR}_{{\bm{\nu}}}^{2}\sum_{i\in[n_{\mathrm{st}}]}\sum_{r\in[m]}\overline{\rho}_{r,i}^{(t)}\right]

\displaystyle\leq\exp\left(-\frac{cn_{\bm{\nu}}^{2}\left\|{\bm{\nu}}\right\|^{4}}{9\cdot 24^{2}\lambda_{\mathrm{st}}^{2}\cdot n_{\mathrm{st}}\sigma_{p}^{4}d}\right)

\displaystyle\leq\exp\left(-\frac{n_{\mathrm{st}}p_{\mathrm{b}}^{2}\left\|{\bm{\nu}}\right\|^{4}}{C_{3}\sigma_{p}^{4}d}\right),

with some constant
C_{3}>0
.

C.3.2
Test Error Lower Bound

We consider the case
({\bm{X}},y)\in{\mathcal{S}}_{\mathrm{h}}
. Define
g:S\rightarrow\mathbb{R}
as
g({\bm{z}}):=\frac{1}{m}\sum_{r\in[m]}\sigma\left(\left\langle{\bm{w}}_{1,r}^{(t)},{\bm{z}}\right\rangle\right)-\frac{1}{m}\sum_{r\in[m]}\sigma\left(\left\langle{\bm{w}}_{-1,r}^{(t)},{\bm{z}}\right\rangle\right)
for any
{\bm{z}}\in S
. Then, we have

\displaystyle\quad yf_{\mathrm{st}}\left({\bm{W}}^{(t)},{\bm{X}}\right)

\displaystyle=F_{y}\left({\bm{W}}_{y}^{(t)},{\bm{X}}\right)-F_{-y}\left({\bm{W}}_{-y}^{(t)},{\bm{X}}\right)

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{m}\sum_{l\in[2]}\sum_{r\in[m]}\sigma\left(\left\langle{\bm{w}}_{y,r}^{(t)},{\bm{v}}^{(l)}\right\rangle\right)+\frac{1}{m}\sum_{r\in[m]}\sigma\left(\left\langle{\bm{w}}_{y,r}^{(t)},{\bm{\xi}}\right\rangle\right)

\displaystyle\quad-\frac{1}{m}\sum_{l\in[2]}\sum_{r\in[m]}\sigma\left(\left\langle{\bm{w}}_{-y,r}^{(t)},{\bm{v}}^{(l)}\right\rangle\right)-\frac{1}{m}\sum_{r\in[m]}\sigma\left(\left\langle{\bm{w}}_{-y,r}^{(t)},{\bm{\xi}}\right\rangle\right)

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{m}\sum_{r\in[m]}\sigma\left(\left\langle{\bm{w}}_{y,r}^{(t)},{\bm{\xi}}\right\rangle\right)-\frac{1}{m}\sum_{r\in[m]}\sigma\left(\left\langle{\bm{w}}_{-y,r}^{(t)},{\bm{\xi}}\right\rangle\right)+\frac{1}{m}\sum_{l\in[2]}\sum_{r\in[m]}\sigma\left(\left\langle{\bm{w}}_{y,r}^{(t)},{\bm{v}}^{(l)}\right\rangle\right)

\displaystyle\leq yg({\bm{\xi}})+\frac{2}{m}\max\left\{\sum_{r\in{\mathcal{A}}_{y}}\overline{N}_{y,r}^{(t)},-\sum_{r\in{\mathcal{B}}_{y}}\overline{N}_{y,r}^{(t)}\right\}+2\alpha_{\mathrm{st}}

\displaystyle\leq yg({\bm{\xi}})+\frac{3}{m}\max\left\{\sum_{r\in{\mathcal{A}}_{y}}\overline{N}_{y,r}^{(t)},-\sum_{r\in{\mathcal{B}}_{y}}\overline{N}_{y,r}^{(t)}\right\}

\displaystyle\leq yg({\bm{\xi}})+\frac{3}{m}\max_{s\in\{\pm 1\}}\left\{\sum_{r\in{\mathcal{A}}_{s}}\overline{N}_{s,r}^{(t)},-\sum_{r\in{\mathcal{B}}_{s}}\overline{N}_{s,r}^{(t)}\right\},

where the second inequality follows from (
C.3.1
). Therefore, we have

\mathbb{P}\left[yf_{\mathrm{st}}\left({\bm{W}}^{(t)},{\bm{X}}\right)\,\middle|\,({\bm{X}},y)\in{\mathcal{S}}_{\mathrm{h}}\right]\geq\frac{1}{2}\mathbb{P}\left[|g({\bm{\xi}})|\geq\frac{3}{m}\max_{s\in\{\pm 1\}}\left\{\sum_{r\in{\mathcal{A}}_{s}}\overline{N}_{s,r}^{(t)},-\sum_{r\in{\mathcal{B}}_{s}}\overline{N}_{s,r}^{(t)}\right\}\right].

We define the set

\bm{\Omega}:=\left\{{\bm{z}}\in S:\left|g({\bm{z}})\right|\geq\frac{3}{m}\max_{s\in\{\pm 1\}}\left\{\sum_{r\in{\mathcal{A}}_{s}}\overline{N}_{s,r}^{(t)},-\sum_{r\in{\mathcal{B}}_{s}}\overline{N}_{s,r}^{(t)}\right\}\right\}.

We immediately obtain
\mathbb{P}\left[yf_{\mathrm{st}}\left({\bm{W}}^{(t)},{\bm{X}}\right)\,\middle|\,({\bm{X}},y)\in{\mathcal{S}}_{\mathrm{h}}\right]\geq\frac{1}{2}\mathbb{P}[{\bm{\xi}}\in\bm{\Omega}]
and thus we will characterize
\mathbb{P}[{\bm{\xi}}\in\bm{\Omega}]
. Denote
{\bm{\zeta}}=C_{6}p_{\mathrm{b}}\mathrm{SNR}_{\bm{\nu}}^{2}\cdot\sum\limits_{\begin{subarray}{c}i\in[n_{\mathrm{st}}]\\
\hat{y}_{i}=1\end{subarray}}\tilde{\bm{\xi}}_{i}
, where
C_{6}>0
is some small constant. Then, we have

\displaystyle\left\|{\bm{\zeta}}\right\|

\displaystyle\leq C_{6}p_{\mathrm{b}}\mathrm{SNR}_{\bm{\nu}}^{2}\left(\sum_{i\in[n_{\mathrm{st}}]}\lVert\tilde{\bm{\xi}}_{i}\rVert^{2}+\sum_{i\in[n_{\mathrm{st}}]}\sum_{j\in[n_{\mathrm{st}}]\setminus\{i\}}\left|\langle\tilde{\bm{\xi}}_{i},\tilde{\bm{\xi}}_{j}\rangle\right|\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}

\displaystyle\leq C_{6}p_{\mathrm{b}}\mathrm{SNR}_{\bm{\nu}}^{2}\sqrt{\frac{3(1+\beta_{\mathrm{st}})n_{\mathrm{st}}\sigma_{p}^{2}d}{2}}

\displaystyle=\sqrt{\frac{2C_{6}^{2}n_{\mathrm{st}}p_{\mathrm{b}}^{2}\left\|{\bm{\nu}}\right\|^{4}}{\sigma_{p}^{2}d}}

\displaystyle\leq 0.02\sigma_{p},

(19)

where the first inequality follows from (
8
) and the last follows from the statement condition
n_{\mathrm{st}}p_{\mathrm{b}}^{2}\left\|{\bm{\nu}}\right\|^{4}\leq C_{4}\sigma_{p}^{4}d
and the small choice of
C_{6}
. Also, for any
r\in[m]
, we have

\displaystyle\quad\sigma\left(\left\langle{\bm{w}}_{1,r}^{(t)},{\bm{\xi}}+{\bm{\zeta}}\right\rangle\right)-\sigma\left(\left\langle{\bm{w}}_{1,r}^{(t)},{\bm{\xi}}\right\rangle\right)+\sigma\left(\left\langle{\bm{w}}_{1,r}^{(t)},-{\bm{\xi}}+{\bm{\zeta}}\right\rangle\right)-\sigma\left(\left\langle{\bm{w}}_{1,r}^{(t)},-{\bm{\xi}}\right\rangle\right)

\displaystyle\geq\mathbbm{1}\left[\left\langle{\bm{w}}_{1,r}^{(t)},{\bm{\xi}}\right\rangle>0\right]\left\langle{\bm{w}}_{1,r}^{(t)},{\bm{\zeta}}\right\rangle+\mathbbm{1}\left[\left\langle{\bm{w}}_{1,r}^{(t)},-{\bm{\xi}}\right\rangle>0\right]\left\langle{\bm{w}}_{1,r}^{(t)},{\bm{\zeta}}\right\rangle

\displaystyle=\left\langle{\bm{w}}_{1,r}^{(t)},{\bm{\zeta}}\right\rangle

\displaystyle=C_{6}p_{\mathrm{b}}\mathrm{SNR}_{\bm{\nu}}^{2}\left[\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}i\in[n_{\mathrm{st}}]\\
\hat{y}_{i}=1\end{subarray}}\overline{\rho}_{r,i}^{(t)}-\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}i\in[n_{\mathrm{st}}]\\
\hat{y}_{i}=1\end{subarray}}\sum_{j\in[n_{\mathrm{st}}]\setminus\{i\}}\rho_{1,r,j}^{(t)}\frac{\langle\tilde{\bm{\xi}}_{i},\tilde{\bm{\xi}}_{j}\rangle}{\lVert\tilde{\bm{\xi}}_{j}\rVert^{2}}+\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}i\in[n_{\mathrm{st}}]\\
\hat{y}_{i}=1\end{subarray}}\left\langle{\bm{w}}_{1,r}^{(0)},\tilde{\bm{\xi}}_{i}\right\rangle\right]

\displaystyle\geq C_{6}p_{\mathrm{b}}\mathrm{SNR}_{\bm{\nu}}^{2}\left[\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}i\in[n_{\mathrm{st}}]\\
\hat{y}_{i}=1\end{subarray}}\overline{\rho}_{r,i}^{(t)}-4\beta_{\mathrm{st}}\log T^{*}-n_{\mathrm{st}}\alpha_{\mathrm{st}}\right]

where the first inequality follows from the convexity of ReLU, and the second inequality follows from
(S1)
, (
7
), and (
8
). In addition, for any
r\in[m]
, we have

\displaystyle\quad\sigma\left(\left\langle{\bm{w}}_{-1,r}^{(t)},{\bm{\xi}}+{\bm{\zeta}}\right\rangle\right)-\sigma\left(\left\langle{\bm{w}}_{-1,r}^{(t)},{\bm{\xi}}\right\rangle\right)+\sigma\left(\left\langle{\bm{w}}_{-1,r}^{(t)},-{\bm{\xi}}+{\bm{\zeta}}\right\rangle\right)-\sigma\left(\left\langle{\bm{w}}_{-1,r}^{(t)},-{\bm{\xi}}\right\rangle\right)

\displaystyle\leq 2\left|\left\langle{\bm{w}}_{-1,r}^{(t)},{\bm{\zeta}}\right\rangle\right|

\displaystyle\leq 2\lambda\left[\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}i\in[n_{\mathrm{st}}]\\
\hat{y}_{i}=1\end{subarray}}\left|\underline{\rho}_{r,i}^{(t)}\right|+\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}i\in[n_{\mathrm{st}}]\\
\hat{y}_{i}=1\end{subarray}}\sum_{j\in[n_{\mathrm{st}}]\setminus\{i\}}\left|\rho_{-1,r,j}^{(t)}\right|\frac{\left|\langle\tilde{\bm{\xi}}_{i},\tilde{\bm{\xi}}_{j}\rangle\right|}{\lVert\tilde{\bm{\xi}}_{j}\rVert^{2}}+\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}i\in[n_{\mathrm{st}}]\\
\hat{y}_{i}=1\end{subarray}}\left|\left\langle{\bm{w}}_{-1,r}^{(0)},\tilde{\bm{\xi}}_{i}\right\rangle\right|\right]

\displaystyle\leq 2C_{6}p_{\mathrm{b}}\mathrm{SNR}_{\bm{\nu}}^{2}\big(n_{\mathrm{st}}(\alpha_{\mathrm{st}}+5\beta_{\mathrm{st}}\log T^{*})+4\beta_{\mathrm{st}}\log T^{*}+n_{\mathrm{st}}\alpha_{\mathrm{st}}\big)

\displaystyle=2C_{6}p_{\mathrm{b}}\mathrm{SNR}_{\bm{\nu}}^{2}n_{\mathrm{st}}(2\alpha_{\mathrm{st}}+9\beta_{\mathrm{st}}\log T^{*}),

where the first inequality holds since ReLU is
1
-Lipschitz and the second inequality follows from
(S1)
, (
7
), and (
8
). Therefore, we have

\displaystyle\quad g({\bm{\xi}}+{\bm{\zeta}})-g({\bm{\xi}})+g(-{\bm{\xi}}+{\bm{\zeta}})-g(-{\bm{\xi}})

\displaystyle\geq\frac{C_{6}p_{\mathrm{b}}\mathrm{SNR}_{\bm{\nu}}^{2}}{m}\left[\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}i\in[n_{\mathrm{st}}]\\
\hat{y}_{i}=1\end{subarray}}\overline{\rho}_{r,i}^{(t)}-n_{\mathrm{st}}(7\alpha_{\mathrm{st}}+12\beta_{\mathrm{st}}\log T^{*})\right]

\displaystyle\geq\frac{C_{6}p_{\mathrm{b}}\mathrm{SNR}_{\bm{\nu}}^{2}}{2m}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}i\in[n_{\mathrm{st}}]\\
\hat{y}_{i}=1\end{subarray}}\overline{\rho}_{r,i}^{(t)}

\displaystyle\geq\frac{C_{6}p_{\mathrm{b}}\mathrm{SNR}_{\bm{\nu}}^{2}}{2m}\cdot\frac{\left|{\mathcal{C}}_{{\bm{\mu}}_{1}}^{(1)}\right|+\left|{\mathcal{C}}_{{\bm{\nu}}_{1}}^{(1)}\right|+\left|{\mathcal{C}}_{-{\bm{\nu}}_{1}}^{(1)}\right|}{3\lambda_{\mathrm{st}}n_{\bm{\nu}}\mathrm{SNR}_{\bm{\nu}}^{2}}\cdot\max_{s\in\{\pm 1\}}\left\{\sum_{r\in{\mathcal{A}}_{s}}\overline{N}_{s,r}^{(t)},-\sum_{r\in{\mathcal{B}}_{s}}\overline{N}_{s,r}^{(t)}\right\}

\displaystyle\geq\frac{12}{m}\max_{s\in\{\pm 1\}}\left\{\sum_{r\in{\mathcal{A}}_{s}}\overline{N}_{s,r}^{(t)},-\sum_{r\in{\mathcal{B}}_{s}}\overline{N}_{s,r}^{(t)}\right\},

where the second inequality follows from (
16
) and (
6
), the third inequality follows from
(S3)
and the last inequality follows from the choice of
C_{6}>0
and

\left|{\mathcal{C}}_{{\bm{\mu}}_{1}}^{(1)}\right|+\left|{\mathcal{C}}_{{\bm{\nu}}_{1}}^{(1)}\right|+\left|{\mathcal{C}}_{-{\bm{\nu}}_{1}}^{(1)}\right|\geq\left(1-C_{\mathrm{st}}^{-1}\right)\cdot n_{\bm{\mu}}+2(1-C_{\mathrm{st}}^{-1})n_{\bm{\nu}}=\frac{\left(1-C_{\mathrm{st}}^{-1}\right)(p_{\mathrm{e}}+p_{\mathrm{b}})n_{\mathrm{st}}}{2}\geq\frac{n_{\mathrm{st}}}{8}.

By the pigeonhole principle, it implies that at least one of
{\bm{\xi}},-{\bm{\xi}},{\bm{\xi}}+{\bm{\zeta}},-{\bm{\xi}}+{\bm{\zeta}}
belongs to
\bm{\Omega}
. Hence,

\mathbb{P}[{\bm{\xi}}\in\bm{\Omega}]+\mathbb{P}[-{\bm{\xi}}\in\bm{\Omega}]+\mathbb{P}[{\bm{\xi}}+{\bm{\zeta}}\in\bm{\Omega}]+\mathbb{P}[-{\bm{\xi}}+{\bm{\zeta}}\in\bm{\Omega}]\geq 1.

Also, from symmetry, we have
\mathbb{P}[{\bm{\xi}}\in\bm{\Omega}]=\mathbb{P}[-{\bm{\xi}}\in\bm{\Omega}]
and
\mathbb{P}[-{\bm{\xi}}+{\bm{\zeta}}\in\bm{\Omega}]=\mathbb{P}[{\bm{\xi}}-{\bm{\zeta}}\in\bm{\Omega}]
. The following lemma allows us to relate the probability
\mathbb{P}[{\bm{\xi}}\in\bm{\Omega}]
to the probabilities
\mathbb{P}[{\bm{\xi}}\pm{\bm{\zeta}}\in\bm{\Omega}]
.

Lemma C.5

(Direct from Proposition 2.1 in
Devroye et al. 2018
)
.

For any
{\bm{v}}\in S
the total variation distance
\mathrm{TV}(\cdot,\cdot)
between
{\mathcal{N}}(0,\sigma_{p}^{2}{\bm{\Lambda}})
and
{\mathcal{N}}({\bm{v}},\sigma_{p}^{2}{\bm{\Lambda}})
is smaller than
\frac{\left\|{\bm{v}}\right\|}{2\sigma_{p}}
.

By Lemma
C.5
and (
C.3.2
), we have

\left|\mathbb{P}[{\bm{\xi}}\in\Omega]-\mathbb{P}[{\bm{\xi}}\in\Omega\pm{\bm{\zeta}}]\right|\leq\mathrm{TV}\left({\mathcal{N}}({\bm{0}},\sigma_{p}^{2}{\bm{\Lambda}}),{\mathcal{N}}(\pm{\bm{\zeta}},\sigma_{p}^{2}{\bm{\Lambda}})\right)\leq\frac{\left\|{\bm{\zeta}}\right\|}{2\sigma_{p}}\leq 0.01.

Therefore, we have

1\leq\mathbb{P}[{\bm{\xi}}\in\bm{\Omega}]+\mathbb{P}[-{\bm{\xi}}\in\bm{\Omega}]+\mathbb{P}[{\bm{\xi}}+{\bm{\zeta}}\in\bm{\Omega}]+\mathbb{P}[-{\bm{\xi}}+{\bm{\zeta}}\in\bm{\Omega}]\leq 4\mathbb{P}[{\bm{\xi}}\in\bm{\Omega}]+0.02

and thus
\mathbb{P}[{\bm{\xi}}\in\bm{\Omega}]\geq 0.24
. We conclude that

\mathbb{P}\left[yf_{\mathrm{st}}\left({\bm{W}}^{(t)},{\bm{X}}\right)\,\middle|\,({\bm{X}},y)\in{\mathcal{S}}_{\mathrm{h}}\right]\geq 0.12.

Appendix D
Proof of Theorem
3.6

It suffices to prove the following restatements of Theorem
3.6
.

Theorem D.1

(Weak-to-Strong Training, Data-Abundant Regime)
.

Let
{\bm{W}}^{(t)}
be the iterates of the weak-to-strong training, with the weak model
f_{\mathrm{wk}}({\bm{w}}^{*},\cdot)
satisfying the conclusion of Theorem
3.3
.
For any
\delta\in(0,1)
satisfying Condition
3.5
,
with probability at least
1-\delta
, there exists early stopping time
T_{\mathrm{es}}=\mathcal{O}(\eta^{-1}m(2p_{\mathrm{e}}+p_{\mathrm{b}})^{-1}\left\|{\bm{\mu}}\right\|^{-2})
such that the following statements hold:

1.

The early stopped strong model
f_{\mathrm{st}}\left({\bm{W}}^{(T_{\mathrm{es}})},\cdot\right)
perfectly fits training data having correct label (i.e.
\hat{y}_{i}=\tilde{y}_{i}
) but fails to training data with flipped label (i.e.
\hat{y}_{i}\neq\tilde{y}_{i}
). In other words, the model predicts the true label
\tilde{y}_{i}
for any training data point
\tilde{\bm{X}}_{i}
.

2.

Let
({\bm{X}},y)\sim{\mathcal{D}}
be an unseen test example, independent of the training set
\{(\tilde{\bm{X}}_{i},\hat{y}_{i})\}_{i=1}^{n_{\mathrm{st}}}
. We have

\mathbb{P}\left[yf_{\mathrm{st}}\left({\bm{W}}^{(T_{\mathrm{es}})},{\bm{X}}\right)<0\,\middle|\,({\bm{X}},y)\in{\mathcal{S}}_{\mathrm{e}}\cup{\mathcal{S}}_{\mathrm{b}}\right]\leq\exp\left(-\frac{n_{\mathrm{st}}(2p_{\mathrm{e}}+p_{\mathrm{b}})^{2}\lVert{\bm{\mu}}\rVert^{4}}{C_{5}\sigma_{p}^{4}d}\right),

and

\mathbb{P}\left[yf_{\mathrm{st}}\left({\bm{W}}^{(T_{\mathrm{es}})},{\bm{X}}\right)<0\,\middle|\,({\bm{X}},y)\in{\mathcal{S}}_{\mathrm{h}}\right]\leq\exp\left(-\frac{n_{\mathrm{st}}p_{\mathrm{b}}^{2}\lVert{\bm{\nu}}\rVert^{4}}{C_{5}\sigma_{p}^{4}d}\right),

Here,
C_{5}>0
is a constant.

For the proof, we first analyze the early training dynamics and characterize the early stopping iteration (Appendix
D.1
). We then show that the early-stopped model perfectly fits the training data with true labels (Appendix
D.2
), and finally, we establish a bound on the test error (Appendix
D.3
).

D.1
Analyzing Early Phase

First, we establish upper bounds on the noise coefficients.

Lemma D.2
.

Under Condition
3.5
and the event
E_{\mathrm{st}}
, for any
t\in\left[0,T^{*}\right],s\in\{\pm 1\},r\in[m]
and
i\in[n_{\mathrm{st}}]
, it holds that

\left|\rho_{s,r,i}^{(t)}\right|\leq\frac{3\eta\sigma_{p}^{2}d}{2mn_{\mathrm{st}}}t,\quad\left|\left\langle{\bm{w}}_{s,r}^{(t)},\tilde{\bm{\xi}}_{i}\right\rangle\right|\leq\alpha_{\mathrm{st}}+\frac{3\eta\sigma_{p}^{2}d}{mn_{\mathrm{st}}}t.

Proof of Lemma
D.2
.

We fix arbitrary
s\in\{\pm 1\},r\in[m]
and
i\in[n_{\mathrm{st}}]
. For any iteration
0<t\leq T^{*}
, we have

\left|\rho_{s,r,i}^{(t)}\right|\leq\left|\rho_{s,r,i}^{(t-1)}\right|+\frac{\eta}{mn_{\mathrm{st}}}\tilde{g}_{i}^{(t-1)}\lVert\tilde{\bm{\xi}}_{i}\rVert^{2}\leq\left|\rho_{s,r,i}^{(t-1)}\right|+\frac{3\eta\sigma_{p}^{2}d}{2mn_{\mathrm{st}}}\leq\cdots\leq\left|\rho_{s,r,i}^{(0)}\right|+\frac{3\eta\sigma_{p}^{2}d}{2mn_{\mathrm{st}}}t=\frac{3\eta\sigma_{p}^{2}d}{2mn_{\mathrm{st}}}t,

where the first inequality is due to the triangular inequality and the others are due to (
8
).
Therefore, we have

\displaystyle\left|\left\langle{\bm{w}}_{s,r}^{(t)},\tilde{\bm{\xi}}_{i}\right\rangle\right|

\displaystyle\leq\left|\left\langle{\bm{w}}_{s,r}^{(0)},\tilde{\bm{\xi}}_{i}\right\rangle\right|+\left|\rho_{s,r,i}^{(t)}\right|+\sum_{j\in[n_{\mathrm{st}}]\setminus\{i\}}\left|\rho_{s,r,j}^{(t)}\right|\frac{\left|\langle\tilde{\bm{\xi}}_{i},\tilde{\bm{\xi}}_{j}\rangle\right|}{\lVert\tilde{\bm{\xi}}_{j}\rVert^{2}}

\displaystyle\leq\alpha_{\mathrm{st}}+\frac{3\eta\sigma_{p}^{2}d}{2mn_{\mathrm{st}}}t(1+\beta_{\mathrm{st}})

\displaystyle\leq\alpha_{\mathrm{st}}+\frac{3\eta\sigma_{p}^{2}d}{mn_{\mathrm{st}}}t,

where the second inequality follows from (
7
) and (
8
).
∎

The following lemma can be inductively applied when we characterize the early phase of learning dynamics.

Lemma D.3
.

Suppose the iteration
\tau\in\left[0,\frac{mn_{\mathrm{st}}}{\eta\sigma_{p}^{2}d\log T^{*}}\right]
satisfy the following:

1.

\frac{1}{m}\sum_{r\in[m]}\overline{M}_{1,r}^{(\tau)},\frac{1}{m}\sum_{r\in[m]}\overline{M}_{-1,r}^{(\tau)}<\frac{1}{2}
.

2.

For each
s\in\{\pm 1\}
, it holds that
\overline{M}_{s,r}^{(\tau)},\left\langle{\bm{w}}_{s,r}^{(\tau)},{\bm{\mu}}_{s}\right\rangle>0
if
r\in{\mathcal{M}}_{s}
and
\overline{M}_{s,r}^{(\tau)}=0
if
r\notin{\mathcal{M}}_{s}
.

3.

For each
s\in\{\pm 1\}
, it holds that
\overline{N}_{s,r}^{(\tau)},\left\langle{\bm{w}}_{s,r}^{(\tau)},{\bm{\nu}}_{s}\right\rangle>0
if
r\in{\mathcal{A}}_{s}
and
\overline{N}_{s,r}^{(\tau)},\left\langle{\bm{w}}_{s,r}^{(\tau)},{\bm{\nu}}_{s}\right\rangle<0
if
r\in{\mathcal{B}}_{s}
.

4.

\frac{1}{60}\sum_{r\in[m]}\overline{M}_{-1,r}^{(\tau)}\leq\sum_{r\in[m]}\overline{M}_{1,r}^{(\tau)}\leq 60\sum_{r\in[m]}\overline{M}_{-1,r}^{(\tau)}
.

5.

For each
s,s^{\prime}\in\{\pm 1\}
,

\frac{p_{\mathrm{b}}\left\|{\bm{\nu}}\right\|^{2}}{120(2p_{\mathrm{e}}+p_{\mathrm{b}})\left\|{\bm{\mu}}\right\|^{2}}\sum_{r\in[m]}\overline{M}_{s^{\prime},r}^{(\tau)}\leq\sum_{r\in{\mathcal{A}}_{s}}\overline{N}_{s,r}^{(\tau)},\,-\sum_{r\in{\mathcal{B}}_{s}}\overline{N}_{s,r}^{(\tau)}\leq\sum_{r\in[m]}\overline{M}_{s^{\prime},r}^{(\tau)}

6.

For any
s\in\{\pm 1\}
and
r\in[m]
, it holds that
\left|\underline{M}_{s,r}^{(\tau)}\right|,\left|\underline{N}_{s,r}^{(\tau)}\right|\leq\alpha_{\mathrm{st}}+\beta_{\mathrm{st}}
.

Then under Condition
3.5
and the event
E_{\mathrm{st}}
, the following hold:

1.

For any
s\in\{\pm 1\}
, it holds that
\overline{M}_{s,r}^{(\tau+1)}\geq\overline{M}_{s,r}^{(\tau)}
if
r\in[m]
,
\overline{N}_{s,r}^{(\tau+1)}\geq\overline{N}_{s,r}^{(\tau)}
if
r\in{\mathcal{A}}_{s}
, and
\overline{N}_{s,r}^{(\tau+1)}\leq\overline{N}_{s,r}^{(\tau)}
if
r\in{\mathcal{B}}_{s}
.

2.

For each
s\in\{\pm 1\}
, it holds that
\overline{M}_{s,r}^{(\tau+1)},\left\langle{\bm{w}}_{s,r}^{(\tau+1)},{\bm{\mu}}_{s}\right\rangle>0
if
r\in{\mathcal{M}}_{s}
and
\overline{M}_{s,r}^{(\tau+1)}=0
if
r\notin{\mathcal{M}}_{s}
.

3.

For each
s\in\{\pm 1\}
, it holds that
\overline{N}_{s,r}^{(\tau+1)},\left\langle{\bm{w}}_{s,r}^{(\tau+1)},{\bm{\nu}}_{s}\right\rangle>0
if
r\in{\mathcal{A}}_{s}
and
\overline{N}_{s,r}^{(\tau+1)},\left\langle{\bm{w}}_{s,r}^{(\tau+1)},{\bm{\nu}}_{s}\right\rangle<0
if
r\in{\mathcal{B}}_{s}
.

4.

For each
s\in\{\pm 1\}
,

\frac{1}{m}\sum_{r\in[m]}\overline{M}_{s,r}^{(\tau+1)}\geq\frac{1}{m}\sum_{r\in[m]}\overline{M}_{s,r}^{(\tau)}+\frac{\eta(2p_{\mathrm{e}}+p_{\mathrm{b}})\left\|{\bm{\mu}}\right\|^{2}}{80m}.

5.

For each
s\in\{\pm 1\}
,

\frac{1}{m}\sum_{r\in{\mathcal{A}}_{s}}\overline{N}_{s,r}^{(\tau+1)}-\frac{1}{m}\sum_{r\in{\mathcal{A}}_{s}}\overline{N}_{s,r}^{(\tau)}\geq\frac{\eta p_{\mathrm{b}}\left\|{\bm{\nu}}\right\|^{2}}{160m},\quad-\frac{1}{m}\sum_{r\in{\mathcal{B}}_{s}}\overline{N}_{s,r}^{(\tau+1)}+\frac{1}{m}\sum_{r\in{\mathcal{B}}_{s}}\overline{N}_{s,r}^{(\tau)}\geq\frac{\eta p_{\mathrm{b}}\left\|{\bm{\nu}}\right\|^{2}}{160m}.

6.

\frac{1}{60}\sum_{r\in[m]}\overline{M}_{-1,r}^{(\tau+1)}\leq\sum_{r\in[m]}\overline{M}_{1,r}^{(\tau+1)}\leq 60\sum_{r\in[m]}\overline{M}_{-1,r}^{(\tau+1)}
.

7.

For each
s,s^{\prime}\in\{\pm 1\}
,

\frac{p_{\mathrm{b}}\left\|{\bm{\nu}}\right\|^{2}}{120(2p_{\mathrm{e}}+p_{\mathrm{b}})\left\|{\bm{\mu}}\right\|^{2}}\sum_{r\in[m]}\overline{M}_{s,r}^{(\tau+1)}\leq\sum_{r\in{\mathcal{A}}_{s}}\overline{N}_{s^{\prime},r}^{(\tau+1)},\,-\sum_{r\in{\mathcal{B}}_{s}}\overline{N}_{s^{\prime},r}^{(\tau+1)}\leq\sum_{r\in[m]}\overline{M}_{s,r}^{(\tau+1)}.

8.

For any
s\in\{\pm 1\}
and
r\in[m]
,
\left|\underline{M}_{s,r}^{(\tau+1)}\right|,\left|\underline{N}_{s,r}^{(\tau+1)}\right|\leq\alpha_{\mathrm{st}}+\beta_{\mathrm{st}}
.

Proof of Lemma
D.3
.

For any
i\in[n_{\mathrm{st}}]
, we have

\displaystyle\hat{y}_{i}f_{\mathrm{st}}\left({\bm{W}}^{(\tau)},\tilde{\bm{X}}_{i}\right)

\displaystyle=F_{\hat{y}_{i}}\left({\bm{W}}^{(\tau)}_{\hat{y}_{i}},\tilde{\bm{X}}_{i}\right)-F_{-\hat{y}_{i}}\left({\bm{W}}^{(\tau)}_{\hat{y}_{i}},\tilde{\bm{X}}_{i}\right)

\displaystyle\leq F_{\hat{y}_{i}}\left({\bm{W}}^{(\tau)}_{\hat{y}_{i}},\tilde{\bm{X}}_{i}\right)

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{m}\sum_{l\in[2]}\sum_{r\in[m]}\sigma\left(\left\langle{\bm{w}}_{\hat{y}_{i},r}^{(\tau)},\tilde{\bm{v}}_{i}^{(l)}\right\rangle\right)+\frac{1}{m}\sum_{r\in[m]}\sigma\left(\left\langle{\bm{w}}_{\hat{y}_{i},r}^{(\tau)},\tilde{\bm{\xi}}_{i}\right\rangle\right).

For each
i\in[n_{\mathrm{st}}]
and
l\in[2]
, we have

\displaystyle\quad\frac{1}{m}\sum_{r\in[m]}\sigma\left(\left\langle{\bm{w}}_{\hat{y}_{i},r}^{(\tau)},\tilde{\bm{v}}_{i}^{(l)}\right\rangle\right)

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{m}\sum_{r\in[m]}\sigma\left(\left\langle{\bm{w}}_{\hat{y}_{i},r}^{(0)},\tilde{\bm{v}}_{i}^{(l)}\right\rangle+\max_{s\in\{\pm 1\}}\left\{\overline{M}_{s,r}^{(\tau)},\pm\overline{N}_{s,r}^{(\tau)}\right\}\right)

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{m}\sum_{r\in[m]}\left[\sigma\left(\left\langle{\bm{w}}_{\hat{y}_{i},r}^{(0)},\tilde{\bm{v}}_{i}^{(l)}\right\rangle\right)+\sigma\left(\max_{s\in\{\pm 1\}}\left\{\overline{M}_{s,r}^{(\tau)},\pm\overline{N}_{s,r}^{(\tau)}\right\}\right)\right]

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{m}\sum_{r\in[m]}\left[\sigma\left(\left\langle{\bm{w}}_{\hat{y}_{i},r}^{(0)},\tilde{\bm{v}}_{i}^{(l)}\right\rangle\right)+\max_{s\in\{\pm 1\}}\left\{\sigma\left(\overline{M}_{s,r}^{(\tau)}\right),\sigma\left(\overline{N}_{s,r}^{(\tau)}\right)\sigma\left(-\overline{N}_{s,r}^{(\tau)}\right)\right\}\right]

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{m}\sum_{r\in[m]}\sigma\left(\left\langle{\bm{w}}_{\hat{y}_{i},r}^{(0)},\tilde{\bm{v}}_{i}^{(l)}\right\rangle\right)+\max_{s\in\{\pm 1\}}\left\{\frac{1}{m}\sum_{r\in[m]}\overline{M}_{s,r}^{(\tau)},\frac{1}{m}\sum_{r\in{\mathcal{A}}_{s}}\overline{N}_{s,r}^{(\tau)},-\frac{1}{m}\sum_{r\in{\mathcal{B}}_{s}}\overline{N}_{s,r}^{(\tau)}\right\}

\displaystyle\leq\alpha_{\mathrm{st}}+\frac{1}{2}.

Combining with Lemma
D.2
, for any
i\in[n_{\mathrm{st}}]
, we have

\hat{y}_{i}f_{\mathrm{st}}\left({\bm{W}}^{(\tau)},\tilde{\bm{X}}_{i}\right)\leq 2\cdot\left(\alpha_{\mathrm{st}}+\frac{1}{2}\right)+\alpha_{\mathrm{st}}+\frac{3}{\log T^{*}}\leq 2,

where the last inequality follows from (
6
) and thus we have

1\geq\tilde{g}_{i}^{(\tau)}=\frac{1}{1+\exp\left(\hat{y}_{i}f_{\mathrm{st}}\left({\bm{W}}^{(\tau)},\tilde{\bm{X}}_{i}\right)\right)}\geq\frac{1}{1+\exp(2)}\geq\frac{1}{9},

(20)

for any
i\in[n_{\mathrm{st}}]
.

From Lemma
A.3
and the event
E_{\mathrm{st}}
, for any
s\in\{\pm 1\}
and
r\in[m]
, we obtain

\displaystyle\overline{M}_{s,r}^{(\tau+1)}-\overline{M}_{s,r}^{(\tau)}=

\displaystyle\frac{\eta}{mn_{\mathrm{st}}}\sum_{l\in[2]}\left(\sum_{i\in{\mathcal{C}}_{{\bm{\mu}}_{s}}^{(l)}}\tilde{g}_{i}^{(\tau)}-\sum_{i\in{\mathcal{F}}_{{\bm{\mu}}_{s}}^{(l)}}\tilde{g}_{i}^{(\tau)}\right)\left\|{\bm{\mu}}\right\|^{2}\cdot\mathbbm{1}\left[\left\langle{\bm{w}}_{s,r}^{(\tau)},{\bm{\mu}}_{s}\right\rangle>0\right]

\displaystyle\geq\frac{\eta}{mn_{\mathrm{st}}}\sum_{l\in[2]}\left(\frac{1}{9}\left|{\mathcal{C}}_{{\bm{\mu}}_{s}}^{(l)}\right|-\left|{\mathcal{F}}_{{\bm{\mu}}_{s}}^{(l)}\right|\right)\left\|{\bm{\mu}}\right\|^{2}\cdot\mathbbm{1}\left[\left\langle{\bm{w}}_{s,r}^{(\tau)},{\bm{\mu}}_{s}\right\rangle>0\right]

\displaystyle\geq\frac{2\eta}{mn_{\mathrm{st}}}\left(\frac{1-C_{\mathrm{st}}^{-1}}{9}\cdot n_{\bm{\mu}}-C_{\mathrm{st}}^{-1}\cdot n_{\bm{\mu}}\right)\left\|{\bm{\mu}}\right\|^{2}\cdot\mathbbm{1}\left[\left\langle{\bm{w}}_{s,r}^{(\tau)},{\bm{\mu}}_{s}\right\rangle>0\right]

\displaystyle\geq\frac{\eta}{5mn_{\mathrm{st}}}n_{\bm{\mu}}\left\|{\bm{\mu}}\right\|^{2}\cdot\mathbbm{1}\left[\left\langle{\bm{w}}_{s,r}^{(\tau)},{\bm{\mu}}_{s}\right\rangle>0\right]

\displaystyle=\frac{\eta(2p_{\mathrm{e}}+p_{\mathrm{b}})}{20m}\left\|{\bm{\mu}}\right\|^{2}\cdot\mathbbm{1}\left[\left\langle{\bm{w}}_{s,r}^{(\tau)},{\bm{\mu}}_{s}\right\rangle>0\right]

\displaystyle\geq 0.

Hence, if
r\in{\mathcal{M}}_{s}
, we have

\left\langle{\bm{w}}_{s,r}^{(\tau+1)},{\bm{\mu}}_{s}\right\rangle=\left\langle{\bm{w}}_{s,r}^{(0)},{\bm{\mu}}_{s}\right\rangle+\overline{M}_{s,r}^{(\tau+1)}\geq\left\langle{\bm{w}}_{s,r}^{(0)},{\bm{\mu}}_{s}\right\rangle+\overline{M}_{s,r}^{(\tau)}=\left\langle{\bm{w}}_{s,r}^{(\tau)},{\bm{\mu}}_{s}\right\rangle>0

and if
r\notin{\mathcal{M}}_{s}
, we have
\overline{M}_{s,r}^{(\tau+1)}=\overline{M}_{s,r}^{(\tau)}=0
.

In addition, we have

\displaystyle\frac{1}{m}\sum_{r\in[m]}\overline{M}_{s,r}^{(\tau+1)}

\displaystyle\geq\frac{1}{m}\sum_{r\in[m]}\overline{M}_{s,r}^{(\tau)}+\frac{\eta(2p_{\mathrm{e}}+p_{\mathrm{b}})}{20m}\left\|{\bm{\mu}}\right\|^{2}\cdot\frac{1}{m}\sum_{r\in[m]}\mathbbm{1}\left[\left\langle{\bm{w}}_{s,r}^{(\tau)},{\bm{\mu}}_{s}\right\rangle>0\right]

\displaystyle\geq\frac{1}{m}\sum_{r\in[m]}\overline{M}_{s,r}^{(\tau)}+\frac{\eta(2p_{\mathrm{e}}+p_{\mathrm{b}})}{20m}\left\|{\bm{\mu}}\right\|^{2}\cdot\frac{|{\mathcal{M}}_{s}|}{m}

\displaystyle\geq\frac{1}{m}\sum_{r\in[m]}\overline{M}_{s,r}^{(\tau)}+\frac{\eta(2p_{\mathrm{e}}+p_{\mathrm{b}})}{80m}\left\|{\bm{\mu}}\right\|^{2},

where the last inequality follows from (
9
).

Similarly, for any
s\in\{\pm 1\}
and
r\in{\mathcal{A}}_{s}
we obtain

\displaystyle\overline{N}_{s,r}^{(\tau+1)}-\overline{N}_{s,r}^{(\tau)}=

\displaystyle\frac{\eta}{mn_{\mathrm{st}}}\sum_{l\in[2]}\left(\sum_{i\in{\mathcal{C}}_{{\bm{\nu}}_{s}}^{(l)}}\tilde{g}_{i}^{(\tau)}-\sum_{i\in{\mathcal{F}}_{{\bm{\nu}}_{s}}^{(l)}}\tilde{g}_{i}^{(\tau)}\right)\left\|{\bm{\nu}}\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle\geq\frac{\eta}{mn_{\mathrm{st}}}\sum_{l\in[2]}\left(\frac{1}{9}\left|{\mathcal{C}}_{{\bm{\nu}}_{s}}^{(l)}\right|-\left|{\mathcal{F}}_{{\bm{\nu}}_{s}}^{(l)}\right|\right)\left\|{\bm{\nu}}\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle\geq\frac{2\eta}{mn_{\mathrm{st}}}\left(\frac{1-C_{\mathrm{st}}^{-1}}{9}\cdot n_{\bm{\nu}}-C_{\mathrm{st}}^{-1}\cdot n_{\bm{\nu}}\right)\left\|{\bm{\nu}}\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle\geq\frac{\eta}{5mn_{\mathrm{st}}}n_{\bm{\nu}}\left\|{\bm{\nu}}\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle=\frac{\eta p_{\mathrm{b}}}{40m}\left\|{\bm{\nu}}\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle\geq 0.

Hence, if
r\in{\mathcal{A}}_{s}
, we have

\left\langle{\bm{w}}_{s,r}^{(\tau+1)},{\bm{\nu}}_{s}\right\rangle=\left\langle{\bm{w}}_{s,r}^{(0)},{\bm{\nu}}_{s}\right\rangle+\overline{N}_{s,r}^{(\tau+1)}\geq\left\langle{\bm{w}}_{s,r}^{(0)},{\bm{\nu}}_{s}\right\rangle+\overline{N}_{s,r}^{(\tau)}=\left\langle{\bm{w}}_{s,r}^{(\tau)},{\bm{\nu}}_{s}\right\rangle>0.

In addition, we have

\displaystyle\frac{1}{m}\sum_{r\in{\mathcal{A}}_{s}}\overline{N}_{s,r}^{(\tau+1)}

\displaystyle\geq\frac{1}{m}\sum_{r\in{\mathcal{A}}_{s}}\overline{N}_{s,r}^{(\tau)}+\frac{\eta p_{\mathrm{b}}}{40}\left\|{\bm{\nu}}\right\|^{2}\cdot\frac{\left|{\mathcal{A}}_{s}\right|}{m}

\displaystyle\geq\frac{1}{m}\sum_{r\in{\mathcal{A}}_{s}}\overline{N}_{s,r}^{(\tau)}+\frac{\eta p_{\mathrm{b}}}{160m}\left\|{\bm{\nu}}\right\|^{2}.

We can obtain similar conclusions for
{\mathcal{B}}_{s}
. Thus, we obtain the first five statements.

For any
s\in\{\pm 1\}
, we have

\displaystyle\frac{1}{m}\sum_{r\in[m]}\overline{M}_{s,r}^{(\tau+1)}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{m}\sum_{r\in[m]}\overline{M}_{s,r}^{(\tau)}+\frac{\eta}{mn_{\mathrm{st}}}\left(\left|{\mathcal{C}}_{{\bm{\mu}}_{s}}^{(1)}\right|+\left|{\mathcal{C}}_{{\bm{\mu}}_{s}}^{(2)}\right|\right)\left\|{\bm{\mu}}\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{m}\sum_{r\in[m]}\overline{M}_{s,r}^{(\tau)}+\frac{2\left(1+C_{\mathrm{st}}^{-1}\right)\eta n_{\bm{\mu}}}{mn_{\mathrm{st}}}\left\|{\bm{\mu}}\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{m}\sum_{r\in[m]}\overline{M}_{s,r}^{(\tau)}+\frac{3\eta(2p_{\mathrm{e}}+p_{\mathrm{b}})}{4m}\left\|{\bm{\mu}}\right\|^{2}.

In addition, we have

\displaystyle\frac{1}{m}\sum_{r\in{\mathcal{A}}_{s}}\overline{N}_{s,r}^{(\tau+1)}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{m}\sum_{r\in{\mathcal{A}}_{s}}\overline{N}_{s,r}^{(\tau)}+\frac{\eta}{mn_{\mathrm{st}}}\left(\left|{\mathcal{C}}_{{\bm{\nu}}_{s}}^{(1)}\right|+\left|{\mathcal{C}}_{{\bm{\nu}}_{s}}^{(2)}\right|\right)\left\|{\bm{\nu}}\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{m}\sum_{r\in{\mathcal{A}}_{s}}\overline{N}_{s,r}^{(\tau)}+\frac{2\left(1+C_{\mathrm{st}}^{-1}\right)\eta n_{\bm{\nu}}}{mn_{\mathrm{st}}}\left\|{\bm{\nu}}\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{m}\sum_{r\in{\mathcal{A}}_{s}}\overline{N}_{s,r}^{(\tau)}+\frac{3\eta p_{\mathrm{b}}}{8m}\left\|{\bm{\nu}}\right\|^{2}.

Similarly, we have

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{m}\sum_{r\in{\mathcal{B}}_{s}}\overline{N}_{s,r}^{(\tau+1)}

\displaystyle\leq-\frac{1}{m}\sum_{r\in{\mathcal{B}}_{s}}\overline{N}_{s,r}^{(\tau)}+\frac{\eta}{mn_{\mathrm{st}}}\left(\left|{\mathcal{C}}_{-{\bm{\nu}}_{s}}^{(1)}\right|+\left|{\mathcal{C}}_{-{\bm{\nu}}_{s}}^{(2)}\right|\right)\left\|{\bm{\nu}}\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle\leq-\frac{1}{m}\sum_{r\in{\mathcal{B}}_{s}}\overline{N}_{s,r}^{(\tau)}+\frac{2\left(1+C_{\mathrm{st}}^{-1}\right)\eta n_{\bm{\nu}}}{mn_{\mathrm{st}}}\left\|{\bm{\nu}}\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle\leq-\frac{1}{m}\sum_{r\in{\mathcal{B}}_{s}}\overline{N}_{s,r}^{(\tau)}+\frac{3p_{\mathrm{b}}\eta}{8m}\left\|{\bm{\nu}}\right\|^{2}.

Using these, we have

\displaystyle\sum_{r\in[m]}\overline{M}_{1,r}^{(\tau+1)}

\displaystyle=\sum_{r\in[m]}\overline{M}_{1,r}^{(\tau)}+\left[\sum_{r\in[m]}\overline{M}_{1,r}^{(\tau+1)}-\sum_{r\in[m]}\overline{M}_{1,r}^{(\tau)}\right]

\displaystyle\geq\sum_{r\in[m]}\overline{M}_{1,r}^{(\tau)}+\frac{\eta(2p_{\mathrm{e}}+p_{\mathrm{b}})}{80}\left\|{\bm{\mu}}\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle\geq\sum_{r\in[m]}\overline{M}_{1,r}^{(\tau)}+\frac{1}{60}\left[\sum_{r\in[m]}\overline{M}_{-1,r}^{(\tau+1)}-\sum_{r\in[m]}\overline{M}_{-1,r}^{(\tau)}\right]

\displaystyle\geq\frac{1}{60}\sum_{r\in[m]}\overline{M}_{-1,r}^{(\tau)}+\frac{1}{60}\left[\sum_{r\in[m]}\overline{M}_{-1,r}^{(\tau+1)}-\sum_{r\in[m]}\overline{M}_{-1,r}^{(\tau)}\right]

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{60}\sum_{r\in[m]}\overline{M}_{-1,r}^{(\tau+1)}.

By using symmetric arguments, we can obtain
\sum_{r\in[m]}\overline{M}_{1,r}^{(\tau+1)}\leq 60\sum_{r\in[m]}\overline{M}_{-1,r}^{(\tau+1)}
.

Similarly, for any
s,s^{\prime}\in\{\pm 1\}
, we have

\displaystyle\sum_{r\in{\mathcal{A}}_{s}}\overline{N}_{s,r}^{(\tau+1)}

\displaystyle=\sum_{r\in{\mathcal{A}}_{s}}\overline{N}_{s,r}^{(\tau)}+\left[\sum_{r\in{\mathcal{A}}_{s}}\overline{N}_{s,r}^{(\tau+1)}-\sum_{r\in{\mathcal{A}}_{s}}\overline{N}_{s,r}^{(\tau)}\right]

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{r\in{\mathcal{A}}_{s}}\overline{N}_{s,r}^{(\tau)}+\frac{3\eta p_{\mathrm{b}}}{8}\left\|{\bm{\nu}}\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{r\in{\mathcal{A}}_{s}}\overline{N}_{s,r}^{(\tau)}+\frac{\eta(2p_{\mathrm{e}}+p_{\mathrm{b}})}{80}\left\|{\bm{\mu}}\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{r\in[m]}\overline{M}_{s^{\prime},r}^{(\tau)}+\left[\sum_{r\in[m]}\overline{M}_{s^{\prime},r}^{(\tau+1)}-\sum_{r\in[m]}\overline{M}_{s^{\prime},r}^{(\tau)}\right]

\displaystyle=\sum_{r\in[m]}\overline{M}_{s^{\prime},r}^{(\tau+1)}.

In addition, we have

\displaystyle\quad\sum_{r\in{\mathcal{A}}_{s}}\overline{N}_{s,r}^{(\tau+1)}

\displaystyle=\sum_{r\in{\mathcal{A}}_{s}}\overline{N}_{s,r}^{(\tau)}+\left[\sum_{r\in{\mathcal{A}}_{s}}\overline{N}_{s,r}^{(\tau+1)}-\sum_{r\in{\mathcal{A}}_{s}}\overline{N}_{s,r}^{(\tau)}\right]

\displaystyle\geq\sum_{r\in{\mathcal{A}}_{s}}\overline{N}_{s,r}^{(\tau)}+\frac{p_{\mathrm{b}}\eta}{160}\left\|{\bm{\nu}}\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle=\sum_{r\in{\mathcal{A}}_{s}}\overline{N}_{s,r}^{(\tau)}+\frac{p_{\mathrm{b}}\left\|{\bm{\nu}}\right\|^{2}}{120(2p_{\mathrm{e}}+p_{\mathrm{b}})\left\|{\bm{\mu}}\right\|^{2}}\cdot\frac{3\eta(2p_{\mathrm{e}}+p_{\mathrm{b}})\left\|{\bm{\mu}}\right\|^{2}}{4m}

\displaystyle\geq\frac{p_{\mathrm{b}}\left\|{\bm{\nu}}\right\|^{2}}{120(2p_{\mathrm{e}}+p_{\mathrm{b}})\left\|{\bm{\mu}}\right\|^{2}}\sum_{r\in[m]}\overline{M}_{s^{\prime},r}^{(\tau)}+\frac{p_{\mathrm{b}}\left\|{\bm{\nu}}\right\|^{2}}{120(2p_{\mathrm{e}}+p_{\mathrm{b}})\left\|{\bm{\mu}}\right\|^{2}}\cdot\left[\sum_{r\in[m]}\overline{M}_{s^{\prime},r}^{(\tau+1)}-\sum_{r\in[m]}\overline{M}_{s^{\prime},r}^{(\tau)}\right]

\displaystyle=\frac{p_{\mathrm{b}}\left\|{\bm{\nu}}\right\|^{2}}{120(2p_{\mathrm{e}}+p_{\mathrm{b}})\left\|{\bm{\mu}}\right\|^{2}}\sum_{r\in[m]}\overline{M}_{s^{\prime},r}^{(\tau+1)}.

Now, we prove the last statement.
For any
r\in[m]
, if
\underline{M}_{s,r}^{(\tau)}\leq-\alpha_{\mathrm{st}}
, then we have
\left\langle{\bm{w}}_{s,r}^{(\tau)},{\bm{\mu}}_{-s}\right\rangle<0
.
Hence,
\left|\underline{M}_{s,r}^{(\tau+1)}\right|=\left|\underline{M}_{s,r}^{(\tau)}\right|\leq\alpha_{\mathrm{st}}+\beta_{\mathrm{st}}
by Lemma
A.3
. Otherwise,
\underline{M}_{s,r}^{(\tau)}>-\alpha_{\mathrm{st}}
implies that

\displaystyle\quad\frac{mn_{\mathrm{st}}}{\eta\lVert{\bm{\mu}}\rVert^{2}}\left(\underline{M}_{s,r}^{(\tau+1)}-\underline{M}_{s,r}^{(\tau)}\right)

\displaystyle=-\sum_{l\in[2]}\left(\sum_{j\in{\mathcal{C}}_{{\bm{\mu}}_{-s}}^{(l)}}\tilde{g}_{j}^{(\tau)}-\sum_{j\in{\mathcal{F}}_{{\bm{\mu}}_{-s}}^{(l)}}\tilde{g}_{j}^{(\tau)}\right)\cdot\mathbbm{1}\left[\left\langle{\bm{w}}_{s,r}^{(\tau)},{\bm{\mu}}_{-s}\right\rangle>0\right]

\displaystyle\leq-\sum_{l\in[2]}\left(\frac{1}{9}\left|{\mathcal{C}}_{{\bm{\mu}}_{-s}}^{(l)}\right|-\left|{\mathcal{F}}_{{\bm{\mu}}_{-s}}^{(l)}\right|\right)\cdot\mathbbm{1}\left[\left\langle{\bm{w}}_{s,r}^{(\tau)},{\bm{\mu}}_{-s}\right\rangle>0\right]

\displaystyle\leq 0,

where the first inequality follows from (
20
) and the last inequality follows from the event
E_{\mathrm{st}}
.
Thus,
\underline{M}_{s,r}^{(\tau+1)}\leq\underline{M}_{s,r}^{(\tau)}\leq\alpha_{\mathrm{st}}+\beta_{\mathrm{st}}
.
In addition, we have

\displaystyle\quad\frac{mn_{\mathrm{st}}}{\eta\lVert{\bm{\mu}}\rVert^{2}}\left(\underline{M}_{s,r}^{(\tau+1)}-\underline{M}_{s,r}^{(\tau)}\right)

\displaystyle=-\sum_{l\in[2]}\left(\sum_{j\in{\mathcal{C}}_{{\bm{\mu}}_{-s}}^{(l)}}\tilde{g}_{j}^{(\tau)}-\sum_{j\in{\mathcal{F}}_{{\bm{\mu}}_{-s}}^{(l)}}\tilde{g}_{j}^{(\tau)}\right)\cdot\mathbbm{1}\left[\left\langle{\bm{w}}_{s,r}^{(\tau)},{\bm{\mu}}_{-s}\right\rangle>0\right]

\displaystyle\geq-\sum_{l\in[2]}\left|{\mathcal{C}}_{{\bm{\mu}}_{s}}^{(l)}\right|\cdot\mathbbm{1}\left[\left\langle{\bm{w}}_{s,r}^{(\tau)},{\bm{\mu}}_{-s}\right\rangle>0\right]

\displaystyle\geq-2n_{\mathrm{st}}.

Therefore, we have

\underline{M}_{s,r}^{(\tau+1)}\geq\underline{M}_{s,r}^{(\tau)}-\frac{2\eta\lVert{\bm{\mu}}\rVert^{2}}{m}\geq-\alpha_{\mathrm{st}}-\frac{2\eta\lVert{\bm{\mu}}\rVert^{2}}{m}\geq-\alpha_{\mathrm{st}}-\beta_{\mathrm{st}},

where the last inequality follows from (
10
).

From Lemma
A.3
, for any
r\in[m]
,

\left|\underline{N}_{s,r}^{(\tau+1)}-\underline{N}_{s,r}^{(\tau)}\right|\leq\frac{2\eta\left\|{\bm{\nu}}\right\|^{2}}{m}\leq\alpha_{\mathrm{st}}.

Therefore, it suffices to show that
\underline{N}_{s,r}^{(\tau+1)}\leq\underline{N}_{s,r}^{(\tau)}
when
\underline{N}_{s,r}^{(\tau)}>\alpha_{\mathrm{st}}
and
\underline{N}_{s,r}^{(\tau+1)}\geq\underline{N}_{s,r}^{(\tau)}
when
\underline{N}_{s,r}^{(\tau)}<-\alpha_{\mathrm{st}}
.
If
\underline{N}_{s,r}^{(\tau)}>\alpha_{\mathrm{st}}
, then we have

\left\langle{\bm{w}}_{s,r}^{(\tau)},{\bm{\nu}}_{-s}\right\rangle=\left\langle{\bm{w}}_{s,r}^{(0)},{\bm{\nu}}_{-s}\right\rangle+\underline{N}_{s,r}^{(\tau)}>0.

Hence, we have

\displaystyle\quad\frac{mn_{\mathrm{st}}}{\eta\lVert{\bm{\nu}}\rVert^{2}}\left(\underline{N}_{s,r}^{(\tau+1)}-\underline{N}_{s,r}^{(\tau)}\right)

\displaystyle=-\sum_{l\in[2]}\left(\sum_{j\in{\mathcal{C}}_{{\bm{\nu}}_{-s}}^{(l)}}\tilde{g}_{j}^{(\tau)}-\sum_{j\in{\mathcal{F}}_{{\bm{\nu}}_{-s}}^{(l)}}\tilde{g}_{j}^{(\tau)}\right)

\displaystyle\leq-\sum_{l\in[2]}\left(\frac{1}{9}\left|{\mathcal{C}}_{{\bm{\nu}}_{-s}}^{(l)}\right|-\left|{\mathcal{F}}_{{\bm{\nu}}_{-s}}^{(l)}\right|\right)

\displaystyle\leq-2\left(\frac{\left(1-C_{\mathrm{st}}^{-1}\right)}{9}\cdot n_{\bm{\nu}}-C_{\mathrm{st}}^{-1}\cdot n_{\bm{\nu}}\right)

\displaystyle\leq 0,

where the first inequality follows from (
20
) and the last inequality follows from the event
E_{\mathrm{st}}
.
Using a similar argument, we can also show that
\underline{N}_{s,r}^{(\tau+1)}\geq\underline{N}_{s,r}^{(\tau)}
when
\underline{N}_{s,r}^{(\tau)}<-\alpha_{\mathrm{st}}
and we have desired conclusion.
∎

Next, we characterize the early-phase learning dynamics of easy signals.

Lemma D.4
.

Under Condition
3.5
and the event
E_{\mathrm{st}}
, there exists the smallest iteration
T_{\mathrm{es}}\in\left[0,\frac{200m}{\eta(2p_{\mathrm{e}}+p_{\mathrm{b}})\left\|{\bm{\mu}}\right\|^{2}}\right]
such that

\max\left\{\frac{1}{m}\sum_{r\in[m]}\overline{M}_{1,r}^{(T_{\mathrm{es}})},\frac{1}{m}\sum_{r\in[m]}\overline{M}_{-1,r}^{(T_{\mathrm{es}})}\right\}\geq\frac{1}{2}.

Proof of Lemma
D.4
.

Suppose there is no such iteration. We fix an arbitrary
s\in\{\pm 1\}
.
Note that from Condition
3.5
,

\frac{100m}{\eta(2p_{\mathrm{e}}+p_{\mathrm{b}})\left\|{\bm{\mu}}\right\|^{2}}\leq\frac{mn_{\mathrm{st}}}{\eta\sigma_{p}^{2}d\log T^{*}}
. Thus, we can apply
Lemma
D.3
and for any
t\in\left[0,\frac{100m}{\eta(2p_{\mathrm{e}}+p_{\mathrm{b}})\left\|{\bm{\mu}}\right\|^{2}}\right]
, we have

\displaystyle\frac{1}{m}\sum_{r\in[m]}\overline{M}_{s,r}^{(t)}

\displaystyle\geq\frac{1}{m}\sum_{r\in[m]}\overline{M}_{s,r}^{(t-1)}+\frac{\eta(2p_{\mathrm{e}}+p_{\mathrm{b}})}{80m}\left\|{\bm{\mu}}\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle\phantom{\geq}\vdots

\displaystyle\geq\frac{1}{m}\sum_{r\in[m]}\overline{M}_{s,r}^{(0)}+\frac{\eta(2p_{\mathrm{e}}+p_{\mathrm{b}})}{80m}\left\|{\bm{\mu}}\right\|^{2}t

\displaystyle=\frac{\eta(2p_{\mathrm{e}}+p_{\mathrm{b}})}{80}\left\|{\bm{\mu}}\right\|^{2}t.

By choosing
t=\frac{40m}{\eta(2p_{\mathrm{e}}+p_{\mathrm{b}})\left\|{\bm{\mu}}\right\|^{2}}\in\left[0,\frac{100m}{\eta(2p_{\mathrm{e}}+p_{\mathrm{b}})\left\|{\bm{\mu}}\right\|^{2}}\right]
, we obtain contradiction.
Therefore, there exists an iteration
t\in\left[0,\frac{100m}{\eta(2p_{\mathrm{e}}+p_{\mathrm{b}})\left\|{\bm{\mu}}\right\|^{2}}\right]
such that

\max\left\{\frac{1}{m}\sum_{r\in[m]}\overline{M}_{1,r}^{(t)},\frac{1}{m}\sum_{r\in[m]}\overline{M}_{-1,r}^{(t)}\right\}\geq\frac{1}{2}.

We then define
T_{\mathrm{es}}
as the smallest such iteration.
∎

We will show that iteration
T_{\mathrm{es}}
obtained from Lemma
D.4
is our desired stopping time. By sequentially applying Lemma
D.3
, for any
s\in\{\pm 1\}
, we
have
\overline{M}_{s,r}^{(T_{\mathrm{es}})}\geq 0
for all
r\in[m]
,
\overline{N}_{s,r}^{(T_{\mathrm{es}})}\geq 0
if
r\in{\mathcal{A}}_{s}
, and
\overline{N}_{s,r}^{(T_{\mathrm{es}})}\leq 0
if
r\in{\mathcal{B}}_{s}
. Furthermore, we have

\frac{1}{m}\sum_{r\in[m]}\overline{M}_{s,r}^{(T_{\mathrm{es}})}\geq\frac{1}{120},\quad\frac{1}{m}\sum_{r\in{\mathcal{A}}_{s}}\overline{N}_{s,r}^{(T_{\mathrm{es}})},-\frac{1}{m}\sum_{r\in{\mathcal{B}}_{s}}\overline{N}_{s,r}^{(T_{\mathrm{es}})}\geq\frac{p_{\mathrm{b}}\left\|{\bm{\nu}}\right\|^{2}}{240(2p_{\mathrm{e}}+p_{\mathrm{b}})\left\|{\bm{\mu}}\right\|^{2}}

(21)

and for any
r\in[m]
, we have

\left|\underline{M}_{s,r}^{(T_{\mathrm{es}})}\right|,\left|\underline{N}_{s,r}^{(T_{\mathrm{es}})}\right|\leq\alpha_{\mathrm{st}}+\beta_{\mathrm{st}}.

(22)

Combining the upper bound on
T_{\mathrm{es}}
and Lemma
D.2
leads to the following bound: for any
s\in\{\pm 1\},r\in[m]
, and
i\in[n_{\mathrm{st}}]
,

\left|\rho_{s,r,i}^{(T_{\mathrm{es}})}\right|,\left|\left\langle{\bm{w}}_{s,r}^{(T_{\mathrm{es}})},\tilde{\bm{\xi}}_{i}\right\rangle\right|\leq\alpha_{\mathrm{st}}+\frac{3\eta\sigma_{p}^{2}d}{mn_{\mathrm{st}}}\cdot\frac{100m}{\eta(2p_{\mathrm{e}}+p_{\mathrm{b}})\left\|{\bm{\mu}}\right\|^{2}}\leq\frac{400\sigma_{p}^{2}d}{(2p_{\mathrm{e}}+p_{\mathrm{b}})n_{\mathrm{st}}\left\|{\bm{\mu}}\right\|^{2}},

(23)

where the last inequality follows from (
6
).

D.2
Train Error

In this subsection, we prove the first conclusion conditioned on the event
E_{\mathrm{st}}
.
For any
i\in[n_{\mathrm{st}}]
, we have

\displaystyle\quad\tilde{y}_{i}f_{\mathrm{st}}\left({\bm{W}}^{(T_{\mathrm{es}})},\tilde{\bm{X}}_{i}\right)

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{m}\sum_{l\in[2]}\sum_{r\in[m]}\phi\left(\left\langle{\bm{w}}_{\tilde{y}_{i},r}^{(T_{\mathrm{es}})},\tilde{\bm{v}}_{i}^{(l)}\right\rangle\right)-\frac{1}{m}\sum_{l\in[2]}\sum_{r\in[m]}\phi\left(\left\langle{\bm{w}}_{-\tilde{y}_{i},r}^{(T_{\mathrm{es}})},\tilde{\bm{v}}_{i}^{(l)}\right\rangle\right)

\displaystyle\quad+\frac{1}{m}\sum_{r\in[m]}\phi\left(\left\langle{\bm{w}}_{\tilde{y}_{i},r}^{(T_{\mathrm{es}})},\tilde{\bm{\xi}}_{i}\right\rangle\right)-\frac{1}{m}\sum_{r\in[m]}\phi\left(\left\langle{\bm{w}}_{-\tilde{y}_{i},r}^{(T_{\mathrm{es}})},\tilde{\bm{\xi}}_{i}\right\rangle\right)

\displaystyle\geq\frac{1}{m}\sum_{l\in[2]}\sum_{r\in[m]}\phi\left(\left\langle{\bm{w}}_{\tilde{y}_{i},r}^{(T_{\mathrm{es}})},\tilde{\bm{v}}_{i}^{(l)}\right\rangle\right)-2\cdot(\alpha_{\mathrm{st}}+\alpha_{\mathrm{st}}+\beta_{\mathrm{st}})-\frac{400\sigma_{p}^{2}d}{(2p_{\mathrm{e}}+p_{\mathrm{b}})n_{\mathrm{st}}\left\|{\bm{\mu}}\right\|^{2}}

\displaystyle\geq\frac{2}{m}\min\left\{\sum_{r\in[m]}\overline{M}_{\tilde{y}_{i},r}^{(T_{\mathrm{es}})},\sum_{r\in[m]}\overline{N}_{\tilde{y}_{i},r}^{(T_{\mathrm{es}})},-\sum_{r\in{\mathcal{B}}_{\tilde{y}_{i}}}\overline{N}_{\tilde{y}_{i},r}^{(T_{\mathrm{es}})}\right\}-2(2\alpha_{\mathrm{st}}+\beta_{\mathrm{st}})-\frac{400\sigma_{p}^{2}d}{(2p_{\mathrm{e}}+p_{\mathrm{b}})n_{\mathrm{st}}\left\|{\bm{\mu}}\right\|^{2}}

\displaystyle\geq\frac{p_{\mathrm{b}}\left\|{\bm{\nu}}\right\|^{2}}{120(2p_{\mathrm{e}}+p_{\mathrm{b}})\left\|{\bm{\mu}}\right\|^{2}}-2(\alpha_{\mathrm{st}}+\beta_{\mathrm{st}})-\frac{400\sigma_{p}^{2}d}{(2p_{\mathrm{e}}+p_{\mathrm{b}})n_{\mathrm{st}}\left\|{\bm{\mu}}\right\|^{2}}

\displaystyle>0,

where the first inequality follows from (
22
) and (
23
), the third inequality follows from (
21
), and the last inequality follows from (
6
) and Condition
3.5
.

\square

D.3
Test Error

In this subsection, we characterize the test error of the strong model. All arguments in this subsection are under the event
E_{\mathrm{st}}
.
Define
{\bm{v}}^{(1)}
,
{\bm{v}}^{(2)}
, and
{\bm{\xi}}
as the signal vectors and the noise vector in the test data
({\bm{X}},y)
, respectively.

We define a function
h:S\rightarrow\mathbb{R}
as
h({\bm{z}}):=\frac{1}{m}\sum_{r\in[m]}\sigma\left(\left\langle{\bm{w}}_{-y,r}^{(T_{\mathrm{es}})},{\bm{z}}\right\rangle\right)
for any
{\mathbf{z}}\in S
. It plays a crucial role when we prove the upper bounds on test error. We have

\mathbb{E}[h({\bm{\xi}})]=\frac{1}{m}\mathbb{E}_{{\mathbf{z}}_{1},\dots,{\mathbf{z}}_{m}}\left[\sum_{r\in[m]}\sigma({\mathbf{z}}_{r})\right]=\frac{1}{2m}\mathbb{E}_{{\mathbf{z}}_{1},\dots,{\mathbf{z}}_{m}}\left[\sum_{r\in[m]}\left|{\mathbf{z}}_{r}\right|\right]=\frac{\sigma_{p}}{\sqrt{2\pi}m}\sum_{r\in[m]}\left\|\Pi_{S}{\bm{w}}^{(T_{\mathrm{es}})}_{-y,r}\right\|,

where
{\mathbf{z}}_{r}\sim{\mathcal{N}}\left(0,\sigma_{p}^{2}\left\|\Pi_{S}{\bm{w}}^{(T_{\mathrm{es}})}_{-y,r}\right\|^{2}\right)
for each
r\in[m]
. Also, for any
{\bm{z}}_{1},{\bm{z}}_{2}\in S
, we have

\displaystyle\left|h({\bm{z}}_{1})-h({\bm{z}}_{2})\right|

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{m}\sum_{r\in[m]}\left|\sigma\left(\left\langle{\bm{w}}_{-y,r}^{(T_{\mathrm{es}})},{\bm{z}}_{1}\right\rangle\right)-\sigma\left(\left\langle{\bm{w}}_{-y,r}^{(T_{\mathrm{es}})},{\bm{z}}_{2}\right\rangle\right)\right|

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{m}\sum_{r\in[m]}\left|\left\langle{\bm{w}}_{-y,r}^{(T_{\mathrm{es}})},{\bm{z}}_{1}\right\rangle-\left\langle{\bm{w}}_{-y,r}^{(T_{\mathrm{es}})},{\bm{z}}_{2}\right\rangle\right|

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{m}\sum_{r\in[m]}\left|\left\langle\Pi_{S}{\bm{w}}_{-y,r}^{(T_{\mathrm{es}})},{\bm{z}}_{1}\right\rangle-\left\langle\Pi_{S}{\bm{w}}_{-y,r}^{(T_{\mathrm{es}})},{\bm{z}}_{2}\right\rangle\right|

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{m}\sum_{r\in[m]}\left\|\Pi_{S}{\bm{w}}_{-y,r}^{(T_{\mathrm{es}})}\right\|\left\|{\bm{z}}_{1}-{\bm{z}}_{2}\right\|.

Hence,
h
is
\frac{1}{m}\sum_{r\in[m]}\left\|\Pi_{S}{\bm{w}}_{-y,r}^{(T_{\mathrm{es}})}\right\|
-Lipschitz.

The following lemma characterizes
\left\|\Pi_{S}{\bm{w}}_{-y,r}^{(T_{\mathrm{es}})}\right\|
’s which is related to key properties of
h
.

Lemma D.5
.

For any
s\in\{\pm 1\}
, it holds that

\sum_{r\in[m]}\left\|\Pi_{S}{\bm{w}}_{s,r}^{(T_{\mathrm{es}})}\right\|\leq\frac{900m\sigma_{p}d^{\frac{1}{2}}}{(2p_{\mathrm{e}}+p_{\mathrm{b}})n^{\frac{1}{2}}\left\|{\bm{\mu}}\right\|^{2}}.

Proof of Lemma
D.5
.

From Lemma
A.3
and triangular inequality, we have

\left\|\Pi_{S}{\bm{w}}_{s,r}^{(T_{\mathrm{es}})}\right\|\leq\left\|\Pi_{S}{\bm{w}}_{s,r}^{(0)}\right\|+\left\|\sum_{i\in[n_{\mathrm{st}}]}\rho_{s,r,i}^{(T_{\mathrm{es}})}\tilde{\bm{\xi}}_{i}\lVert\tilde{\bm{\xi}}_{i}\rVert^{-2}\right\|\leq\sqrt{2}\sigma_{0}d^{\frac{1}{2}}+\left\|\sum_{i\in[n_{\mathrm{st}}]}\rho_{s,r,i}^{(T_{\mathrm{es}})}\tilde{\bm{\xi}}_{i}\lVert\tilde{\bm{\xi}}_{i}\rVert^{-2}\right\|.

We have

\displaystyle\quad\left\|\sum_{i\in[n_{\mathrm{st}}]}\rho_{s,r,i}^{(T_{\mathrm{es}})}\tilde{\bm{\xi}}_{i}\lVert\tilde{\bm{\xi}}_{i}\rVert^{-2}\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle=\sum_{i\in[n_{\mathrm{st}}]}\left(\rho_{s,r,i}^{(T_{\mathrm{es}})}\right)^{2}\lVert\tilde{\bm{\xi}}_{i}\rVert^{-2}+\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}i,j\in[n_{\mathrm{st}}]\\
i\neq j\end{subarray}}\rho_{s,r,i}^{(T_{\mathrm{es}})}\rho_{s,r,j}^{(T_{\mathrm{es}})}\langle\tilde{\bm{\xi}}_{i},\tilde{\bm{\xi}}_{j}\rangle\lVert\tilde{\bm{\xi}}_{i}\rVert^{-2}\lVert\tilde{\bm{\xi}}_{j}\rVert^{-2}

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{i\in[n_{\mathrm{st}}]}\left(\rho_{s,r,i}^{(T_{\mathrm{es}})}\right)^{2}\lVert\tilde{\bm{\xi}}_{i}\rVert^{-2}+\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}i,j\in[n_{\mathrm{st}}]\\
i\neq j\end{subarray}}\left|\rho_{s,r,i}^{(T_{\mathrm{es}})}\rho_{s,r,j}^{(T_{\mathrm{es}})}\right|\left|\langle\tilde{\bm{\xi}}_{i},\tilde{\bm{\xi}}_{j}\rangle\right|\lVert\tilde{\bm{\xi}}_{i}\rVert^{-2}\lVert\tilde{\bm{\xi}}_{j}\rVert^{-2}

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{i\in[n_{\mathrm{st}}]}\left(\rho_{s,r,i}^{(T_{\mathrm{es}})}\right)^{2}\lVert\tilde{\bm{\xi}}_{i}\rVert^{-2}+\frac{1}{2}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}i,j\in[n_{\mathrm{st}}]\\
i\neq j\end{subarray}}\left(\left(\rho_{s,r,i}^{(T_{\mathrm{es}})}\right)^{2}+\left(\rho_{s,r,j}^{(T_{\mathrm{es}})}\right)^{2}\right)\left|\langle\tilde{\bm{\xi}}_{i},\tilde{\bm{\xi}}_{j}\rangle\right|\lVert\tilde{\bm{\xi}}_{i}\rVert^{-2}\lVert\tilde{\bm{\xi}}_{j}\rVert^{-2}

\displaystyle\leq(1+\beta_{\mathrm{st}})\sum_{i\in[n_{\mathrm{st}}]}\left(\rho_{s,r,i}^{(T_{\mathrm{es}})}\right)^{2}\lVert\tilde{\bm{\xi}}_{i}\rVert^{-2}

\displaystyle\leq\left(\frac{800\sigma_{p}d^{\frac{1}{2}}}{(2p_{\mathrm{e}}+p_{\mathrm{b}})n_{\mathrm{st}}^{\frac{1}{2}}\left\|{\bm{\mu}}\right\|^{2}}\right)^{2}

where the third inequality follows from (
8
) and the fourth inequality follows from (
23
) and (
8
).
Therefore, we have

\sum_{r\in[m]}\left\|\Pi_{S}{\bm{w}}_{s,r}^{(T_{\mathrm{es}})}\right\|\leq\sqrt{2}m\sigma_{0}d^{\frac{1}{2}}+\frac{800m\sigma_{p}d^{\frac{1}{2}}}{(2p_{\mathrm{e}}+p_{\mathrm{b}})n_{\mathrm{st}}^{\frac{1}{2}}\left\|{\bm{\mu}}\right\|^{2}}\leq\frac{900m\sigma_{p}d^{\frac{1}{2}}}{(2p_{\mathrm{e}}+p_{\mathrm{b}})n_{\mathrm{st}}^{\frac{1}{2}}\left\|{\bm{\mu}}\right\|^{2}},

where the last inequality follows from
(C3)
.

∎

By Theorem 5.2.2 in
Vershynin 2018
, for any
z>0
, it holds that

\mathbb{P}[h({\bm{\xi}})-\mathbb{E}[h({\bm{\xi}})]\geq z]\leq\exp\left(-\frac{cz^{2}}{\sigma_{p}^{2}\left\|h\right\|_{\mathrm{Lip}}^{2}}\right)

where
c
is a universal constant and
\left\|\cdot\right\|_{\mathrm{Lip}}
denotes the best Lipschitz constant. Combining with Lemma
C.4
, we have

\mathbb{P}[h({\bm{\xi}})-\mathbb{E}[h({\bm{\xi}})]\geq z]\leq\exp\left(-\frac{c(2p_{\mathrm{e}}+p_{\mathrm{b}})^{2}\left\|{\bm{\mu}}\right\|^{4}}{900^{2}\sigma_{p}^{4}d}z^{2}\right).

(24)

Now, we characterize the test error. First, we consider the case
({\bm{X}},y)\in{\mathcal{S}}_{\mathrm{e}}\cup{\mathcal{S}}_{\mathrm{b}}
. We have

\displaystyle\quad yf_{\mathrm{st}}\left({\bm{W}}^{(T_{\mathrm{es}})},{\bm{X}}\right)

\displaystyle=F_{y}\left({\bm{W}}_{y}^{(T_{\mathrm{es}})},{\bm{X}}\right)-F_{-y}\left({\bm{W}}_{-y}^{(T_{\mathrm{es}})},{\bm{X}}\right)

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{m}\sum_{l\in[2]}\sum_{r\in[m]}\sigma\left(\left\langle{\bm{w}}_{y,r}^{(T_{\mathrm{es}})},{\bm{v}}^{(l)}\right\rangle\right)+\frac{1}{m}\sum_{r\in[m]}\sigma\left(\left\langle{\bm{w}}_{y,r}^{(T_{\mathrm{es}})},{\bm{\xi}}\right\rangle\right)

\displaystyle\quad-\frac{1}{m}\sum_{l\in[2]}\sum_{r\in[m]}\sigma\left(\left\langle{\bm{w}}_{-y,r}^{(T_{\mathrm{es}})},{\bm{v}}^{(l)}\right\rangle\right)-\frac{1}{m}\sum_{r\in[m]}\sigma\left(\left\langle{\bm{w}}_{-y,r}^{(T_{\mathrm{es}})},{\bm{\xi}}\right\rangle\right)

\displaystyle\geq\frac{1}{m}\sum_{r\in[m]}\sigma\left(\left\langle{\bm{w}}_{y,r}^{(T_{\mathrm{es}})},{\bm{\xi}}\right\rangle\right)-\frac{1}{m}\sum_{r\in[m]}\sigma\left(\left\langle{\bm{w}}_{-y,r}^{(T_{\mathrm{es}})},{\bm{\xi}}\right\rangle\right)+\frac{1}{m}\sum_{r\in[m]}\overline{M}_{y,r}^{(T_{\mathrm{es}})}-4\alpha_{\mathrm{st}}

\displaystyle\geq-\frac{1}{m}\sum_{r\in[m]}\sigma\left(\left\langle{\bm{w}}_{-y,r}^{(T_{\mathrm{es}})},{\bm{\xi}}\right\rangle\right)+\frac{1}{120}-4\alpha_{\mathrm{st}}

\displaystyle\geq-h({\bm{\xi}})+\frac{1}{200},

where the first inequality follows from (
7
) and (
22
).
From (
24
) and Lemma
D.5
, we have

\displaystyle\quad\mathbb{P}\left[yf_{\mathrm{st}}\left({\bm{W}}^{(T_{\mathrm{es}})},{\bm{X}}\right)<0\,\middle|\,({\bm{X}},y)\in{\mathcal{S}}_{\mathrm{e}}\cup{\mathcal{S}}_{\mathrm{b}}\right]

\displaystyle\leq\mathbb{P}\left[h({\bm{\xi}})>\frac{1}{200}\right]=\mathbb{P}\left[h({\bm{\xi}})-\mathbb{E}[h({\bm{\xi}})]>\frac{1}{200}-\mathbb{E}[h({\bm{\xi}})]\right]

\displaystyle\leq\mathbb{P}\left[h({\bm{\xi}})-\mathbb{E}[h({\bm{\xi}})]>\frac{1}{200}-\frac{900\sigma_{p}d^{\frac{1}{2}}}{(2p_{\mathrm{e}}+p_{\mathrm{b}})n_{\mathrm{st}}^{\frac{1}{2}}\left\|{\bm{\mu}}\right\|^{2}}\right]

\displaystyle\leq\mathbb{P}\left[h({\bm{\xi}})-\mathbb{E}[h({\bm{\xi}})]>\frac{1}{250}\right]

\displaystyle\leq\exp\left(-\frac{n_{\mathrm{st}}(2p_{\mathrm{e}}+p_{\mathrm{b}})^{2}\left\|{\bm{\mu}}\right\|^{4}}{C_{5}\sigma_{p}^{4}d}\right),

with some constant
C_{5}>0
.

Using a similar argument, we can prove the upper bound on the test error for the case
({\bm{X}},y)\in{\mathcal{S}}_{\mathrm{h}}
. In this case, we have

\displaystyle\quad yf_{\mathrm{st}}\left({\bm{W}}^{(T_{\mathrm{es}})},{\bm{X}}\right)

\displaystyle=F_{y}\left({\bm{W}}_{y}^{(T_{\mathrm{es}})},{\bm{X}}\right)-F_{-y}\left({\bm{W}}_{-y}^{(T_{\mathrm{es}})},{\bm{X}}\right)
